# Customer portal — Customer contacts · Excel import of contacts and of portal accounts

Doc ref **GAS-CP-R17-001** · Patch: `contacts-import-patch.zip` (143 files incl. 57 manual figures, cumulative v1.17.0 → v1.23.1) + **eight database steps** in this order (only the ones Settings › About shows as *Missing*): `install/migrate_cp_r17_contacts.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r17_companies.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r17_offer_statuses.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r18_financials.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r18_notifications.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r19_customers_invoicing.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r20_archive.sql`, `install/migrate_cp_r21_kb.sql` (after R16; the `-goldensas_portal.sql` copy selects the database itself). Portal version after this patch: **v1.18.0**. The patch includes everything from v1.16.x (theme, dashboard, account filters), so it can be applied directly on v1.15.2 as well — then run the R16 step too.

## 1. Contacts (menu › Commercial › Contacts — Golden A/S staff only; owners never see it)

A contact is a person: **full name, position, category** (Owner, Owner's representative, Management company, Captain, Chief engineer, ETO, Crew, Yard, Class society, Supplier, Consultant / designer, Other), **customer** (a customer of the portal) and/or **company** (a yard, class society or supplier that is not a customer), e-mail, phone, mobile, an optional link to the person's **portal account**, **birthday** (with the year or day-and-month only), **name day** (day and month), **interests** (free text) and active / inactive.

- **Vessels**: every contact is linked to any number of vessels with the **role on that vessel** (Captain, Owner's rep, Chief engineer…) and one ★ primary vessel. The edit form lists the vessels grouped by customer with a filter box; the contact page has *Link / Unlink*. Each **vessel page** (overview) shows its contacts with role, category, reach, birthday and name day — marked *Internal*.
- **Notes**: a dated, signed note thread on every contact (add on the page or as the "first note" when creating; the author or an administrator can delete). A note can also come in through the Excel import.
- **Dates to remember**: the contact page shows the next birthday (with the age the person turns) and the next name day, in days. The Contacts page opens with counters (contacts, companies, vessels covered, **birthdays in 30 days, name days in 30 days**) and a **Celebrations** column listing every birthday 🎂 and name day 🎉 of the next 30 days, soonest first.
- **Filters**: search (name, company, e-mail, phone, position, interests), customer (incl. "no customer"), vessel (incl. "no vessel"), category, *coming up* (birthdays / name days in 30 days), active / inactive / all; paged 50 per page.
- Administrators can delete a contact (with its notes and vessel links); staff can create and edit.

## 2. Excel import of contacts (Settings › Import from Excel › Contacts, or ⤒ on the Contacts page)

Template `GAS-contacts-import-template.xlsx` (sheet *Contacts* + *How to*): **Full name · Company · Category · Position · Email · Phone · Mobile · Vessels · Birthday · Name day · Interests · Notes**.

- *Company* = a customer name of the portal (links the contact to that customer) or any other company.
- *Vessels* = vessel names as in the portal separated by `;`, with the role in brackets: `MY GY-85-02 (Owner's rep); MY-GY-62-01`. Spelling is forgiving (`MY GY-85-02`, `MY-GY-85-02`, `gy8502` and the IMO all match); the vessel must already exist — unknown names are listed under *Warnings* and skipped, the contact is still imported.
- *Birthday* `15.03.1975` or `15.03` (no year); *Name day* `06.12`; Excel dates, `yyyy-mm-dd` and `15 March` also work. Category in English or the portal language.
- Matching by **e-mail**, else by **name + company** (or name alone when it is unique). Existing contacts are updated: filled cells overwrite, blank cells keep. A note is added once (not repeated on re-import). *Check only* first; the result counts rows / contacts added / updated / vessel links / notes, lists skipped rows (no name, invalid e-mail) and warnings (unknown category → Other, unknown vessel, date not understood).

## 3. Excel import of portal accounts (Settings › Import from Excel › Portal accounts — administrators)

Template `GAS-accounts-import-template.xlsx` (sheet *Accounts* + *How to*): **Full name · Email · Role · Customer · Golden A/S role · Discipline · Manager · Language · Password · 2FA required · Active**.

- *Email* identifies the account: existing → updated, new → created (*Full name* required). *Role* customer / staff / admin (default customer); *Customer* required for customer accounts and created if missing. *Golden A/S role* is created if unknown; an unknown *Discipline* or *Manager* is reported. *Language* en / el / it / uk / bg (also "Greek", "gr"…). *2FA required* / *Active* yes / no, blank keeps (new accounts: 2FA required, active).
- *Password*: at least 10 characters; blank keeps the current one. A **new account without a password gets a generated one** (`FDR4R-LVmR-WQzR!` style), listed **once** in the result page — copy them there and hand them over securely; the *check only* run shows them masked.
- Skipped rows: invalid e-mail, missing name for a new account, unknown role, short password, customer account without customer. Filled cells overwrite, blank cells keep.

## 4. Install

1. Upload the patch in binary mode, Ctrl+F5.
2. Select **goldensas_portal** in phpMyAdmin, import `migrate_cp_r17_contacts.sql` (or the `-goldensas_portal.sql` copy). (If coming from v1.15.2: also `migrate_cp_r16_theme.sql`; `migrate_cp_r16_indexes.sql` is optional.)
3. `cp_selfcheck.php` → *R17 migration (contacts) OK*, *201 files match*; Settings › About shows v1.18.0 with both R17 steps *Applied*. Until the step runs, the Contacts menu entry stays hidden and the import card shows a hint.

## 5. Verified before shipping

- Contacts import (6 rows): 4 contacts, 5 vessel links, 2 notes; skipped: invalid e-mail, missing name; warnings: unknown vessel; loose vessel spelling matched; `02.11` and `19.10` read as day.month (not as Excel serials); re-import updates without duplicating notes ✔
- Accounts import (6 rows): 3 created (2 with generated passwords, 1 with a given one), 1 updated (language), 1 customer created; skipped: short password, customer account without customer; warning: unknown discipline; **both the generated and the given password sign in** (2FA enrolment follows as required) ✔
- Contact created from the form with two vessels + roles + primary + first note; edited (all vessels cleared, name day typed as "6 December", set inactive); link / unlink, add / delete note; staff cannot delete a contact (403), administrator can ✔
- Owners get 403 on every contacts page; vessel page card is staff-only ✔
- Contrast (text) and field audits on Contacts, contact page, edit form, import page and vessel page — 0 findings in light and dark; lint clean; five-language parity (1 561 keys each); manifest rebuilt, 194 files match — screenshots `preview-contacts-dark.png`, `preview-contact-light.png`, `preview-contact-edit-dark.png`, `preview-import-result.png`.

## 6. v1.17.1 — GAS-CP-R17-002 (companies, communication channels, bulk actions, contacts on the project page)

Second database step `install/migrate_cp_r17_companies.sql` (run after the R17 contacts step). It creates the `companies` master list and **seeds it automatically**: one company per portal customer (kind *Owner*, linked to that customer) plus every company name typed on existing contacts; existing contacts are re-linked, and every contact with an e-mail gets *E-mail* as its channel.

- **Companies master list** (Contacts › 🏢 Companies): name, kind (Owner, Management company, Yard, Class society, Supplier, Consultant / designer, Broker, Agent, Other), the portal customer it belongs to, country, city, website, e-mail, phone, notes, active. Administrators add / edit / delete (delete only when no contact uses it); staff see the list. On the contact form the **company is now a drop-down** of this list; picking a company that belongs to a customer fills the customer automatically. The contact lists and vessel/project cards show the company with its kind.
- **Excel import of companies** (Settings › Import from Excel › Companies, administrators) — template `GAS-companies-import-template.xlsx`: Company, Kind, Customer ("yes" when it is the same name, or the customer's name / ERP code — created if missing), Country, City, Website, Email, Phone, Notes, Active. Matched by name. The contacts import now resolves *Company* against this list and creates missing ones as kind *Other* (reported under Warnings so you can set the kind).
- **Communication methods**: each contact carries **channels** (E-mail, Phone, Mobile, WhatsApp, Viber, SMS, Teams, LinkedIn, Post — how they prefer to be reached) and **lists** they agreed to receive (Newsletter, Campaigns, Events, Greetings). Both are chip selectors on the form, columns in the list, filters (*Communication*, *Lists*), and columns *Channels* / *Subscriptions* in the contacts import template (`E-mail; WhatsApp` · `Newsletter; Events` · `all`).
- **Campaign selection**: filter the list (e.g. *Lists = Newsletter*, or *Category = Captain*), then **⤓ Export** downloads the filtered contacts as a UTF-8 CSV for the mailing tool (name, company, customer, category, position, e-mail, phone, mobile, vessels, channels, lists, birthday, name day, interests), and **✉ E-mail all (n)** opens a new mail with every listed address in BCC.
- **Bulk actions** on the list: tick rows or *All n matching the filters*, then + / − a list (newsletter, campaigns, events, greetings), set active / inactive, or — administrators only — **Delete**; a confirmation dialog precedes the action and the page returns to the same filters.
- **Contacts where the communication happens**: the **project tree** shows a collapsible *Contacts — <vessel>* block under every project of a vessel (name, role, company, e-mail / phone, ✉ E-mail all); the **sub-project page** has a *Contacts* tab; the **vessel page** card is the same block in full (channels, birthday, name day). All three are staff-only.

Verified: companies import (5 rows → 3 added, 1 updated with "yes" customer link, 1 skipped without name, 1 unknown kind recorded as Other); contacts import with channels/lists and a new company (created as Other with warning, `all` expands to every list); form save through the company drop-down links the customer automatically; bulk "+ Events" by filter, bulk delete by filter and by ticked rows (notes and vessel links removed too), staff bulk delete refused (403); CSV export opens in Excel with UTF-8 names (Lürssen); contrast and field audits 0 findings in both themes on contacts, companies, contact, contact form, projects, sub-project Contacts tab and vessel page; manifest 199 files. Screenshots `preview-contacts-light.png`, `preview-companies-dark.png`, `preview-contact-edit-light.png`, `preview-projects-contacts-light.png`.

## 7. v1.17.2 — GAS-CP-R17-003 (Excel export of Contacts, Vessels, Projects)

Each of the three list pages has a **⤓ Export** button that downloads a real Excel workbook (`.xlsx`, no library needed on the server — written by the portal itself; falls back to CSV only if the hosting lacks the zip extension) **with the filters currently applied on the page**. **Administrators only** (v1.17.3, GAS-CP-R17-004): the button is shown only to administrators and the download endpoints refuse everyone else (403), staff and owners alike:

| Export | File | Columns |
|---|---|---|
| Contacts | `GAS-contacts-<date>.xlsx` | Full name, Company, Customer, Category, Position, Email, Phone, Mobile, Vessels (with roles), Channels, Subscriptions, Birthday, Name day, Interests, Notes (count), Active |
| Vessels | `GAS-vessels-<date>.xlsx` | Vessel, IMO, Customer, Yard, Projects, Active projects, Project codes, Open tickets, Contacts, Active, Created |
| Companies (v1.17.4) | `GAS-companies-<date>.xlsx` | Company, Kind, Customer, Country, City, Website, Email, Phone, number of Contacts, number of Vessels (through its contacts), Notes, Active, Created — with the page's search / kind filter |
| Projects | `GAS-projects-<date>.xlsx` — two sheets | **Projects**: code, ERP code, name, vessel, customer, status, PM, category, number of sub-projects, overall progress %, deadline, next milestone, offers sent / amount, approved / amount, updated · **Sub-projects**: project, vessel, code, name, vessel system, status, the seven stage percentages, overall %, deadline, invoicing, invoiced %, updated |

Navy header row in Aptos with white bold text, frozen header, auto-filter on every sheet, numbers stay numeric (sortable, summable), column widths set. Verified by re-opening every file with openpyxl and LibreOffice (`sample-export-*.xlsx`, `preview-export-projects.png`). No database step.

## 8. v1.17.6 — GAS-CP-R17-007 (administrator delete)

Every ticket, work order, service report, timesheet and offer page ends with an **Administrator — delete** card (administrators only; the endpoints refuse staff and owners). Deleting removes the record in **any status** with its history, comments, attachments / photos and generated PDF / Word files; records that reference it are kept and unlinked (a deleted ticket leaves its work orders and service reports; a deleted work order leaves its service reports). Engineers keep their existing right to delete their own draft / rejected timesheets. Each delete asks for confirmation first and returns to the list.

## 9. v1.17.7 — GAS-CP-R17-008 (your own offer statuses)

Third database step `install/migrate_cp_r17_offer_statuses.sql` (creates `offer_statuses` with the seven built-ins and widens `offers.status`). New tab **Settings › Offer statuses** (administrators):

- **Add a status** with a label (e.g. *Post season planning*, *Not replied*, *Negotiation*), a colour (gold / green / blue / navy / grey / red) and how it **behaves**: *Awaiting (open)* — counts as open, ages like Sent, shows on dashboards and in the project tree as pending; *Approved / in hand* — counts in the approved totals; *Closed — won* — approved but no longer open; *Closed — lost* — neither.
- The seven built-in statuses keep their flow and meaning; you can change their order and colour and hide any of them except Sent. Your own statuses can be deleted while no offer uses them (otherwise set inactive).
- Where they appear: the *Progress* buttons on the offer page (your statuses are reachable from any status and lead to any status), the status filter and tiles on Offers, the project tree pills, the dashboard, the weekly report and the **Excel import** (status matched by label — so a row with `Post season planning` imports straight into that status).
- Verified: two custom statuses created, an offer moved Sent → Post season planning → Sent, list filtered by the custom status and by *Open (all)* including it, an Excel row imported with the custom label, unused custom status deleted, staff refused (403); contrast / field audits 0 findings.

## 10. v1.17.8 — GAS-CP-R17-009 (offers in the project tree and on the sub-project page)

Opening a project in the **Projects** tree now shows its **Offers** table first, above the sub-project rows: offer number and revision, sent date, sub-project (or *whole project*), subject, customer amount, status, aging and — when a document is attached — a **⤓ download button with the file name**. *+ New offer* opens the form pre-filled with the project (staff). Every **sub-project page** opens with the same table limited to that sub-project (Process tab, above the stage percentages), with *+ New offer* pre-filled with project and sub-project. Owners see only their own projects' offers, as everywhere. No database step.

## 11. v1.18.0 — GAS-CP-R18-001 (financial data per Golden A/S role)

Database step `install/migrate_cp_r18_financials.sql` (one column on `staff_roles`, default *visible* — nothing changes until you untick a role). **Settings › Golden A/S roles** gets a **Financial data — visible** checkbox per role (and on the add form). When unticked for a role, staff in that role see **•••** instead of every amount: offer amounts on the Offers page and tiles, the offer page, the project tree (roll-ups and sub-project pills), the offers tables, the dashboard (the *offers awaiting* tile shows the count instead of k€), the weekly report on screen and in PDF / Word, and amounts quoted in the offer history; the **invoicing** status / percentages disappear from the project tree, sub-project page and forms; the timesheets *Invoicing* view and the invoice action are refused; the offer form hides the amount (the stored amount is kept on save). Owners always see their own amounts; administrators always see everything; staff without a role see everything. Verified by flipping the Project Manager role: 0 amounts on 8 pages for that staff member, unchanged for administrator and owner, flag restored.

## 12. v1.18.1 — GAS-CP-R18-002 (notifications)

Database step `install/migrate_cp_r18_notifications.sql` (two tables: `notifications`, `nf_cursor`; the cursors start at the *current* end of every event table, so nothing from before the step is announced). Then replace the files and Ctrl+F5.

**What the user sees**

- A **bell** in the top bar with the number of unseen entries; it opens a drop-down with the eight newest unseen entries (group pill, what happened, the object, who did it, how long ago), *Mark all as seen* and *All notifications*. No JavaScript — it is a plain `<details>` element, so it works on every browser and in both themes.
- A **gold dot** on each menu group (Fleet · Work · Commercial · Library) that has unseen entries, next to the existing "waiting for you" badges.
- A **Notifications page** (`?p=notifications`) with tabs *All · Fleet · Work · Commercial · Library* (each with its unseen count), an *All / Unseen* switch, 40 entries per page, *Open* (goes to the ticket / offer / sub-project tab… and marks the entry as seen), *Mark as seen* per entry, *Mark {group} as seen* and *Mark all as seen*. Seen entries stay listed for 90 days, greyed with the time they were seen.

**Where the entries come from** — nothing new is written when something happens; a sweep (at most once a minute, on any page load, under a database lock so two visitors never run it twice) reads the history tables the modules already keep, past a per-source cursor, and turns each new row into entries:

| Group | Source | Announced | Not announced |
|---|---|---|---|
| Work | tickets: history + comments | created, assigned, status changes, owner replied, work-order / report linked, new comment | *edited*; internal comments go to Golden A/S only |
| Work | work orders | submitted, approved, rejected, started, done, cancelled, reopened, note | *saved*; drafts go to Golden A/S only |
| Work | service reports | submitted, accepted, disputed (sent back), reopened | *created*, *saved*; drafts go to Golden A/S only |
| Work | timesheets (staff only) | submitted, approved, rejected, invoiced, un-invoiced, withdrawn | *created*, *saved* |
| Commercial | offers | sent, re-sent, approved, rejected, imported, every status change | *edited*; amounts in the note are masked for roles without financial visibility (R18-001) |
| Commercial | owner approvals | new document submitted, comment, approved, approved with comments, rejected, new revision | drafts go to Golden A/S only |
| Commercial | contact notes (administrators) | new note | — |
| Fleet | class submittals, drawing requests | new rows — several added at once are folded into one entry ("3 drawing requests added") | — |
| Fleet | weekly notes, sub-project comments | new note / comment | internal ones go to Golden A/S only |
| Library | documents | new document | — |

**Who receives them** — for anything under a project: the project manager, every administrator, and (when it is visible to the owner) the active users of the project's customer; plus the people on the record itself (ticket assignee and creator, work-order requester, report author, timesheet owner and approver). The person who made the change never gets an entry about it. One unseen entry per object per person: a second change on the same ticket replaces the earlier unseen entry (the newest event wins), so a busy ticket is one line, not ten.

**Verified before shipping** — events posted as owner and as staff over HTTP (comment, status change, offer decision, approval comment) and rows inserted for every other source; the correct recipients and nobody else (internal weekly note not seen by the owner, timesheets never seen by the owner, actor excluded); open-and-redirect, mark one / group / all; counts in bell, menu dots and tabs; owner cannot open another person's entry; amount masking in offer notes for a role with financials hidden; contrast audit 0 findings in both themes; five-language parity (1 717 keys); lint clean; manifest 212 files; `cp_selfcheck.php` → *R18-002 migration (notifications) OK*. Screenshots `preview-nf-bell.png`, `preview-nf-page.png`, `preview-nf-page-dark.png`, `preview-nf-customer.png`.

## 13. v1.19.0 — GAS-CP-R19-001 (Customers in Settings · invoicing details)

Database step `install/migrate_cp_r19_customers_invoicing.sql` (eight address/contact columns on `customers`, new table `invoicing_details`).

**Where customers are edited** — until now a customer only came into being through the vessel / project imports (SoftOne or Excel) and had no page of its own. **Settings › Customers** (administrators) now lists every customer with ERP code, VAT, vessel / project / account counts and the number of invoicing details, searchable by name, ERP code, VAT, city or country, active / disabled / all. *New customer* and *Edit* open the form on the right: name, ERP code (what the imports match on), address, postal code, city, country, VAT number, website, e-mail, phone, notes, active. The card below lists the companies linked to that customer (Commercial › Companies) and the invoicing details. A customer with no vessels, projects or accounts can be deleted; one in use is disabled instead (its people can no longer sign in and it leaves the drop-downs). The *Customer* field on the company form links straight to this page.

**Invoicing details** — one card, the same on three pages: **Settings › Customers › edit**, **Commercial › Companies › edit**, and the **contact page** (for an individual who is billed personally, or who has his own billing company). Each owner can have **any number** of billing identities — *Head office*, *Management company*, *Owning SPV — M/Y …* — with one marked **default** (the first one automatically, *Make default* on any other). Fields: label, legal name, VAT number, tax office, registration number, for-the-attention-of, address, postal code, city, country, invoice e-mail, phone, bank, IBAN, SWIFT/BIC, currency, payment terms, notes, active. IBAN, SWIFT and VAT are stored without spaces in upper case; the invoice e-mail is validated. Administrators add, edit, delete and set the default; staff see the cards read-only. A contact that belongs to a company with invoicing details shows "n on {company}" in its details, linking to that company's card.

**Data model** — `invoicing_details (owner_type customer|company|contact, owner_id, label, legal_name, vat_no, tax_office, reg_no, address, city, postal_code, country, email, phone, attn, bank, iban, swift, currency, payment_terms, notes, is_default, is_active)`, indexed on owner. Nothing else refers to it yet; the next natural step is to pick an invoicing identity on an offer / timesheet-invoicing record and print it on the documents — say the word.

**Verified before shipping** — create / duplicate-name / duplicate-ERP / edit / disable / delete-in-use / delete-unused over HTTP as administrator; invoicing details added on a customer, a company and a contact, default switching and deletion re-assigning the default, invalid e-mail refused, wrong owner refused, staff POST refused (403) and the Customers tab hidden from staff; contrast and field audits 0 findings in both themes; five-language parity (1 777 keys); lint clean; manifest 217 files; `cp_selfcheck.php` → *R19 migration (customers, invoicing details) OK*. Screenshots `preview-customers.png`, `preview-company-invoicing.png`.

### 13a. Customers — Excel import and export (same patch)

**Settings › Import from Excel › Customers** (administrators) — template `GAS-customers-import-template.xlsx`. One row per customer, *or* one row per invoicing identity with the customer repeated. Columns: Customer, ERP code, Country, City, Postal code, Address, VAT, Email, Phone, Website, Notes, Active, then Invoicing label, Legal name, Invoicing VAT, Tax office, Registration no, Attn, Invoicing address / postal code / city / country / email / phone, Bank, IBAN, SWIFT, Currency, Payment terms, Invoicing notes, Default. A customer is matched by ERP code first, then by name; an invoicing identity by legal name or label; filled cells overwrite, blank cells keep; nothing is deleted. IBAN / SWIFT / VAT are normalised, e-mails validated (a bad one is reported and left blank), duplicate ERP codes reported. "Check only" first, as for the other imports. The result card counts customers created / updated and invoicing details added / updated.

**Export** — ⤓ on Settings › Customers (honours the search and status filter) and *Export current list* on the import card: `GAS-customers-YYYYMMDD.xlsx` in exactly the import layout, so the fastest way to fill in VAT / addresses / invoicing for everybody is: export → complete in Excel → import. Verified: template imported (3 rows → 1 customer created, 1 updated, 2 invoicing identities), exported (7 rows) and the export re-imported with no duplicates (6 updated, 0 created).

## 14. v1.20.0 — GAS-CP-R20-001 (file archive — implements GAS-2026-PORTAL-STO-001)

Database step `install/migrate_cp_r20_archive.sql` (columns `doc_no`, `revision`, `seq`, `sha256`, `archived_at` on `sp_files`; `seq`, `sha256`, `archived_at` on `ticket_files`; tables `doc_sequences`, `archive_moves`; new kinds `issued`, `file`, `comment`).

**Layout (as approved):** `storage/uploads/customers/<ERP code>_<name>/<project code>/<Y.nn>_<name>/{drawings · owner-approvals · class · tests · weekly-notes · files · service-reports · reports}`; project level `offers · ga · tickets/<TK no> · reports · service-reports` (FSR without sub-project); vessel tickets without a project under `customers/<customer>/_vessel_<name>/tickets/<TK no>`. Customers without ERP code are `C<id>_<name>` until the code is imported (then *Reorganise* re-files them). File name `<document number>_<revision>_<sequence>_<title>.<ext>` — Greek transliterated, ASCII only, ≤110 characters; collisions get `-2`, `-3`.

**Numbering:** drawings `<project>-<sub>-DRW-nnn`, tests `-FAT/HAT/SAT-nnn`, files `-FILE-nnn`, weekly-note photos `-WN-<ISO week>_nn`, GA `<project>-GA-nn` (sequences in `doc_sequences`, assigned when the file is registered); owner / class documents take the record's own document number and revision; FSR documents `<report no>_R<version>.pdf/.docx`, photos `<report no>_nn_<caption>`; offers `<offer no>_<rev>_<title>`; tickets `<TK no>_nn_<name>`; issued documents `<doc no>_R<n>`.

**New files are filed on upload** — every `cp_store_file` / `cp_store_bytes` / ticket upload computes the archive path and checksum immediately (if that fails the file stays in the old place and the next Reorganise picks it up). **Existing files:** Settings › About › File archive → *Reorganise — check only* (lists every move, consumes no numbers) → *Reorganise — apply* (moves in transactions of 200, logs every move, writes `_manifest.csv`) → *Undo* per run for 30 days. *Verify (sample)* / *Verify all + orphans* check existence, SHA-256 and files on disk that no register knows; *Write manifest* refreshes `storage/uploads/_manifest.csv` (path;size;sha256;table;id;doc_no;revision;kind;created). Sizes per family and per customer are shown. **Re-file** on the project edit page does the same for one project after a code / name change.

**Issue:** weekly vessel report, monthly sub-project report (DOCX — the PDF is the print view), vessel history and timesheet statement (per project, internal) now have an *Issued revisions* card with an **Issue R[n]** button for staff: the document is rendered, stored as kind `issued` with its document number and the next revision, and listed with PDF / DOCX downloads served from the archive. Vessel-level documents are filed under the vessel's newest active project. FSR documents already worked this way.

**Downloads:** Golden A/S-issued documents (FSR, issued reports) download under their archive name; uploads keep the name the person gave (OP-07). Access is unchanged — still by id with the same checks.

**Deviations from the proposal, for the record:** timesheet statements are filed under the project (`<project>/reports`, visibility internal) rather than `_golden/timesheets`, because every register entry needs a project; `_exports` retention and `_superseded` folders are not implemented (revisions sit side by side; nothing is deleted); library documents (`documents` table) keep their current path column.

**Verified:** dry run / apply on 19 sandbox files (all moved, 0 errors, names as in the proposal), idempotent re-run (0 moves), full verify 0 missing / 0 mismatch / 0 orphans, manifest written; new ticket attachment and weekly-note photo filed on upload with Greek transliterated; Issue for all four document families (R0 then R1), customer POST refused (403); Re-file after a sub-project rename (14 moved) and Undo (14 put back, verify clean); downloads with archive / original names; contrast 0 findings both themes on 6 pages; lint clean; 1 834 keys × 5 languages; manifest 222 files; self-check green. Screenshots `preview-archive.png`, `preview-issued.png`, `preview-refile.png`.

## 15. v1.20.1 — GAS-CP-R20-002 (user manual in the portal)

No database step. **Library › Support** now opens with two cards: *User manual — owners and customers* (`?p=manual&for=customer`, everybody) and *User manual — Golden A/S* (`?p=manual&for=golden`, staff and administrators only; owners asking for it get the owner edition). Each manual is one page with a sticky table of contents, numbered sections, numbered figures with a list of figures at the end, tips, and a *Print / save as PDF* button (print stylesheet hides the menu and top bar). Staff can switch editions at the top.

- **Owner edition, 14 sections:** signing in and 2FA · finding your way around · dashboard · vessels (history, weekly report) · projects and sub-projects with the seven stages and every tab · owner approvals (with flow diagram) · tickets (lifecycle diagram, new-ticket form) · work orders (flow) · accepting service reports (flow) · offers (statuses) · documentation · notifications · account, theme, language, support · FAQ. 28 figures.
- **Golden A/S edition, 15 sections:** roles and access (diagram) · dashboard · projects, stages, tabs, weekly notes, files · reports and *Issue* · tickets · work orders · field service reports · timesheets and man-hours · offers and statuses · contacts, companies, customers, invoicing · notifications · Settings (accounts, roles, imports) · file archive, versions, integrity · vessels · numbering conventions. 29 figures.
- Figures are real screenshots of the portal (light theme, demo data) in `assets/manual/*.jpg` plus eight SVG workflow diagrams (ticket, approval, FSR, work order, timesheet, offer, stages, roles) in the house colours; click a figure to open it full size. Manual text is in English; page chrome (titles, buttons, cards) is in all five languages.
- To refresh the screenshots after a future change, re-run the capture script and replace the files in `assets/manual/`; captions are in `views/_manual_customer.php` / `_manual_golden.php`.

## 16. v1.20.2 — GAS-CP-R20-003 (Support page for staff)

Fix: a Golden A/S account opening Library › Support and sending a request got *Column 'customer_id' cannot be null* — support requests belong to a customer, and staff accounts have none. The page is now role-aware: **owners** keep the request form and their request history; **Golden A/S staff** see an inbox of every owner request (subject and text, customer and person, vessel, date, status) with *Close* / *Re-open* — close a request once it is answered by e-mail, the owner sees the status on their page. Staff who try to send a request get a plain message instead of the error. No database step.

## 17. v1.21.0 — GAS-CP-R21-001 (knowledge base)

Database step `install/migrate_cp_r21_kb.sql` (tables `kb_questions`, `kb_answers`, `kb_votes`). **Library › Knowledge base** (menu entry and a card on the Support page with the six most-viewed questions): questions and answers from every user of the portal.

- Anyone signed in asks (title ≥ 8 characters, details, category: Using the portal · Systems on board · Documents & approvals · Service & tickets · Other) and answers; answers by Golden A/S people carry a *Golden A/S* tag.
- The author of the question or any staff member marks the accepted answer (✓ solved); everyone can mark an answer 👍 helpful (one vote per person, toggled). Views are counted once per session.
- Search in questions and answers; filters by category and status (open / solved / unanswered); sort by recent activity or most viewed; paging 20.
- Staff may post a question *visible to Golden A/S only*; owners never see those.
- **Administrators delete** questions (with their answers and votes) and single answers; deleting the accepted answer un-solves the question. A customer trying the delete action gets 403.
- Verified over HTTP: ask (owner and staff, internal visibility hidden from the owner), answer, accept, vote toggle, admin delete of an answer (counter updated) and a question, contrast and field audits 0 findings both themes, 1 903 keys × 5 languages, manifest 279 files, self-check green. Both user manuals gained a Knowledge base section.

## 18. v1.21.1 — GAS-CP-R21-002 (knowledge-base attachments)

`install/migrate_cp_r21_kb.sql` now also creates `kb_files` — re-run it if you already ran the v1.21.0 copy (the existing tables report "already exists", harmless). Questions and answers accept **up to five attachments of 20 MB** each (photos, PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, DWG/DXF, text, CSV, ZIP, short videos); images are shown as thumbnails, other files as download chips. Files are stored under `storage/uploads/_kb/Q<question id>/KB-<q>[-A<answer>]_<nn>_<name>.<ext>` with a SHA-256, served only through the portal (`?p=kbfile&id=`) after the visibility check (Golden A/S-only questions stay inside Golden A/S), and are deleted together with the answer or question. The archive integrity check ignores `_kb/`. Verified: upload on a question (two files, Greek name transliterated) and on an answer, thumbnails and chips rendered, streaming with the original name, deletion of the answer removed its file; contrast 0 findings.

## 19. v1.21.2 — GAS-CP-R21-003 (world clock strip)

No database step. A 34 px strip under the top bar on every page (option A): **UTC · GR · AE · GB · IT · NL · DE · US (Dallas)** in 24-hour time with seconds, ticking in the browser (`Intl.DateTimeFormat` per zone, so summer/winter time is always right and the server does nothing). The home zone (GR, Europe/Athens) is framed in gold; a zone on another calendar day carries −1d / +1d; the right end shows the home date and ISO week. The strip is sticky with the top bar, scrolls horizontally on phones, and is hidden when printing. The zone list is in `views/_layout.php` (`$clocks` — code, IANA zone, city) should you want to change it. Verified in light and dark themes and at phone width; contrast 0 findings.

## 20. v1.22.0 — GAS-CP-R22-001 licence · GAS-CP-R22-002 tasks and reminders

**Database step (one file for both):** phpMyAdmin › select `goldensas_portal` › Import `install/migrate_cp_r22_license_tasks-goldensas_portal.sql` (creates `cp_settings`, `tasks`, `task_log`). Then upload the patch zip (binary), Ctrl+F5, and check Settings › About › Versions shows R22 as applied.

**Licence — do this right after the upload.** From the moment the R22 code runs, the portal counts 14 activation days; administrators see a yellow banner with the countdown. Open **Settings › Licence › Install a key**, paste the content of `GAS-CP-2026-0001_goldensas.com.lic` (valid for goldensas.com / www.goldensas.com until 31 Dec 2036, unlimited accounts) and press Install. The banner disappears and the footer shows "Licensed to Golden A/S … · S/N GAS-CP-2026-0001".

What the licence does: serial number + service-contract period in a key signed with Golden A/S's private key (ECDSA P-256 / SHA-256 via OpenSSL) and verified offline by the public key in `app/lic.php` — no call home. It can be bound to host names and to the **Installation id** shown on the Licence page, can cap the number of active accounts, and can carry a brand name for white-label portals (shown in the top bar and the page titles instead of "Golden Automation Systems"; logos are swapped by replacing the files in `assets/`). States: Valid → Expiring (bell reminders to administrators at 60/30/14/7/1 days, banner from 30) → Grace (contract ended, 30 days, red banner, daily reminder) → **Locked** (every page becomes the Licence page; only login, account and licence work; an administrator pastes the renewal key; nothing is deleted). Wrong signature / other address / other installation = locked at once. The portal remembers the latest date it has seen, so turning the server clock back does not help.

**Vendor kit** (`golden-license-kit.zip`) — keep it private, never upload to any portal: `vendor.key` (the secret key — back it up; if lost, a new pair must be generated and `app/lic.php` re-shipped everywhere), `issue.php` to issue keys, `verify.php` to inspect one, `register.csv` of everything issued, README with the command line. Renewal of a customer = issue a new key with the new end date and e-mail it; the customer pastes it.

**Tasks and reminders** — Work › Tasks (staff). Tasks per project / sub-project / vessel with due date + time, priority, assignee (a person or "Project team" = project manager + administrators), notes, and a recurrence like Microsoft To Do: daily / weekly (chosen weekdays) / monthly (day of month, 31 = last day) / yearly, every n, optional end date. One row per repeating task: completing it (the ring) moves it to the next occurrence and logs the completion; Skip moves on without one. Reminders: at the due time or 15 min … 1 week before, delivered through the notification bell to the assignee or the project team; overdue tasks are reminded again once a day. Views: My day · Assigned to me · Planned (grouped Overdue / Today / Tomorrow / This week / Next 30 days / Later) · Done · All, with project / sub-project / type / assignee filters; the menu badge counts your tasks due today or overdue. **Report reminders** (administrators, button top right) creates the standing reminders for every active project — weekly report Fri 15:00, monthly report 1st 10:00, timesheets Mon 10:00, vessel history 1st 11:00 — and a progress update per sub-project Thu 12:00, assigned to each project manager (or to one person you choose); it never duplicates existing ones. Reminders are generated by the same once-a-minute sweep as the notifications, i.e. on any page load; the Golden A/S manual has a new section with figures.

## 21. v1.22.1 — GAS-CP-R22-003 (sunrise / sunset and weather on the clock strip — option D)

No database step. The GR clock now carries a small badge (icon + current temperature); clicking **any** city in the strip (GR, AE, GB, IT, NL, DE, US — not UTC) opens a pop-over with sunrise → sunset and daylight length, the conditions now, today's min → max, rain chance and wind, and the hourly forecast 06:00–21:00. Esc or a click outside closes it. Data comes from Open-Meteo (free, no key): the **user's browser** fetches it directly, so GoDaddy and the Sucuri WAF are not involved and the server does nothing; results are cached in the browser for 30 minutes. If the service is unreachable the pop-over says so and the badge simply stays hidden — nothing else is affected. Coordinates per city are in the `$clocks` array of `views/_layout.php`; labels are in the five languages; works in light and dark themes.

## 22. v1.22.2 — GAS-CP-R22-004 (licence verification without sodium)

The first licence build used PHP's `sodium` extension, which GoDaddy's PHP does not load ("Undefined constant SODIUM_CRYPTO_SIGN_BYTES" on Settings › Licence). Verification now uses the OpenSSL extension (ECDSA P-256 / SHA-256), present on every GoDaddy PHP. The key format is **GASL2**; the earlier GASL1 key is refused with a clear message. Upload the patch, Ctrl+F5, then paste the **new** `GAS-CP-2026-0001_goldensas.com.lic` (same serial, same validity to 31 Dec 2036). The vendor kit zip is replaced too (new `vendor.key` / `vendor.pub`); the old Ed25519 pair is kept inside it under `old-ed25519/` for the record only. The self-check now reports the OpenSSL extension.

## 23. v1.22.3 — GAS-CP-R22-005 (tasks on project / sub-project, task form)

No database step. Open tasks now appear where the work is: on **Fleet › Projects** under each expanded project (after the offers block — the task's sub-project is shown) and on the **sub-project page, Process tab** (tasks of that sub-project). Each list has the Done ring, + New task with project / sub-project pre-filled, and an "All tasks" link to the filtered Tasks page. In the task form the sub-project drop-down is no longer greyed out until a project is chosen: it lists every sub-project grouped per project; choosing a project narrows the list, choosing a sub-project fills in its project.

## 24. v1.22.4 — GAS-CP-R22-006 (task types configurable)

**Database step:** re-import `install/migrate_cp_r22_license_tasks-goldensas_portal.sql` (it now also creates `task_kinds` with the 11 built-in types; the earlier tables report "already exists", which is fine). If you have not run the R22 file yet, one import covers everything.

**Settings › Task types** (administrators): add your own types (Class survey, Commissioning report, …) with a colour and an order; hide built-in types you don't use (hidden types stay on existing tasks); a type in use cannot be deleted. Built-in names stay translated in the five languages; your own are shown as typed. In the task form, administrators also find **"+ New type…"** at the end of the Type list — type the name and save the task; the type is created and applied in one step. Types apply to any task, so each sub-project can carry whatever document/report types you define.

## 25. v1.22.5 — GAS-CP-R22-007 (file categories configurable)

**Database step:** import `install/migrate_cp_r22c_file_kinds-goldensas_portal.sql` (changes `sp_files.kind` from a fixed list to free text and creates `file_kinds` with the 18 built-in categories). Then upload the patch, Ctrl+F5.

**Settings › File categories** (administrators): the Category list of the sub-project Files tab is now yours — add categories (Commissioning report, Cable schedule, …) with a **document code** (goes into the file number: `GY-85-02-Y.01-CMR-001`) and the **archive folder** they file into (Files, Drawings, Owner approvals, Class, Tests, Weekly notes, Reports, Service reports); order and hide built-ins (hidden ones stay on existing files); a category in use cannot be deleted. The Files tab has a "Manage file categories" link for administrators. The categories the portal assigns itself (service-report document / photo / signature, offer document, issued document, attachments) are listed as *system* and no longer appear as raw keys like `fk.fsr_doc` in the Files list — they now read "Service report document" etc. in all five languages.

## 26. v1.23.0 — GAS-CP-R23-001 (working hours, national holidays, hour classification, manhour rates)

**Database step:** import `install/migrate_cp_r23_calendars-goldensas_portal.sql` (tables `work_calendars`, `work_hours`, `work_holidays`; `customer_users.work_calendar`; `disciplines.rate_*`; `timesheet_lines.auto_type`). Then upload the patch, Ctrl+F5.

**Settings › Working hours.** Two calendars come pre-filled — **GR** (Mon–Fri 08:30–17:00, 30 min break = 8 h; Saturday counts as overtime, Sunday as holiday) and **IT** (Mon–Fri 08:30–17:30, 60 min break = 8 h) — edit them freely or add another country. Holidays per year: press **"Add the standard holidays of GR for 2026"** (fixed dates plus Clean Monday, Good Friday, Easter Monday, Whit Monday computed from the Orthodox Easter; for Italy Easter Monday from the Western Easter), **import an Excel** (columns Calendar, Date, Name — template on the page, dry run first) or add single days. Do this for 2026 and 2027 after installing. Each staff account follows a calendar: Settings › Portal accounts, next to the discipline (default GR).

**Timesheets.** On save, the engineer's calendar reclassifies lines: working day → hours up to the daily normal stay normal, the rest becomes overtime (line split, "auto" mark); public holiday or Sunday → holiday; Saturday → overtime (per weekday you choose Overtime or Holiday for non-working days). Hours already marked overtime / holiday by the engineer are kept. The save message says how many hours moved.

**Disciplines.** Settings › Disciplines now has Normal / Overtime / Holiday €/h per discipline; Reports › Manhours shows a "Manhour cost by discipline" table (hours × rates) for roles with financial visibility.

**Help files** updated: Golden A/S manual gained "Working hours, holidays and hour classification" and "Configurable lists" (task types, file categories) with figures; both manuals describe the clock strip weather; this guide carries §19–§26.

## 28. v1.23.2 — GAS-CP-R23-003 (inspection reports, form SF-GLD-020)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › select `goldensas_portal` › Import › `customer/install/migrate_cp_r23_isr-goldensas_portal.sql` (or `migrate_cp_r23_isr.sql` with the database already selected). It creates `isr_reports`, `isr_items`, `isr_events`, adds `isr_id` / `isr_item` to `sp_files` and registers the two system file categories `isr_doc` / `isr_file` (code ISR, archive folder `inspections`). Settings › About shows the line **R23-003** green once it is in; `install/cp_selfcheck.php` prints `R23-003 migration (inspection reports)`.

**What it does.** Sub-project › Service reports tab › **+ New inspection report** (also *Inspection reports* on the project list). The cover carries the SF-GLD-020 report data (date, inspector, customer, PIC, vessel, location, class / flag, builder / year, hull no, subject) and the three introductory texts. **+ Add system** adds a block: the system is picked from the vessel-systems list plus the template's section names (126 entries) — the checklist is pre-filled from the template's question bank (34 systems, 249 questions) — or **Other…** with a free description. Each block has condition as found, checklist (edit / remove / add; *Fill from template*), class regulations, risk level (low / medium / high / critical) with analysis, proposed corrective actions and attachments with captions (images become numbered figures). Blocks are added, re-ordered and removed like the FSR tasks; attachments follow their block.

**Workflow.** Draft → *Submit for check* → *Check & issue* by someone other than the author (administrators may issue anything) → PDF + Word generated and archived under `…/inspections/<report no>_R<n>.pdf|docx`; attachments as `<report no>_<nn>_sys<n>.<ext>`. Report numbers `ISR-yyyymmdd-<initials>-nn`. Owners see issued reports only (Work › Service Reports › Inspection reports, sub-project Service tab); notifications go to project staff on submit / issue / re-open and to the owner on issue. Re-opening an issued report and issuing again produces the next revision.

**Files:** `app/isr.php`, `app/isr_bank.php`, `views/isr_edit.php`, `views/isr_view.php`, `views/_isr_list.php`, `views/isr_list.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r23_isr*.sql`; touched `index.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `app/ar.php`, `app/fk.php`, `app/nf.php`, `app/version.php`, `views/projects.php`, `views/subproject.php`, `views/reports.php`, `views/support.php`, both manuals, `assets/portal.css`, all five language files (+100 keys each), manual figures `flow-isr.svg`, `g-isr-edit.jpg`, `g-isr.jpg`, `g-isr-list.jpg`, `c-isr.jpg`.

## 30. v1.24.1 — GAS-CP-R24-002 (online presence, project links in breadcrumbs)

No database step. **Online** now means *signed in and connected*: every open portal page sends a one-request heartbeat per minute (`?p=ping`, 204/401, never extends the idle time-out), so a person stays in "N online" as long as the portal is open in a browser, disappears about **3 minutes after closing the browser** (the only way a closed window can be detected) and **immediately on sign-out or idle time-out** (last_seen_at cleared). The Login history hint says so. **Breadcrumbs:** the project code on the sub-project page — and on FSR/ISR, offer, approval, timesheet, drawings, monthly, GA overlay, work-order pages — links to the project on the project list, opened and scrolled into view (`?p=projects&q=<code>&f=1&open=<id>#pr<id>`). Files: `app/auth.php`, `app/pm.php` (`pr_link`), `index.php`, `assets/portal.js`, the views above, five language files, manual.

## 33. v1.25.0 — GAS-CP-R25-001 (sub-project teams — option A, active sub-projects only)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r25_teams-goldensas_portal.sql` (table `sp_team`). About › R25-001 green; self-check prints `R25-001 migration (sub-project teams)`.

**How it works.** Sub-project › Process tab › **Team** box: the project manager or an administrator ticks the engineers (grouped by discipline; the PM is always in). `app/tm.php`: `tm_my_subs()` = active sub-projects of active projects where the user is in the team or is the project's PM (administrators: all) — combined with the branch scope; `tm_allowed_ids($pid)`; `tm_restricted()`. Forms: `timesheet_edit` (projects and lines limited, no "main project" line for a limited engineer), `fsr_edit`, `isr_edit` (sub-project list limited; "whole project" option hidden; 403 page when nothing is assigned on that project). Saves refuse an unassigned sub-project (`tm.not_allowed`) in `ts_save_from_post`, `fsr_save_from_post`, `isr_save_from_post`. Dashboard: *My sub-projects* card for limited engineers. Settings › Portal accounts: **strict / open** switch (`cp_settings.tm_strict`): strict = everyone limited; open (default) = only engineers with at least one assignment are limited, so you can roll teams out gradually. Accounts export/import: *Assigned sub-projects* column (`GY-85-02 Y.01, …`) — on import it replaces the person's assignments; leave the column out to keep them.

## 35. v1.26.0 — GAS-CP-R26-001 (planning board — option 1)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r26_planning-goldensas_portal.sql` (tables `plan_entries`, `plan_cars`). About › R26-001 green; self-check prints `R26-001 migration (planning board)`.

**What it does.** Work › **Planning** (`views/planning.php`, `app/pl.php`): month board, engineers × days grouped by discipline, weekends/holidays from the GR calendar greyed, today framed, future unplanned workdays hatched, totals (work / leave / sick / vessels) per engineer. Editor: engineer, From/To, what (sub-project · project · leave · sick · parental · strike · office · warehouse · training · travel · other), car, note, "include weekends and holidays", "replace". Several entries per engineer and day are allowed. A sub-project assignment inserts the engineer into `sp_team` (R25-001). Chip click → details + delete; `?p=plan_items&project=&sub=` returns the **open items** (FSR task open issues + issued ISR corrective actions) shown in the editor and on the engineer's own view. **My planning** (`?p=planning&me=1`, automatic for non-planners): own row + this and next week with car, note and open items; dashboard card **My week**. Planners: administrators, the *Project Manager* Golden A/S role, PMs of active projects. **Cars** master (admins, bottom of the page). **Import Excel matrix** (`pl_import`): finds the date row, matches names in any order (Greek/Latin transliteration, surname-only when unique), vessels → active project of the vessel, status codes (ADEIA, SICK, PARENTAL, APERGIA, warehouse, GOLDEN), splits "A / B" cells, keeps unknown cells as *other* with the text; dry-run report lists unmatched names and codes. **Export year** (`?p=planning_export&year=`) returns the matrix with counters. Nav entry Work › Planning; manual section "Planning board" with three figures; Support topic.

**Files:** `app/pl.php`, `views/planning.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r26_planning*.sql`, `assets/manual/g-planning*.jpg`; touched `index.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/dashboard.php`, `views/_manual_golden.php`, `views/support.php`, `assets/portal.css`, five language files (+71 keys), version, self-check.

**Suggested next step (v1.26.1):** vessel clearances from the ADEIES sheet (person × vessel "OK") with a warning on the board, leave requests by the engineer with PM approval, and timesheet pre-fill from the plan.

**Planning disciplines (same release):** Settings › Disciplines › *Planning board — disciplines on the board*: tick the disciplines whose engineers appear on Work › Planning (plus "No discipline" for accounts without one); nothing ticked = everybody. Stored in `cp_settings.pl_disciplines`; `pl_people()` honours it, the Excel import matches every account but skips people whose discipline is not on the board (listed in the report). Files: `app/pl.php`, `views/admin_disciplines.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, manual figure `g-planning-discs.jpg`.

## 36. v1.27.0 — GAS-CP-R27-001 (equipment register — service history per serial number)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r27_equipment-goldensas_portal.sql` (tables `equipment`, `equipment_history`, `fsr_equipment`). About › R27-001 green; self-check prints `R27-001 migration (equipment register)`.

**What it does.** Fleet › **Equipment** (`views/equipment.php`, `app/eq.php`): the register of units by serial number — type (shore converter, UPS, drive, switchboard, automation, NAVCOM, other), maker, family, model, set, vessel **or** site, sold by us + date, warranty until + reference, first attendance, retired, contacts on board, notes. KPIs (units / in service / under warranty / sold by us), filters (search, maker, family, vessel, status), paging, Excel export (`?p=equipment_export`). **Serial number page** (`views/equipment_view.php`): unit data, the other units of the set, parts replaced (collected from the records), and the **service history** timeline with source tabs — *Imported records*, *Service report*, *Manual records*, *Ticket* — and a record editor (date, type, engineer, parts, text). **Field service reports:** `views/fsr_edit.php` shows an *Equipment serviced* box (units of the vessel to tick + free text for new serial numbers); `eq_fsr_link()` on save, `eq_fsr_sync()` on submit writes one record per unit (tasks + open issues as body, parts, engineers, type guessed from the text) and on re-open deletes them — the history always mirrors the issued report. **Vessel page:** Equipment tab (`views/vessel_view.php`). **Customers:** units of their own vessels, records of non-draft reports only, no editing. **Import maker list** (`eq_import`): header SN · Vessel Name · 1st Attendance · Vessel Contacts · Service Records · Model · SoldByUs · Date Sold · Warranty Expiration · Retired · Retirement Date; maker chosen once per file; sets `5433-001/002/003`, `4806-001/4806-002`, `98108/107`, `4299-001A/B`, `4799-001-002-003` expanded to one unit per member (set kept in `set_code`); every `dd/mm/yyyy ---` / `mm/yyyy ---` paragraph of *Service Records* becomes a dated record with the type guessed (repair / troubleshooting / commissioning / health check / upgrade / inspection / maintenance / warranty); family from the model (SPA II, SPA, Classic I/II/III, Ultra Classic, Classic, UHF, SHF, SP200, VSP5, VSP, Ultra UPS, Ultra, Elite); vessel matched to the fleet ignoring the M/Y–S/Y–M/V prefix, "M/Y …" names not found are **created without a customer** (assign them in Fleet › Vessels), other names kept as `site_name` with a warning. Re-import replaces `source = import` records only. Tested with the two real files: MAGNUS 8 rows → 8 units / 6 records; ATLAS 95 rows → 115 units / 778 records / 89 vessels, 2 sites (QATAR AIR BASE, S/R THUWAL).

**Files:** `app/eq.php`, `views/equipment.php`, `views/equipment_view.php`, `views/equipment_edit.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r27_equipment*.sql`, `assets/manual/g-equipment*.jpg`; touched `index.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `app/fsr.php`, `views/fsr_edit.php`, `views/vessel_view.php`, `views/_layout.php`, both manuals, `views/support.php`, `assets/portal.css`, five language files (+99 keys), version, self-check.

**Suggested next step (v1.27.1):** warranty / health-check reminders on the dashboard, a *Service history* PDF per serial number for the owner, and the ticket link (a ticket opened for a unit writes to its history).

## 38. v1.27.2 — GAS-CP-R27-003 (equipment ↔ inspection reports, tasks, contacts)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r27_equipment_links-goldensas_portal.sql` — extends `equipment_history.source` (`isr`, `task`) with `isr_id` / `task_id`, creates `isr_equipment`, `task_equipment`, `equipment_contacts`. About › R27-003 green; self-check prints `R27-003 migration (equipment links)`.

**Inspection reports.** `views/isr_edit.php` — *Equipment inspected* box (units of the project's vessel + new serial numbers); `isr_save_from_post` → `eq_isr_link`; `isr_issue` → `eq_isr_sync` writes one `inspection` record per unit (system: condition found [risk] — corrective actions); `isr_reopen` → sync removes them. Customers see `isr` records of issued reports only. **Tasks.** `views/task_edit.php` — *Equipment concerned* box with the units of every active project's vessel, narrowed by JS to the chosen project (`?eq=ID` pre-ticks the unit and picks the vessel's active project); `td_save_from_post` → `eq_task_link`; `td_done` → `eq_task_done` (one record per unit, kind guessed, else `maintenance`, engineer = assignee); `td_reopen` → `eq_task_reopen` deletes the latest record per unit (recurring tasks keep earlier occurrences); skipped occurrences write nothing. Tasks are internal — never shown to customers. **Contacts.** `equipment_contacts (equipment_id, contact_id, role)`; unit page card *Linked contacts* with a **picker dialog** (`<dialog>`, JSON endpoint `?p=eq_contact_search&eq=&q=&customer=&category=&vessel=1&page=` over `ct_list()`, 40 per page, already linked contacts greyed; several ticked contacts — across pages — are posted as `contact_ids[]` with one role); actions `eq_contact_add` (batch via `eq_contact_add_many`) / `eq_contact_remove`; printed on the history document (kv row *Linked contacts*). Unit page also lists the inspection reports and tasks of the unit, history tabs *Inspection reports* / *Tasks*, `eq_delete` clears the links. Also fixed: ISR view events used `created_at` instead of `at` (warnings in the log).

**Files:** `install/migrate_cp_r27_equipment_links*.sql`, `assets/manual/g-equipment-links.jpg`, `g-equipment-isr.jpg`, `g-equipment-task.jpg`; touched `app/eq.php`, `app/isr.php`, `app/td.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `views/equipment_view.php`, `views/isr_edit.php`, `views/isr_view.php`, `views/task_edit.php`, manuals, `assets/portal.css`, five language files (+21 keys), version, self-check.

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r28_offer_notes-goldensas_portal.sql` — table `offer_notes`, column `tasks.offer_id`. About › R28-001 green; self-check prints `R28-001 migration (offer notes / tasks)`.

**Internal notes.** `app/of.php`: `ofn_has / ofn_list / ofn_count / ofn_can_edit / ofn_save / ofn_delete`; card *Internal notes* on `views/offer.php` (staff only — `of_load` is unchanged, customers never receive the notes), thread with author, time, "edited" mark, edit (`?note=ID`) and delete for the author or an administrator; actions `ofn_save` / `ofn_delete` (`redirect()` gained an optional `#hash`). A new note pushes a notification `of:note` (label `of.ev_note`) to `nf_project_staff()` minus the author; the existing comment-with-status-change still goes to the trail (visible to the owner). **Tasks on offers.** `tasks.offer_id`; `td_load / td_list` join `offers ofr` (alias — `of` is a reserved word in MySQL 8); `td_save_from_post` reads `offer_id` (project / sub-project taken from the offer when empty, dropped if the project differs); `td_context()` appends "Offer <no>"; task form: *Offer* select of the offers of the listed projects narrowed per project (`?offer=ID` pre-selects and pre-sets project and sub-project); offer page: *Tasks* card (`of_tasks`) with *+ New task*. ## 41. v1.28.1 — GAS-CP-R28-002 (Freshdesk ticket history import)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r28_freshdesk-goldensas_portal.sql` — `tickets.vessel_id` becomes nullable, `tickets.legacy_id / legacy_no / legacy_type / legacy_vessel`, `ticket_comments.legacy_id`. About › R28-002 green; self-check prints `R28-002 migration (Freshdesk import)`.

**Where the files go.** FTP (not the np@ account) → `customer/storage/import/freshdesk/` (created on first visit of the page; create it by hand if the FTP client needs it). The page also uploads files up to the server's `upload_max_filesize`. The Freshdesk `Companies*.xml` files are not needed (no ticket references them).

**Module.** `app/fd.php`: `fd_files / fd_check / fd_import_batch` (XMLReader streaming, 100 tickets per request, `set_time_limit(300)`), `fd_vessel_id` (fleet matched by `fd_norm` — prefixes M/Y S/Y P/S M/V M/T MS RV CS stripped, "(ex NAME)" tried, spaces ignored; non-vessel values `Unknown / IT Tickets / codes` → no vessel; creation optional), `fd_project_sub` (`PRJ.000616.Y.002` → project + sub-project), `fd_staff_id` (agent name → staff account, surname + first 3 letters), `fd_status / fd_priority / fd_category`, `fd_text` (HTML mails → text), users learnt from requesters / responders into `storage/import/freshdesk/users.json`. Notes: `helpdesk-note` elements; sources 4 (system meta) and 15 (automated mails) skipped, 13 (summary) internal. Page `views/fd_import.php` (admin, Settings tab *Freshdesk import*): file table with Check / Import (create missing vessels) / delete, upload form, check report, progress card with `<meta refresh>` continuation (`?p=fd_import&run=<file>&go=1&cv=1` runs one batch in `index.php` before rendering), "How it works". `tk_load / tk_list / dashboard` use `LEFT JOIN vessels`; ticket lists order by date inside the status groups; event `imported` labelled `tk.ev_imported`.

**Sandbox run:** Tickets11.xml 42 → 33 imported (9 deleted / spam), 40 conversations; Tickets0.xml 300 → 300 imported, 1847 conversations, 17 equipment records, 0.6 s check, 1.8 s per batch of 100. Whole export (3 342 tickets, 16 289 notes, 285 vessel names): ~13 files, a few minutes.

## 45. v1.29.0 — GAS-CP-R29-001 (open-issues register per sub-project)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `customer/install/migrate_cp_r29_issues-goldensas_portal.sql` (table `sp_issues`). About › R29-001 green; self-check prints `R29-001 migration (open-issues register)`.

**Module `app/iss.php`.** `iss_list / iss_get / iss_open_count / iss_save / iss_set_status / iss_delete`, `iss_fsr_sync($reportId)` (called on FSR submit — both submit paths — and on re-open: one entry per task block with "open issues" text, keyed `fsr_id + fsr_seq`; text follows the report while the entry is open; blocks whose text was removed drop their open entry; closed entries are never touched; a report back in draft withdraws its open entries), `iss_backfill($pid,$sid)` (first opening of the tab syncs reports submitted before the register existed), `iss_report_tables($pid,$sid,$from,$to)` (rows for PDF/Word: open + closed in the window). **Tab** `views/_issues.php` on the sub-project page (count badge = open entries): filters (search, status, who has to act), paging (`ipage`), close with note / start / re-open, edit, admin delete; owners see external entries, can add, close and re-open (not "in progress"). **Reports:** weekly (`wk_sections` section 5 → *Open issues* + *Issues closed <week>*; the old raw FSR table remains only for spare-part tickets or when the register is absent; `wv.s4` renamed), monthly (`rp_sp_docx` section *Open issues*, closed in last 30 days), vessel history (`vh_tables`, per project). **Planning** `pl_open_items` reads the register when present. Fixed `$bd` footer closures in `fsr_render_pdf` and `rp_sp_docx`. Manuals (both), Support topics, figures `g-issues.jpg`, `c-issues.jpg`, `g-issues-weekly.jpg`; +40 lang keys ×5.

## 46. v1.29.1 — GAS-CP-R29-002 (open issues: Excel import / export)

No database step. `iss_import($path,$pid,$sid,$dry)` (header "Issue" required; Details · To be done by · Priority · Raised · Due · Assigned to · Status · Closed on · Closing note · Visibility · Sub-project; existing title in the same sub-project → update of the columns present in the sheet only; otherwise manual entry; names matched to staff accounts, unknown names warned; dates dd/mm/yyyy, yyyy-mm-dd or Excel serial), `iss_export()` (`?p=issues_export&project=&sub=`), template `install/templates/issues-template.xlsx` (`?p=import&tpl=issues`), card on the Open issues tab with check-only, action `iss_import`.

## 47. v1.29.2 / v1.29.3 — GAS-CP-R29-003 / R29-004 (issue numbers and categories)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `migrate_cp_r29_issue_no_and_categories-goldensas_portal.sql` (both parts in one file: `sp_issues.issue_no` with unique key; `sp_issues.category`; table `issue_categories` seeded with the ten built-in categories). About › R29-003 and R29-004 green; self-check prints both lines.

**Numbers (R29-003).** `issue_no` = `OI-<project code>-nnn`, running per project, never reused (`iss_number()`); entries created before get theirs the first time the tab opens (`iss_number_missing()`); shown in the tab (first column, anchor link), the reports, the Excel export (column *No*) and accepted by the import (*No* column matches an existing entry and may retitle it); `?p=issue&no=OI-…` opens the entry; the search box finds numbers. **Categories (R29-004).** `issue_categories` (skey, label, tone, sort_order, is_builtin, is_active) mirrors `task_kinds`; `iss_cat_defs / iss_cats / iss_cat_pill / iss_cat_of` (slug from a typed label or abbreviation — MECH, ELECTR, "spare" …); category on the form, as a pill in the list, as a filter (`icat`), in `iss_list(['category'])`, prefixed in square brackets in the report rows, column *Category* in export / import / template; Settings › **Issue categories** (`views/admin_issue_cats.php`, actions `isc_save` / `isc_delete`, built-ins re-ordered / re-coloured / hidden, own categories added, "Other" always on, in-use categories cannot be deleted). +31 lang keys ×5 (R29-004) in addition to the R29-003 ones.

## 48. v1.29.4 — GAS-CP-R29-005 (issues workbook with drop-downs)

No database step. `xlsxw.php`: sheets accept `'lists' => [col => [values]]` (inline list validation) and `'listrefs' => [col => 'Lists!$A$2:$A$9']` (reference validation) → `<dataValidations>` on rows 2–2000. `iss_template_download()` (`?p=import&tpl=issues`, replaces the static file) and `iss_export()` both add a *Lists* sheet (categories as configured, who acts, priority, status, visibility, staff names) and validations on Category (D), To be done by (F), Priority (G), Assigned to (J), Status (K), Visibility (N). Import value maps extended with BG / UK labels. Static `install/templates/issues-template.xlsx` kept only as fallback. **Several sub-projects per workbook:** the *Sub-project* column (drop-down, Lists column G = sub-projects of the main project; template link carries `&sub=`) routes each row to its sub-project even when importing from a sub-project tab; empty = current sub-project; a code of another project falls back to the current one with a warning (`iss.im_sub_fallback`).

## 49. v1.29.5 — GAS-CP-R29-006 (sorting)

No database step. Open issues: `iss_list(['sort','dir'])` → `iss_order_sql()` (whitelist: no, title, category, source, side, assignee, raised, due, status, priority, sub; default keeps open → priority → due → raised); header links `isort` / `idir` on the tab (`$th()` helper), kept by the pager; the table carries `data-sorted="server"`. Global: `assets/portal.js` turns the header cells of every table (with a thead, ≥ 2 rows, not `data-sorted` / `data-nosort`, header without inputs or links) into click-to-sort — numbers (incl. 1.234,56, %, d, h), dates (yyyy-mm-dd, dd/mm/yyyy, "5 Mar 2025", Greek months) and text (locale, numeric-aware); tables with row/col spans are left alone; CSS `.th-sort` / `.sort-ind`.

## 50. v1.29.6 — GAS-CP-R29-007 / R29-008 (report colour code · remembered list filters)

No database step for either patch. Upload the patch zip (binary), Ctrl+F5; About shows 1.29.6 with R29-007 and R29-008 green.

**Colour code — option B (R29-007).** New module `app/rc.php` (required after `iss.php`): `RC_HEX` (red 9B2C2C · amber C89B3C · green 2F7D5C · navy 0B1E2D · grey C8CED6), `rc_due($date)` (past → red, ≤ `RC_SOON_DAYS` = 7 → amber), `rc_days()`, `rc_status($word)`, `rc_issue()`, `rc_ticket()`, `rc_pct()`, `rc_legend_pdf()` / `rc_legend_docx()` (legend under the title, page 1 only), `rc_bar_pdf()` (1.5 mm bar at the left of a row). `DocxWriter::table()` accepts `'_tone'` (narrow filled marker column) and `'_hl' => [col => tone]` (bold coloured cell) per row. Applied in `wk.php` (weekly: deadlines, delays, parts tickets, offers pending, approvals, work orders, FSRs, class/JO due dates, tickets, progress deltas), `vh.php` (projects, tickets), `rp.php` (stages, drawings, owner/class items, tests, issues), `isr.php` (summary table bar + risk colour, per-item heading bar), `eq.php` (legend), `iss_report_tables()` (rows carry `tone`/`hl`), `fsr.php` (Open issues box accent only — amber, red when disputed; no legend by design). Lang keys `rc.*`.

**Remembered filters (R29-008).** New module `app/flt.php`: `FLT_PAGES` maps each list page to its filter keys and context keys (`view`, `id`, `tab`); `flt_apply($page)` runs in `index.php` right before the view dispatch, GET requests only. Rules: `reset=1` → forget the slot and show defaults (every Clear button now carries it — `views/*.php`); any filter key present in the URL → store the selection in `$_SESSION['flt'][slot]` (empty values included); no filter key → merge the stored selection into `$_GET` / `$_REQUEST` and set `flt_restored()`. Slots: `page[:view][:id:tab]` — Tickets Open / All / Activity, Timesheets and Tasks views are separate, the sub-project Open issues tab is per sub-project (`when => tab=issues`). Pages: tickets, offers, equipment, vessels, projects, contacts, companies, timesheets, manhours, tasks, jo_list, approvals, kb, admin_users, admin_log, admin_customers, subproject (issues tab). Exports run before `flt_apply` and keep using the explicit URL. `_layout.php` sets `<body data-flt="1">` and `data-flt-restored` (translated text) on `<html>`; `portal.js` appends the `.flt-restored` mark to `.fltbar-actions` (or next to the Clear link). Lang keys `flt.restored`, `flt.title` (5 languages, 2838 keys).

**Help.** Both manuals: "Lists that remember your filters" (with `g-filters-remembered.jpg`) and "Colour code in the reports" (with `g-colour-code.jpg`); Support topics link both.

## 51. v1.29.7 — GAS-CP-R29-009 (report tables aligned)

No database step. `rc_fit_cols($cols, 180)` in `app/rc.php` scales a column set proportionally to the text width (last column absorbs the rounding); called at the top of `$hrow` and `$drow` in `wk.php`, `vh.php`, `eq.php` and `ts.php`, so the mm widths written in the table definitions become proportions and every table ends on the same right edge as the title block and the KPI tiles. FSR and ISR tables already used `$W` directly; Word tables are equal-width on a 9000-twip grid and were already aligned.

## 54. v1.30.0 — GAS-CP-R30-001 (one record per hull)

**Database step (required):** phpMyAdmin › `goldensas_portal` › Import `migrate_cp_r30_vessel_unify-goldensas_portal.sql` — `vessels.former_names`, `vessels.merged_into`, tables `vessel_aliases` (unique `alias_norm`) and `vessel_companies` (vessel × customer with role). About › R30-001 green; self-check prints the R30 line.

**Module `app/vm.php`.** `vm_norm()` (matching key: prefix M/Y·S/Y·M/V·M/T·P/S·HSC removed, brackets and "ex/old/new" dropped, punctuation out, Greek look-alike capitals latinised), `vm_prefix()`, `vm_canonical()` (`<prefix> <NAME>`), `vm_ex_name()`, `vm_find($name,$imo,$cid)` (IMO → alias → normalised name → bracketed former name; retired records resolve to the master), `vm_resolve()`, aliases (`vm_alias_add/aliases/remove`), related companies (`vm_related/related_set/related_remove`, roles owner·manager·yard·agent·contractor·charterer·other), `vm_counts()`, `vm_ref_columns()` (every `vessel_id` column from information_schema — a new table joins the merge by itself), `vm_merge($master,$dups)` (one transaction: all vessel_id columns re-pointed — `contact_vessels`/`vessel_companies` with UPDATE IGNORE + delete of the clashing rest —, `fsr_reports`/`isr_reports.vessel_name` text, duplicate's customer → related company when different, IMO/yard filled, former names kept, duplicate name and aliases → aliases of the master, duplicate `is_active=0, merged_into=master`), `vm_groups()` (suggestions), `vm_merge_map($path,$dry)` (sheet "Merge map (for import)": Master name · Duplicate record).

**Hooks.** `pm_find_vessel()` (project / vessel imports): `vm_find` first — found under another customer → related company + alias, never a second record; new vessels created with the canonical name. `fd_vessel_id()`: `vm_find` first; a name without vessel prefix (and not GY…/HULL…) is reported under `not_vessel` and never creates a vessel. `ct_find_vessel()`: `vm_find` first. Equipment import: `vm_find` fallback. `vessel_save`: name canonicalised, duplicate hull refused (`vm.dup_hull` with the existing name), `former_names` and `aliases` saved. `index.php`: `?p=vessel&id=<retired>` redirects to the master; vessels list hides `merged_into IS NOT NULL`. `xlsx_rows()`: absolute sheet targets (`/xl/worksheets/…`, openpyxl workbooks) resolved.

**UI.** Settings › Vessel duplicates (`views/admin_vessel_merge.php`, actions `vm_merge` with optional `rename`, `vm_map_import` dry/apply): merge map card, merge-two-records card, suggested groups (master radio, duplicate checkboxes, holds, source batch by created date), retired list. Vessel page: Related companies card (`vm_related_set/remove`), former names and aliases line. Vessel form: Former names, Aliases textarea, alias chips with ×, naming rule hint. Lang `vm.*` + `im.check`, `im.row` (2915 keys). Manual: "One record per vessel" section with two figures; Support topic linked.

**Live clean-up order.** 1) upload patch, run the migration; 2) Settings › Vessel duplicates › Check with the reviewed `GAS-vessel-unification-plan-20261021.xlsx`, read the plan, Apply; 3) walk the remaining suggested groups and the "Possible typos" sheet with Merge two records; 4) the 19 "Probably not vessels" records: create them as customers/companies where needed, move their tickets with the ticket's Project assignment card, set the vessel record inactive.

## 60. v1.31.3 — GAS-CP-R30-007 (searchable drop-downs)

No database step. `portal.js`: every `<select>` (not multiple, > 8 options, not `data-nosearch`, not in pagers / line tables) is enhanced — the native select stays in the form (hidden, `select.ss-native`), a `.ss-btn` shows the choice, a fixed `.ss-panel` (search input + filtered list, highlighted match, keyboard) is appended to body; picking sets `selectedIndex` and dispatches `change` (inline `onchange` and listeners fire); a MutationObserver re-syncs when options or `disabled` change (cascading selects); the `invalid` event opens the panel and marks the button; panel re-positions on scroll, closes on outside click / Esc / resize; new selects added later are enhanced too. Texts from `data-ss-search` / `data-ss-none` on `<html>` (lang `ss.search`, `ss.none`). CSS `.ss-*` light + dark.

Planning page: template download link in the import card. Lang `pl.tpl_*`.

## 66. v1.32.4 — GAS-CP-R31-005 (Help search)

**What changed.** Library › Support now opens with a search box that searches every manual edition the user is allowed to read (Owner edition for customers; Golden A/S edition, Owner edition and the Project Manager handbook for staff). Every word typed must match the section's title or text; title matches and the exact phrase rank higher. Results show the edition, the section title and a snippet with the words highlighted; a result opens the manual at that section with the other sections folded. The Golden A/S and Owner manuals describe the feature.
