## 67. v1.32.5 — GAS-CP-R31-006 (Offers hidden from staff without financial data)

**What changed.** The "Financial data" switch of a Golden A/S role (Settings › Golden A/S roles) now removes the offers module entirely for the people in that role, instead of only masking the amounts: no Commercial › Offers menu entry, no offer tile on the dashboard (a Tasks tile keeps the four-tile row), no offer trees, counters, sub-project pills or "+ New offer" on the projects and sub-project pages, no Offer field on the task form, no Offer statuses tab in Settings and no offer import card. `?p=offers`, `?p=offer`, `?p=offer_edit` and `?p=admin_offer_statuses` typed by hand, and the offer actions (`of_save`, `of_status`, `of_decide`, `of_import`, offer notes), return them to the dashboard with the notice "Offers are not available to your role (financial data hidden)". Offer notifications (sent, approved, rejected, notes) are no longer pushed to those users. Owners and administrators are unaffected; roles default to financial data visible.

**Files.** `app/of.php` (`of_visible`, `of_require`, `of_users`), `app/nf.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `index.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/dashboard.php`, `views/projects.php`, `views/subproject.php`, `views/task_edit.php`, `views/import.php`, `_manual_golden.php`, lang files (1 key, 3166), `app/version.php`.

**Deploy.** Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL. Check: sign in as a staff member whose role has Financial data off — no Offers in the menu, `?p=offers` returns to the dashboard with the notice.

## 68. v1.32.6 — GAS-CP-R31-007 (@mentions in notes and comments)

**What changed.** Typing `@` in a ticket comment (internal or external), an offer internal note, a sub-project weekly note or comment, an owner-approval comment, a weekly works forecast reply or a contact note opens a list of the Golden A/S colleagues (active staff and administrators); the handle is the part of the e-mail before `@` (`@np`, `@ib`, `@kostas`), typing filters by handle or by the start of a name, arrows + Enter/Tab pick, Esc closes. On save, every colleague mentioned gets one notification "<author> mentioned you in <ticket no / offer no / project › sub-project / approval no / forecast no / contact name>" under Work › Mentions, whose Open link lands on the note; the author never notifies himself. The handle is highlighted (`b.mt`) wherever the note is displayed; unknown handles and e-mail addresses are left as typed. Owners can mention staff too; staff cannot be mentioned on offer notes unless their role sees financial data (R31-006).

**Files.** New `customer/app/mt.php` (`mt_staff`, `mt_handles`, `mt_parse`, `mt_notify`, `mt_html`, `mt_users_json`), `index.php` (require), `app/nf.php` (group/source labels `mt`), `app/pm_actions.php` (tk_comment, oa_comment, note_save, cm_post, ct_note hooks), `app/of.php` (ofn_save), `app/wf.php` (wf_comment_add), views ticket / offer / subproject / approval / wf_view / contact (`data-mt` textareas, `mt_html` rendering), `views/_layout.php` (`data-mt-users` on `<html>`), `assets/portal.js` (autocomplete), `assets/portal.css`, lang (4 keys, 3170), manuals (Golden, Owner, PM), `app/version.php`.

**Deploy.** Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5 (new JS/CSS), no SQL. Check: open a ticket, type `@` in the comment box — the list of colleagues appears; post `@<handle> test` and the colleague sees "… mentioned you in TK-…" in the bell.

## 69. v1.32.7 — GAS-CP-R31-008 (E-mail card with attachments; To instead of Bcc)

**What changed.** The two "✉ E-mail all" buttons (Contacts list, vessel contacts block on vessel / project / sub-project pages) put the addresses in **To** instead of Bcc. New **✉ E-mail** card (staff only) at the foot of an offer, a weekly vessel report, a submitted field service report, a checked/issued inspection report and an issued weekly works forecast: recipients in To — the vessel's active contacts (ticked), the customer's portal accounts (ticked when there is no contact), the FSR's person in charge, plus a free field for more addresses — your own address in Cc, a pre-filled subject ("Offer <no> — <title> — <vessel>", "Weekly report <doc no> — <vessel> — week <iso>", …), a courteous text signed with your name and the branch, and the documents as tick-able attachments (offer file; FSR/ISR PDF + Word; weekly report and forecast PDF + Word generated on the fly). Two buttons: **Outlook draft** downloads an `.eml` (multipart/mixed, header `X-Unsent: 1`) that Outlook opens as a new editable message with the attachments inside; **Mail app** builds a `mailto:` with recipients, Cc, subject and text (no attachments — browsers cannot attach files through mailto). The portal still sends no mail itself; nothing is logged.

**Files.** New `customer/app/em.php` (`em_people`, `em_card_html`, `em_attachment`, `em_build`, `em_draft_from_post`), `index.php` (require), `app/pm_actions.php` (action `em_draft`), views offer / fsr_view / isr_view / wf_view / weekly_vessel (card), contacts / _contacts_vessel (To), `assets/portal.js` (mailto builder), `assets/portal.css`, lang (24 keys, 3194), manuals (Golden, PM), `app/version.php`.

**Deploy.** Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL. Check: open an offer, expand ✉ E-mail, press Outlook draft — an .eml downloads; double-click it in Windows Explorer and Outlook opens a new message with the offer attached. Note for Mac users: Apple Mail opens an .eml as a received message, not a draft — use Mail app and attach the file, or Outlook for Mac (which does open X-Unsent drafts).

## 71. v1.33.0 — GAS-CP-R32-001 (E-mail templates in Settings)

**What changed.** Settings › Lists & categories › **E-mail templates** (administrators): a register of templates — kind (Offer, Weekly vessel report, Field service report, Inspection report, Weekly works forecast, E-mail all (contacts), Signature), name, language (any / EN / EL / IT / BG / UK), branch (any / one), customer (any / one), subject, text, active. Buttons per kind to add one pre-filled with the built-in text; edit, duplicate (inactive copy), delete; preview with sample data showing which template applies; the built-in text of the kind; **Test draft** downloads an .eml with the rendered sample to your own address. Placeholders `{no} {title} {vessel} {customer} {project} {week} {doc} {user} {branch} {date} {signature}`; the signature template is inserted at `{signature}` or appended. Resolution: most specific active template — customer + branch + language (score 7) down to any (0); a template bound to a different customer / branch / language is excluded; no template → built-in text (`et.d_*` lang keys, same wording as before). The language is the most common locale of the customer's portal accounts, else the sender's. The ✉ E-mail card (offer, weekly report, FSR, ISR, forecast) and both "E-mail all" buttons (contacts list, vessel contacts block — now with subject and text too) use `em_text()` → `et_render()`. Administrators see an "Edit the template ›" link under the card.

**Deploy.** 1) phpMyAdmin → select `goldensas_portal` → import `migrate_cp_r32_email_templates-goldensas_portal.sql` (one table, no data). 2) Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5. 3) Settings › About shows 1.33.0 and the R32 check green; Settings › Lists & categories shows E-mail templates. Until a template is saved everything reads exactly as in v1.32.7.

## 72. v1.34.0 — GAS-CP-R33-001 (Rate cards register · Offer builder)

**What changed.**
- **Rate cards** (Settings › Lists & categories › Rate cards, administrators): import of the master file `GAS-PL-<year>_R<n>-service-rates.xlsx` (sheets Document · Hourly rate cards · Remote support & tokens · Fixed service prices). One rate list per branch column pair found (`GAS Local (EUR)` / `GAS Out of Attica / Overseas (EUR)`, `GIT …`); a branch whose price cells are all empty is skipped (it uses the GAS list). Check-only first; importing supersedes the previous active list of that branch; lists can be superseded / re-activated / deleted; the page lists the lines per section and rate card.
- **Offer builder** (staff with "May prepare offers" + financial data; administrators): + New offer asks vessel (required), project (projects of the vessel, or "vessel in general" → the vessel's general project `GEN-<id>` is created on first use), sub-project, ticket (`?ticket=` from the ticket page), layout (Service / System / Remote support plan), VAT %, validity days; "Build it in the portal" saves a **draft** (new built-in status, hidden from owners; portal number `<prefix>-OF-<year>-<nnn>` or the branch series). On the offer page: **Lines** (from the rate cards of the offer's branch — card › line, local / overseas zone, qty, discount; free lines; heading lines; table edit of qty / price / discount / VAT; ↑ ↓ ×; net / VAT / gross stored on the offer, `amount` = net), **Texts** (seven blocks copied from Settings › Offer texts templates in the customer's language, editable; reset), **Document** (PDF / Word preview `?p=offer_doc&id=&fmt=`; **Issue Rn** archives both via `ar_issue` under the offer number, sets the PDF as `file_id`, draft → Sent with the sent date and validity). The offer document: branch logo and legal lines, cover table (offer no, subject, customer, vessel, project, date, valid until, prepared by, ticket), numbered blocks, price table, totals, revision label (DRAFT on previews).
- **Offer texts** (Settings): templates per block (intro, scope, exclusions, validity, payment, terms, closing) × layout / language / branch / customer, most-specific-wins; built-in texts in the five languages derived from the price list terms.

## 74. v1.35.0 — GAS-CP-R34-001 (Budget vs actual per sub-project — all three UI options)

**What changed.** Internal cost control for staff whose role shows financial data.
- **Data.** `sp_budgets` (revision R0, R1 … per sub-project: parts budget, optional offer-value override, remarks, by whom) + `sp_budget_hours` (hours and snapshotted normal rate per discipline) + `sp_costs` (date, kind order / invoice / other, supplier, description, reference, amount, internal file). Actual hours = `timesheet_lines` of the sub-project whose timesheet is approved / invoiced, grouped by the timesheet's discipline, costed with `disciplines.rate_normal / rate_overtime / rate_holiday` per hour type. Offer value = approved / closed-won offers with `subproject_id` (or the override). Result = offer value − (hours cost + parts committed); forecast = hours cost + remaining budgeted hours × average budget rate + max(parts budget, committed); earned hours = budget × `overall_pct`. Flags: hours_over, hours_ahead (used % > progress + 15), parts_over, loss (forecast < 0).
- **Option C — live strip** (`bg_strip_html`) under every sub-project title: result to date / forecast / budgeted, hours bar with progress tick, parts bar, offer value with cost-to-date bar, flags, last change, link to the tab. **Result pill** per sub-project in the project tree (`bg_pill`), **dashboard attention** rows for red sub-projects (`bg_attention`), **notifications** `bg:hours_over` / `bg:parts_over` to the project staff + PM (group Fleet) when a timesheet is approved (`ts_decide` hook) or a cost entry / budget saved.
- **Option A — Budget & actual tab** (`_budget_tab.php`, inserted before Reports): tiles, manhours per discipline table with bars, parts and materials (budget / committed / invoiced, entries, add / edit / delete with file), result build-up, € bars, Edit budget (hours + rate per discipline, parts, offer override, remarks; first revision pre-filled from the offer lines by rate card letter), revision history. Edit rights: administrators, the project's PM, roles named manager / director / CEO / sales.
- **Option B — Cost control board** (`?p=cost_control&project=`, button € Cost control on each project in the tree): five project tiles, sub-project rows with twin bars and status pill, discipline breakdown of the selected row, cumulative cost curve (`bg_curve`: budget phased linearly from project creation to the last deadline; actuals by approval / cost date), **⤓ Cost report PDF** (`?p=cost_report&project=`, document `<prefix>-CC-<project>-<date>`, internal, tables: sub-projects, disciplines per sub-project, cost entries, curve).
- 112 lang keys (3506), CSS `.bg-*`, manuals (Golden A/S: new section "Budget vs actual"; PM handbook: weekly cycle + checklist), version check `r34`, self-check line.

**Files.** New `app/bg.php`, `views/_budget_tab.php`, `views/cost_control.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r34_budget.sql`; changed `views/subproject.php` (tab + strip), `views/projects.php` (pill + button), `views/dashboard.php`, `app/ts.php`, `app/nf.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (bg_save, bg_cost_save, bg_cost_delete), `index.php`, `assets/portal.css`, lang, manuals, `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`.

**Deploy.** 1) phpMyAdmin → `goldensas_portal` → import `migrate_cp_r34_budget-goldensas_portal.sql` (3 new tables). 2) Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5. 3) Settings › Disciplines: make sure every discipline has its three rates (actual hours are costed with them; a missing rate counts as 0). 4) Open a sub-project › Budget & actual › Edit budget › Save R0; add the purchase orders as cost entries.

## 75. v1.36.0 — GAS-CP-R35-001 (Work packages · automatic planning · workload)

**What changed.**
- **Works tab** on every sub-project (staff): work packages `W01…` — title, description, discipline, working area (vessel_areas of the deck tool), planned hours, start / end, priority, engineers (checkbox list grouped by discipline with each person's load of the next 2 weeks and first free day), lead, "Write the planning automatically". Status Planned → Assigned → In progress → Done → Checked, Cancelled. Managers = administrators, the project's PM, planning editors (`pl_can_edit`). Planned hours are compared with the discipline budget of R34.
- **Automatic planning** (`wp_plan`): on save, one `plan_entries` row (kind work, `work_id`, `hours` = person's share ÷ workdays) per engineer and workday between start and end, skipping days that already carry leave / sick / training / travel (a warning names anyone with no free day); Done / Cancelled deletes the entries after today; status back to Assigned rewrites them. Planning chips show `· W01 title`. People are added to the sub-project team (`sp_team`).
- **My works** (`?p=myworks`, Work menu): the engineer's open / done packages, this week's planning, Start / Mark done, "new" badge until acknowledged; the package page (`?p=work&id=`) has Acknowledge, status buttons, people with planned / booked hours, planning days, booked timesheet lines, Book hours.
- **Timesheets**: a work-package select under the sub-project of each line (filtered by the sub-project; `timesheet_lines.work_id`, carried through `wc_classify`), and **Fill from the plan** (`timesheet_edit&from_plan=1&start=`) pre-filling the week's lines from the planning (date, sub-project, package, hours = plan hours or the day's normal hours, description = package title).
- **Weekly works forecast**: `wf_sync_works()` — when a draft forecast is opened (new or existing draft), the packages of that week with an area become Plan A items (once per area) with their days and sub-project.
- **Workload** (`?p=workload`, managers / financial roles): per discipline and engineer, capacity (working calendar normal hours) vs planned load per ISO week (4–12 weeks), hatched off-days, projects (days), open packages with remaining hours, next free day and free days in 8 weeks (`wp_workload`).
- Notifications `wp:assigned` (to the engineers), `wp:done` (to the PM and administrators), group Work. 90 lang keys (3596), CSS `.wp-* .wl-*`, manuals (Golden A/S new section "Work packages"; PM handbook: assigning, daily and weekly checklists), version check `r35`, self-check.

**Files.** New `app/wp.php`, `views/_works_tab.php`, `views/work.php`, `views/myworks.php`, `views/workload.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r35_work_packages.sql`; changed `app/pl.php` (grid joins the package, chip label), `app/wc.php` (classification keeps the package), `app/ts.php` (line work_id), `views/timesheet_edit.php`, `app/wf.php` (`wf_sync_works`), `views/subproject.php` (tab), `views/_layout.php` (menu), `app/pm_actions.php` (wp_save, wp_status, wp_ack, wp_delete), `app/nf.php`, `index.php`, `assets/portal.css`, lang, manuals, `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`.

**Deploy.** 1) phpMyAdmin → `goldensas_portal` → import `migrate_cp_r35_work_packages-goldensas_portal.sql` (2 new tables, columns on plan_entries and timesheet_lines). 2) Binary FTP upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5. 3) Portal accounts: give every engineer his discipline and working calendar (Workload groups and capacity depend on them). 4) Sub-project › Works › + New work package.

## 76. v1.36.1 — GAS-CP-R35-002 (Workload chips link to project / sub-project)

**What changed.** On Work › Workload the chips in the Projects column are links: a chip for a sub-project opens that sub-project's Works tab, a chip for a project (plan entries without sub-project) opens the project in the tree; hover shows the full project › sub-project name; up to five chips, "+n" beyond. `wp_workload()` now aggregates per project / sub-project (ids, label, title, days). **Files.** `app/wp.php`, `views/workload.php`, `assets/portal.css`, `_manual_golden.php`, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL.

**Security review of R35 (per the project instruction).** All work-package actions go through the global CSRF check and `require_staff()`; create / edit / check / cancel / delete require `wp_can_manage()` (administrator, project PM, planning editor); engineers may only Start / Done / Acknowledge packages assigned to them (`wp_set_status` enforces both); the timesheet line's package is validated against the chosen sub-project server-side; Workload and the package pages are staff-only and Workload additionally manager / financial roles; all output escaped with `e()`; no new file uploads or external calls. Nothing to harden in this revision.

## 79. v1.37.0 — GAS-CP-R36-001 (Sign-out icon · Welcome e-mail · lock-out notifications)

**What changed.** (1) A **sign-out icon** (⇥) sits at the far right of the top bar, same round style as the gear; the button at the bottom of the menu stays. (2) **Welcome e-mail** per portal account: Settings › Portal accounts shows a ✉ *Welcome e-mail* button on every row; it opens the mail program with To = the account's address and a ready text in the account's language — portal address, first sign-in, change of password (My Account › New password), enabling 2FA (My Account › Two-factor authentication), and the 15-minute lock-out rule. The text is a new E-mail template kind **Welcome — first connection** (`welcome`) with the usual per-language / branch / customer overrides and three new placeholders `{name}`, `{email}`, `{url}` (the portal address from `app.base_url`, or derived from the request when that is a local address). The temporary password is never in the e-mail — the hint under the table says so. (3) **Lock-out notifications**: when five wrong passwords lock an account, every active administrator gets a bell notification (group Library, source *Portal accounts*) naming the account and the IP; the row shows a red **Locked** pill (hover: until when) and a 🔓 **Unlock** button (new action `cu_unlock`: clears `failed_logins` / `locked_until` and closes the notification for all administrators). A repeated lock refreshes the same open entry instead of piling up. **Files.** `views/_layout.php` (icon + `signout_svg()`), `assets/portal.css`, `app/et.php` (`ET_KINDS` + `welcome`, placeholders, `et_portal_url()`, `et_welcome_mailto()`), `app/auth.php` (`sec_notify_locked()`), `app/nf.php` (`sec` source, `nf_mark_ref_read()`), `app/pm_actions.php` (`cu_unlock`), `views/admin_users.php`, lang (13 keys × 5, now 3614), `_manual_customer.php`, `_manual_golden.php`, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/` (and `assets/portal.css`), Ctrl+F5, **no SQL** (the e-mail-templates table from R32 is reused). Set `app.base_url` in `app/config.php` to `https://goldensas.com/customer` so the welcome e-mail carries the right address.

**Security review (R36).** The welcome e-mail is a `mailto:` built server-side with `rawurlencode` on every part (no user text reaches the URL unencoded) and contains no credential; the template text is the administrators' own. `cu_unlock` requires an administrator, an integer id and the CSRF token, and is logged by the usual flash. `sec_notify_locked()` runs inside `try/catch` so a missing notifications table can never block or reveal anything on the sign-in page, and the sign-in response to the attacker is unchanged (same generic message). The notification shows the first proxied hop IP (Sucuri header first) — useful for spotting password guessing; it does not expose the attempted passwords. Proposal for a later revision: a progressive lock (15 → 60 min after repeated locks of the same account) and an optional e-mail to administrators for locks outside working hours.

Security: no change.

## 81. v1.38.0 — GAS-CP-R36-003 (Drawing requests: manual entry · pending items in weekly and monthly reports)

**What changed.** (1) **Manual entry at project level**: the Drawing tracker page (project › Drawing tracker) now opens with an *Add a drawing request* form — item, type (equipment / principal), sub-project (optional, whole project otherwise), vessel system, responsible (Shipyard / Owner / Golden A/S / Supplier or free text), location + status, requested / expected / received dates, status, file, remarks. Requested defaults to today. The Excel import is folded under it. Every line has ✎ (edit the same form prefilled; Delete inside it, with confirm) and, when pending, **✓ Received** (marks it received today; the file or another date via ✎). Pending lines past their expected date show *n d overdue* in red. A new request raises the existing `drw:new` notification for the owner side (sweep). (2) **Reports**: the weekly vessel / project report (PDF, Word, screen) gets, in section 6 *Approvals*, *Drawing / information requests pending* — every pending request of the project, oldest expected first, with Days left (red = overdue, amber = no date or due within 7 days) — and *Drawings received this week* when any. The monthly project report lists all pending requests under Drawing tracker; the monthly sub-project report (GAS-PR) orders pending first, shows *exp. date* for pending lines (red when overdue) and includes the project-wide pending requests that have no sub-project. **Files.** `views/drawings.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`dr_update`, `dr_delete`), `app/wk.php` (`drw_pending`, `drw_week`, section 6 tables), `views/monthly.php`, `app/rp.php`, `views/report_sp.php`, lang (14 keys × 5, now 3628), manuals (customer, Golden, PM), `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, **no SQL**.

**Security review (R36-003).** `dr_update` / `dr_delete` require staff and the CSRF token; the id is an integer and the sub-project is checked to belong to the same project before being stored; a file goes through `cp_store_file` (type and size rules as everywhere); the quick ✓ Received posts the existing values back as hidden fields, so nothing is overwritten with blanks. Owners never see the form (staff only) and the report queries stay inside the project scope already applied to the report. No new input reaches a URL.

**What changed.** The sub-project's Drawings tab listed only the requests linked to that sub-project, so a tracker imported from Excel at project level (lines without a sub-project) left it empty. It now shows a status summary (total, pending, overdue, received, not required), *Requests of this sub-project* first and then *Requests of the whole project* (the lines without a sub-project), both with overdue marks, remarks, ✎ (edit on the project tracker, where the line can be linked to a sub-project) and ✓ Received. The tab badge counts the pending requests (own + project-wide). The add form on the tab is the same as on the project tracker (requested defaults to today, Responsible has a pick-list). **Files.** `views/subproject.php`, `assets/portal.css` (red KPI tile), lang (4 keys × 5, now 3632), `_manual_golden.php`, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL. Security: same actions as R36-003, no new input paths.

## 83. v1.39.0 — GAS-CP-R37-001 (Rich text in notes, descriptions and comments)

**What changed.** Every multi-line text box in the portal (except e-mail templates and the E-mail card) gets a toolbar: **B**, *I*, U, bulleted list, numbered list, with Ctrl+B / I / U; Enter continues a list, an empty item ends it; the list buttons toggle the selected lines. Nothing changes in the database: the text is stored with portable marks (`**bold**`, `_italic_`, `__underline__`, lines starting `- ` or `1. `). New `app/rt.php`: `rt_html()` renders those marks as safe HTML (the text is escaped first — a user can never inject HTML), inline when there is no list and as a `.rt` block with `<ul>/<ol>` otherwise; `rt_plain()` strips the marks and normalises bullets to `•` for PDF / Word / plain contexts. `PdfWriter::text()` / `wrap()` and `DocxWriter::run()` apply `rt_plain()`, so every existing report is covered without touching the generators. `mt_html()` (comments with @mentions) now renders through `rt_html()`. Display points switched from `nl2br(e())` / `white-space:pre-wrap` to `rt_html()`: ticket description and resolution, comments (tickets, approvals, offers, sub-projects, contacts, works forecast), sub-project description and weekly notes (screen, monthly report), work package description, service and inspection report texts, equipment notes and history, remote-support scope / notes, timesheet notes, invoicing notes, KB questions and answers. **Files.** `app/rt.php` (new), `app/mt.php`, `app/pdf.php`, `app/docx.php`, `index.php` (require), `views/_layout.php` (toolbar labels), `assets/portal.js`, `assets/portal.css`, 21 views, `views/admin_email_templates.php` (`data-plain`), lang (6 keys × 5, now 3638), manuals, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/` + `assets/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL. To keep a box plain, add `data-plain` to its textarea.

**Security review (R37-001).** No HTML is accepted from users: `rt_html()` escapes the whole text first and only then turns its own marks into `<b>/<i>/<u>/<ul>/<ol>/<li>/<p>/<br>` — no attributes, no links, no images — so XSS is impossible through formatting. The @mention hook runs on the escaped text as before. PDF / Word receive plain text only. The toolbar is client-side convenience; the server does not depend on it.

## 84. v1.39.1 — GAS-CP-R37-002 (Tickets: bulk close / re-open / delete)

**What changed.** Work › Tickets (staff) gets the same bulk bar as the contact list: a checkbox per row, *Select page*, *All n matching the filters* (every ticket of the current filter, across pages), the action box — ✓ Close the selected tickets, ↺ Re-open the selected tickets, 🗑 Delete the selected tickets (administrators only) — and Apply, behind the portal confirm dialog. Close / re-open go through `tk_set_status()`, so each ticket gets its status event and the owner side its notification as with a single change; already-closed (or open) tickets are skipped and the count in the flash message says how many changed. Delete uses the new `tk_delete_one()` (the former single-ticket delete factored out): files removed from disk, work orders and service reports unlinked, comments / events / file rows cascade. The filters and page are kept after the action. **Files.** `views/tickets.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`tk_bulk`, `tk_delete_one()`), lang (9 keys × 5, now 3647), `_manual_golden.php`, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL.

**Security review (R37-002).** `tk_bulk` requires staff and CSRF; delete additionally requires an administrator (checked server-side, the option is also hidden otherwise). Ids are cast to integers and each ticket is re-read before acting; "all" uses the id list the page rendered (ints only, de-duplicated), so a tampered list can at most name tickets the staff account already sees. Owners never see the bar. Deleting is irreversible — the confirm text says so.

**What changed.** Settings › Portal accounts: every Golden A/S account gets a checkbox **🔧 May prepare work packages** (in the Discipline / Manager cell). The previous rule stays underneath: administrators, the project manager of the project and planning editors (PM role / PM of an active project) can anyway, so nothing is taken away. `wp_can_manage()` now also checks the flag (`wp_user_can_works()`), cached per request. **Files.** `install/migrate_cp_r36_works_permission.sql` (**SQL: `customer_users.can_works`**), `app/wp.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`cu_update`), `views/admin_users.php`, `app/version.php` (check `r36`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, lang (2 keys × 5, now 3649), `_manual_golden.php`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r36_works_permission-goldensas_portal.sql` in phpMyAdmin (select `goldensas_portal` first), upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5. Until the SQL runs the checkbox is simply not shown and the old rule applies. Security: the flag is set by administrators only (`cu_update` requires `require_cp_admin()`), is forced to 0 for customer accounts, and is checked server-side on every work-package action.

## 87. v1.41.0 — GAS-CP-R38-001 (Accounting register and accounting reports — release A)

**What changed.** The first of the three approved releases. **Register** (`acc_lines`): every approved / invoiced timesheet line and every submitted / accepted service-report hour line is registered once (`acc_sync()`, run when the accounting pages open and by ↻ Refresh) with vessel, project, sub-project, date, engineer, discipline, hour type and hours; service-report WT hours are split normal / overtime with the engineer's working calendar, TT = travel, non-working days = holiday / weekend; timesheets already marked Invoiced by hand enter as *invoiced* so they are never billed again. States: open → draft → reported → invoiced, or excluded. **Reports** (`acc_reports`, GAS-AR-YYYY-NNN R0/R1 per branch prefix): scope vessel / project / sub-project + period + zone → every open line in scope joins and is priced: discipline → rate card (`disciplines.rate_card`, Settings › Disciplines, auto by name: automation D, electrical L, engineering E, navigation I, else A) → rate item by hour type (classified from the item description: travel / holiday / overtime / normal) → price of the zone (fallback to the other zone's price); cost = discipline cost rate. Review page: include / exclude with reason, discount per line, header discount, rate line and zone overrides per line, budget vs invoiced vs this report per sub-project › discipline (red when over), ↩ release a line, delete a draft. Issue: number, revision, PDF (cover table, summary per sub-project / discipline with normal / overtime / holiday / travel hours, budget table, detail per project › sub-project › discipline, excluded lines, signature lines) + Excel (summary + detail sheets) archived under the project (or the vessel's GEN project), lines → reported, source documents → `invoicing = sent`. Send: E-mail card (kind *accounting*, template editable in Settings › E-mail templates) with the branch's `accounting_email` (Settings › Branches; default accounting@goldenas.com.gr) and both files; the .eml download marks the report *sent*. **Accounting answer** (accounts ticked *Accounting* or administrators): invoiced (ref + date; all reported lines), partly (ticked lines), returned (note; lines back to draft, next issue = R1); cascades to `timesheets.invoicing` / status *invoiced* with the ref and to `fsr_reports.invoicing`; bell notification to the PM / preparer (`acc:*`, Commercial group). Timesheet and service-report pages show the accounting line with a link to the report. Permissions: `customer_users.can_accounting` (prepare, whole register) and `is_accounting` (answer) on Portal accounts; the PM of a project prepares for his projects. Menu: Commercial › Accounting. **Files.** `install/migrate_cp_r38_accounting.sql` (**SQL**), `app/acc.php` (new), `app/xlsxw.php` (`xlsx_bytes()`), `app/em.php` (hidden fields, sent hook), `app/et.php` (kind `accounting`), `app/br.php`, `app/nf.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`acc_*`, flags, rate card), `index.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/acc_reports.php`, `views/acc_report.php`, `views/acc_register.php` (new), `views/admin_users.php`, `views/admin_disciplines.php`, `views/admin_branches.php`, `views/timesheet_view.php`, `views/fsr_view.php`, lang (130 keys × 5, now 3783), manuals (Golden, PM), `app/version.php` (check `r38`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r38_accounting-goldensas_portal.sql` in phpMyAdmin (select `goldensas_portal`), upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5. Then: Settings › Disciplines › set the rate card per discipline where the auto choice is wrong; Settings › Portal accounts › tick *May prepare accounting reports* / *Accounting* for the people concerned; Settings › Branches › check the accounting e-mail. Releases B (service calls without project, external reports, conversion) and C (offer numbering at creation, ERP pill) follow.

**Security review (R38-001).** Every action requires staff + CSRF; preparing is checked against `acc_can_prepare(project)` (admin / flag / PM of that project) on create, save, release, issue and delete; answering against `acc_is_accounting()`; the register and lists are restricted to the PM's own projects unless the account has the flag or is accounting. Ids are integers and re-read; a sub-project is resolved to its project server-side; discounts are clamped 0–100; rate overrides must be existing rate items; the e-mail address comes from the branch record, never from the request. Amounts are masked for roles without financial visibility; owners never reach these pages (staff-only views). The PDF / Excel are stored as *internal* files and served only by id through the existing access check. The double-billing guard is structural: a line's state and `report_id` are set in one UPDATE and only *open* lines are collected.

## 88. v1.41.1 — GAS-CP-R38-002 (Accounting: waiting list per vessel and the weekly round)

**What changed.** The Accounting page opens with **Waiting to be reported**: one row per vessel (or per project without vessel) that has open registered hours — hours, number of lines, TS / SR split, oldest and newest date, age (red after 30 days); rows the person may not prepare (not his project, no flag) are shown without button. **+ Collect** creates the draft for that vessel (whole vessel, up to today, local zone); **▶ Weekly round — one draft per vessel** creates a draft for every vessel in the list at once (confirm dialog says how many), each noted "Weekly round of <date>", then lands on the draft list (or the single draft). `acc_create()` is now the shared core (`acc_create_from_post()` and `acc_create_all()` call it; empty scopes are skipped in the round). **Files.** `app/acc.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`acc_new_all`), `views/acc_reports.php`, lang (14 keys × 5, now 3797), `_manual_golden.php`, `app/version.php`. **Deploy.** Upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5, no SQL (needs R38-001's SQL). Security: same checks as R38-001 per created draft (`acc_can_prepare(project)`), the round only touches vessels the person may prepare.

## 89. v1.42.0 — GAS-CP-R38-003 (Service calls without project · external service reports — release B)

**What changed.** (1) **Service calls**: the vessel page has **+ Service report** for staff; without a project the SF-GLD-014 form files the report under the vessel's general project `GEN-<vessel id>` (created on the fly, same mechanism as vessel-only offers), so timesheets, the accounting register and the weekly report work unchanged; the owner sees it under Services. (2) **External reports**: **📎 External service report** (vessel page, project report list; `?p=fsr_ext`) attaches a file (PDF / image / Word / Excel) with date, location, engineers, PIC, a summary and an owner-visibility switch; it is stored as `fsr_reports.kind = 'external'`, status *submitted*, number `EXT-YYYYMMDD-INI-n` (initials of the engineers typed), file kind `fsr_ext`; a PDF is served as the report's PDF, other files via ⤓. Lists and the report page show an *External* pill and *Not processed* until converted; hidden external reports never reach the owner (`fsr_load` / `fsr_list` guard). (3) **Conversion**: **⇄ Create internal report** opens the normal form with `from_ext=<id>` — vessel, project, sub-project, date, location, PIC pre-filled, the external file linked as evidence — and on first save links both ways (`source_report_id` / `converted_id`, events on both); the internal PDF / Word carry "(from EXT-…)". Only internal reports feed `acc_sync()` (`r.kind = 'internal'`). (4) The Accounting page lists **External reports not processed** with a ⇄ button. **Files.** `install/migrate_cp_r38b_service_calls.sql` (**SQL**: 6 columns on `fsr_reports`), `app/fsr.php` (`fsr_ext_has/number/save_from_post/pending`, `fsr_project_for_vessel`, load / list guards, PDF / Word suffix), `app/acc.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (`fsr_ext_save`), `index.php`, `views/fsr_ext.php` (new), `views/fsr_edit.php`, `views/fsr_view.php`, `views/_fsr_list.php`, `views/fsr_list.php`, `views/vessel_view.php`, `views/acc_reports.php`, lang (28 keys × 5, now 3825), manuals (Golden, customer), `app/version.php` (check `r38b`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r38b_service_calls-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5.

**Security review (R38-003).** Attaching requires staff + CSRF; the file goes through `cp_store_file` (type / size whitelist), stored with visibility *internal* when the owner must not see it and *external* otherwise, and served only by id through the existing access check. The owner-visibility flag is enforced server-side on load and in every list (not only hidden in the UI). Conversion accepts only an external report that is not yet converted and only on the first save of a new internal report; the link cannot be rewritten afterwards. The general project is created with the vessel's own customer, so scope rules (`scope_sql`) still apply to owners.

## 91. v1.43.0 — GAS-CP-R39-001 / R39-002 (Project-management KPIs, risk log, sales KPIs — options A + B + C and sales 1 + 2 + 3)

**PM KPIs** (`app/kpi.php`, Work › KPIs, `views/kpis.php`): PCR, PCRt, BAI-b, BAI-o, RUIs, RUIa, PSC, RMI, ROI, CPI, SPI per sub-project (`kpi_sub()`) and per project (`kpi_project()`, hour-weighted roll-up; PCR and PSC are period figures) for this / last month, quarter, YTD, 12 months, filtered by PM, vessel, status. Formulas and data sources are listed on the page (Definitions) and in the Golden manual. Thresholds: `kpi_targets` (target + tolerance per KPI, PSC lower-is-better; defaults target 1 / tolerance 0.1, PSC 0 / 1, ROI 0 % / 10 %) editable under Settings › KPI targets with *notify the PM on red*. **Snapshots** (`kpi_snapshots`): the first visit of a month (KPI page or dashboard) stores every active project's and sub-project's KPIs for the previous month; a KPI red now and not red in the previous snapshot sends `kpi:red` to the PM; the snapshots draw the sparklines and the Trend block. **Cockpit**: dashboard card with the five worst projects (`kpi_cockpit()`). **Reports**: `report_kpi` PDF (GAS-KPI-YYYY-MM[-project]) and Excel. **Data added**: `subprojects.completed_at` (set by `pm_track_completed()` on every sub-project save; back-filled), `customer_users.rate_card` (Portal accounts; RUIs cards A C D E I L, RUIa cards B M; empty = the discipline's card). **Risk log** (`sp_risks`, `_risks.php` partial, project page `risks`, sub-project tab Risks): RK-001…, category, likelihood × impact, owner, due, mitigation, status open / mitigated / accepted / occurred / closed, source manual or inspection; ⇣ *From inspection reports* seeds every issued inspection-report item with a risk level once (`rk_seed_from_inspections()`, unique on the item). RMI = mitigated + closed ÷ all.
**Sales KPIs** (`app/sales.php`, Commercial › Sales KPIs, `views/sales.php`, financial roles): hit rate (count and value), pipeline by aging, weighted forecast (`offer_status_prob`, Settings › Sales targets), decision time and validity breaches, quote turnaround (`offers.requested_at`, new field on the offer form, defaults to the ticket date), average order value, offered → invoiced (accounting register), discounts given, repeat customers and top-5 share, service calls → offers within 30 days; per sales person (= `offers.created_by`) and per customer; this month vs targets (`sales_targets` per person and company, Settings › Sales targets, green ≥ 100 %, amber ≥ 70 %); loss reasons (`offers.lost_reason`, asked when staff reject an offer; owners' rejections = other); `report_sales` PDF (GAS-SR-YYYY-MM) and Excel. **Files.** `install/migrate_cp_r39_kpis.sql` (**SQL**), `app/kpi.php`, `app/sales.php` (new), `app/of.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `index.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/kpis.php`, `views/report_kpi.php`, `views/admin_kpis.php`, `views/_risks.php`, `views/risks.php`, `views/sales.php`, `views/report_sales.php`, `views/admin_sales.php` (new), `views/subproject.php`, `views/projects.php`, `views/dashboard.php`, `views/offer.php`, `views/offer_edit.php`, `views/admin_users.php`, `assets/portal.css`, lang (203 keys × 5), manuals (Golden, PM), `app/version.php` (check `r39`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r39_kpis-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5; then Settings › KPI targets (defaults are the sheet's thresholds with the 0.9 – 1 band), Settings › Sales targets (probabilities + monthly targets), Portal accounts › rate card for assistant engineers (B).

**Security review (R39).** KPI and sales pages are staff-only and gated (`kpi_can()`: admin / financial / PM / planning editors; `sl_can()`: financial roles); BAI and ROI columns are hidden from roles without financial visibility. Risk log actions require staff + CSRF; the sub-project is resolved to its project server-side; the seed is idempotent (unique on the inspection item). Targets and probabilities are admin-only and clamped. The monthly snapshot runs inside page requests (no cron) and only inserts (INSERT IGNORE), so repeated visits cannot alter stored history. No new input reaches a URL or a file name unescaped.

## 92. v1.44.0 — Release C: portal offer numbers for every branch, ERP pill, mean manhour cost per branch (GAS-CP-R40-001 / R40-002)

**R40-001 — offer numbers.** Every offer created in the portal is numbered at creation from the series of the issuing branch: `br_offer_series($b)` = the branch's `offer_series` or, when empty, `<doc_prefix>-OF` — so the head office issues `GAS-OF-2026-001…` and the Italian branch `GIT-OF-2026-001…`; `br_offer_no()` no longer returns '' for a branch without a series. The offer form pre-fills the number for the selected project (`BR` map + `BR0` fallback in `offer_edit.php`), typing over it records an external number. Offers loaded by Settings › Import from Excel › Offers (SoftOne) are inserted with `offers.origin = 'erp'` and keep the ERP number; `of_origin_pill()` shows a grey **ERP** pill in the offer list and the offer heading. The migration flags existing imported offers (any offer with an `imported` event). Settings › Branches shows the series column as `GAS-OF-YYYY-NNN` / `GIT-OF-YYYY-NNN`.

**R40-002 — mean manhour cost.** `branches.manhour_cost DECIMAL(8,2) DEFAULT 35.00`, edited in Settings › Branches (1 – 9999, default 35). `br_manhour_cost($pid, $sid)` / `br_hour_cost($disciplineRow, $hourType, $pid, $sid)` return the discipline's cost rate for the hour type (overtime / holiday falling back to normal) and, when the discipline has none, the branch mean. Applied in: budget save default rate and budget form (`bg_save_from_post`, `_budget_tab.php`), actual cost of approved timesheet hours (`bg_actual_hours`), `bg_calc` budget rates, the monthly cost curve of the cost board (SQL `COALESCE(NULLIF(rate,0), NULLIF(rate_normal,0), <branch mean>)`), the accounting-report cost column (`acc_price_line`), and the Manhours report cost table (`wc_cost`, every discipline now costed). KPIs (BAI, ROI, CPI) inherit the change through `bg_calc`.

**Files.** `app/br.php`, `app/of.php`, `app/bg.php`, `app/acc.php`, `app/wc.php`, `views/admin_branches.php`, `views/offers.php`, `views/offer.php`, `views/offer_edit.php`, `views/_budget_tab.php`, lang (6 keys × 5), manuals (Golden, PM), `app/version.php` (check `r40`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r40_offer_numbers.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r40_offer_numbers-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5; check Settings › Branches (mean manhour cost per branch, series column).

**Security review (R40).** The offer number is generated server-side from the branch of the project (MAX+1 inside the series prefix, bound parameters); a user-typed number is trimmed to 60 characters as before. `origin` is set only by the import code path — the offer form cannot set it. `manhour_cost` is admin-only (`br_save` → `require_cp_admin` + CSRF), cast to float and clamped (1 – 9999, else 35). The branch-cost fallback is passed to SQL as a bound parameter, never interpolated. Cost figures remain behind `fin_visible()` on every screen that shows them. No new pages, no new file paths.

## 93. v1.45.0 — Production orders and production cards (GAS-CP-R41-001)

**What it is.** `app/pd.php` + views `production.php` (list, Fleet › Equipment › Production tab), `production_order.php` (the order), `production_edit.php` (form). A production order `<prefix>-PO-YYYY-NNN` (branch of the project/sub-project) is created from a work package (Works tab 🏭 link, work-package page button) or from the list; it carries project, sub-project, work package, vessel, system, production manager, planned dates, description. **Units** = rows of `production_units` pointing at `equipment` records: new serial numbers typed on the form are created in the Equipment register (maker Golden A/S by default, sold by us, vessel of the order, note "Built under PO …"); existing units of the vessel can be ticked. Each unit has `status` (pending → in_production → tested → delivered → installed → commissioned → accepted; cancelled) and `pct` — the **production card**, edited inline per line by the production manager; the order's `pct` is the mean of its live units (`pd_recalc`). **Phases** of the order (`status`): design → production → fat → delivered → hat → commissioning → sat → closed; cancelled. `pd_set_status()` stamps `delivered_at` / `closed_at`, pulls unit statuses forward (delivered → delivered, hat → installed, commissioning/sat → commissioned, closed → accepted + 100 %), and on *delivered* puts the units on the vessel (`equipment.vessel_id`, `date_sold`). **Documents** (`production_files` → `sp_files`) per phase — draft / approved / asbuilt (kind drawing, revision D1 / R0 / AB suggested), fat / hat / sat, photo, delivery, commissioning, other — uploaded with `cp_store_file` (so archived and on the Files tab) or linked from the project's existing drawings; per order or per unit; visibility defaults per phase (approved, as-built and test records external). **Log** (`production_log`) of every event; **notifications** `pd:*` (group Work) to PM, manager and creator. **Production card PDF** `<po>-PC` (`?p=production_pdf&id=`). The Equipment list shows a Register / Production tab bar with the open-orders counter; a serial number's page shows its production card (milestone strip, status, %, documents, photos). **Permission**: `customer_users.is_production` (Settings › Portal accounts › Production manager) edits everything; otherwise the order's manager, the PM of the project, `wp_can_manage()` users and administrators (`pd_can_edit`). Owners never reach the pages (staff-only, 403).

**Files.** `app/pd.php` (new), `views/production.php`, `views/production_order.php`, `views/production_edit.php` (new), `views/equipment.php`, `views/equipment_view.php`, `views/_works_tab.php`, `views/work.php`, `views/admin_users.php`, `app/pm_actions.php` (pd_* actions, is_production save), `app/nf.php`, `index.php`, `assets/portal.css`, lang (128 keys × 5), manuals (Golden: new section Production orders; PM), `app/version.php` (check `r41`), `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r41_production.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r41_production-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5; tick Production manager on the shop-floor accounts (Settings › Portal accounts).

**Security review (R41).** All pages and actions are staff-only (`require_staff`, owners get 403 on list, order, form and PDF); edits go through `pd_can_edit()` on every action (status, units, files, links) and `pd_can_create()` on creation; deletion is administrator-only. Every POST is CSRF-checked; ids are cast and re-validated against the order (unit → order, file → project, work → sub-project → project, manager → active staff). Uploads use the existing `cp_store_file` (MIME / extension allow-list, random stored name, archive filing); the per-phase visibility only chooses between internal and external — nothing new is exposed to owners that the Files tab would not already allow. Serial numbers are trimmed to 60 chars and de-duplicated per maker; no raw input reaches SQL unbound or HTML unescaped. Notifications carry the order number and title only.

## 94. v1.46.0 — Production stage fed from the serial numbers; subcontracted / external production (GAS-CP-R41-002 / R41-003)

**R41-002.** `pd_sub_pct($sid)` = mean `pct` of the live units of the live orders of a sub-project (null when none). `pd_recalc()` now calls `pd_feed_subproject()` which writes `subprojects.pct_production`; the Process-tab save (`sp_stages`) overwrites the posted production figure with the fed value while an order with units exists, and the form shows the field read-only (gold background) with the feeding orders listed. No migration.

**R41-003.** Table `production_subs` (migration `migrate_cp_r41b_subcontracting.sql`): per order, optional unit, `whole` flag, scope, supplier (+ optional `company_id` from Contacts › Companies), PO, ordered / due / delivered, amount + currency, status (`PS_STATUSES`: planned, ordered, in_production, delivered, inspected, accepted, rejected, cancelled), `pct` (from `PS_PCT` unless typed), inspection note, note. `ps_save_from_post()` (action `ps_save`), `ps_delete`, `ps_of_order`, `ps_list` (filters), `ps_counts`, `ps_suppliers`; `ps_feed_unit()` drives the unit of a whole-equipment line (status in_production / tested when accepted, pct = max). New document phase `subcontract` (kind other, internal). Order page: 🔗 Subcontracting card (table + form, edit via `&sub=`), External pill on the unit; Production list: Subcontracted column + **Subcontracting** tab (`?p=production&view=sub`, partial `_production_sub.php`) with KPI tiles, filters, late lines, total value (financial roles) and the PDF `?p=production_sub_pdf` (`GAS-SC-YYYYMMDD`, grouped by supplier). Production-card PDF prints the subcontracting section. Notifications `pd:sub` on delivered / rejected / accepted. Version check `r41b`; selfcheck line.

**Files.** `app/pd.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `index.php`, `views/production.php`, `views/_production_sub.php` (new), `views/production_order.php`, `views/subproject.php`, manuals (Golden, PM), lang (62 keys × 5), `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r41b_subcontracting.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r41b_subcontracting-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/`, Ctrl+F5.

**Security review (R41-002/003).** Subcontract lines are edited only through `pd_can_edit()` of their order; the line is re-validated against the order on update/delete; unit and company ids are checked against the order and the companies table; amounts are parsed with the offer parser and stored/shown only for roles with financial visibility (masked otherwise, kept on update when the editor cannot see them); dates are pattern-checked; supplier/scope/notes are trimmed and length-limited; the Production stage feed writes a clamped integer with a bound parameter and only when an order exists, so a user cannot push an arbitrary stage value through the production path. The subcontracting PDF is staff-only and filtered through `scope_sql`.

## 95. v1.46.1 — Production order form: vessel follows the project (GAS-CP-R41-004)

`views/production_edit.php`: `fv()` hides every vessel except the project's (`data-v` on the project option) and selects it, on load and on each project change; a project without a vessel leaves the full list. `app/pd.php` `pd_save_from_post()`: the vessel is the project's whenever the project has one (the posted value only counts for a project without a vessel). No migration. **Security:** the server-side rule removes the possibility of filing an order under a vessel foreign to its project.

## 96. v1.47.0 — Sub-project stages fed from the production orders, option C (GAS-CP-R42-001)

**Decisions.** Option C (evidence-driven with override); several orders weighted by number of units; stages without evidence keep the PM's value; the PM can unlock; 🏭 marks in the process line / tree and * in the weekly report.

**Code.** `app/pd.php`: `pd_order_stages($o)` (auto per rule, override from `production_orders.st_<stage>`, effective), `pd_unit_share`, `pd_docs_cover`, `pd_sub_stages($sid)` (weighted by live units; an order without units weighs 1), `pd_sub_manual($sid)` (`subprojects.stage_manual`, comma list of unlocked slugs), `pd_sub_fed($sid)` (fed = evidence and not unlocked; one query to know which sub-projects have orders), `pd_feed_subproject($sid)` (writes every fed stage), `pd_feed_order($oid)`, `pd_stages_save_from_post` (action `pd_stages`, logged `stage_override`), `pd_stage_lock` (action `sp_stage_lock`, logged `stage_unlock` / `stage_lock` on the first order; locking back writes the fed value at once). Feed hooks: `pd_recalc` (units), status change, files add / link / remove, subcontract save / delete. `pm_actions.php` `sp_stages`: every fed stage is overwritten with the fed value on save. `views/subproject.php`: fed stages read-only with 🏭, the orders (✎ when overridden) and 🔓 Unlock; unlocked stages editable with 🔒 Lock to production (hidden forms per stage). `views/production_order.php`: *Stages fed to the sub-project* card (rule, automatic, override, effective). `app/pm.php` `process_line()`: `$sub['_fed']` → `seg--fed` + 🏭 (projects tree and sub-project head pass it). `app/wk.php`: `* ` on fed cells + note under the progress table. Migration `migrate_cp_r42_stage_feed.sql`; check `r42`; selfcheck.

**Files.** `app/pd.php`, `app/pm.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `app/wk.php`, `views/subproject.php`, `views/projects.php`, `views/production_order.php`, `assets/portal.css`, manuals (Golden, PM), lang (30 keys × 5), `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r42_stage_feed.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r42_stage_feed-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5.

**Security review (R42).** Overrides are saved only through `pd_can_edit()` of the order, clamped 0–100, blank = automatic; the unlock action requires staff, a valid sub-project and a known stage slug, and writes a whitelist-filtered list; every stage write to `subprojects` is a clamped integer under a bound parameter and only for stages with evidence, so no user-controlled string reaches the stage columns. Fed values override posted ones server-side, so the read-only fields cannot be bypassed by editing the form. The 🏭 / * marks are computed server-side for staff only; owners' views and reports are unchanged.

## 97. v1.48.0 — Release D1: numbering, invoicing per vessel, sub-project contacts, report e-mail button (GAS-CP-R43-001/002/003)

**Decisions (from the D proposal).** Classes AACE 18R-97 (D2), budgetary offers visible to owners (D2), new Sales permission (D2), VAT default from invoicing details, cash flow month + week (D3), subcontracting as out-flow when a PO exists (D3), numbers `PRJ.000495` / `Y.001` / `PRJ.000495.Y.001.SQ.<id>` / `PRJ.000495.Y.001.PI.<id>.n`, mail-client drafts.

**R43-001 numbering** — `app/num.php`: `num_cfg()` (cp_settings `num_*`: prefixes, digits, next project number, auto on/off), `num_next_project()` (max existing project/ERP code with the prefix + 1, ≥ next), `num_next_sub($pid)` (per project, width follows existing codes), `num_unique('SQ'|'PI')` (doc_sequences scope `portal`), `num_stem`, `num_sq`, `num_pi`. Project / sub-project forms pre-fill the number (field no longer required; empty → generated server-side in `project_save` / `subproject_save`); `of_save` writes `offers.sq_no`; offers list/page show it; search includes it. Settings › Numbering (`admin_numbering.php`, action `num_save`) with a preview of the next numbers.
**R43-002 invoicing per vessel** — `IV_TYPES` + `vessel`, `iv_owner_exists`, `iv_back` → vessel page; `invoicing_details.vat_pct` (form field); vessel page › Invoicing tab (`iv_section('vessel', …)`, financial roles); `subprojects.invoicing_id` + select on the sub-project form (`iv_choices_for_sub`: vessel's then customer's; `iv_sub_save` on create/update); `iv_for_sub()` resolution (chosen → vessel default → customer default) shown on the sub-project head.
**R43-003 sub-project contacts** — table `sp_contacts`; Contacts tab: selected list with sub-project role + "Select from the vessel's contacts" form (`spc_save`), e-mail-all link; `spc_emails($sid)`; `em_people()` got a 4th parameter `$preferred` — when given, only those (and the explicit extras such as the FSR person in charge) are ticked; fsr/isr views pass the sub-project contacts; **✉ E-mail** button in the report actions (`data-open-email` → opens and scrolls to the existing e-mail card, `portal.js`).

**Files.** `app/num.php` (new), `app/iv.php`, `app/em.php`, `app/of.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `index.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/admin_numbering.php` (new), `views/project_new.php`, `views/subproject.php`, `views/vessel_view.php`, `views/_invoicing.php`, `views/offers.php`, `views/offer.php`, `views/fsr_view.php`, `views/isr_view.php`, `assets/portal.js`, manuals (Golden, PM), lang (35 keys × 5), `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r43_numbering.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r43_numbering-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.js`, Ctrl+F5; then Settings › Numbering › set "next project number" above the ERP counter (e.g. 500 if the ERP is at PRJ.000495).

**Security review (R43).** Numbers are generated server-side from bound queries; prefixes are length-limited strings used only in LIKE-free PHP matching; the doc_sequences increment is atomic (LAST_INSERT_ID pattern, as the archive). Invoicing details for vessels reuse the admin-only `iv_save_from_post` (CSRF, owner validated against `vessels`); the sub-project's `invoicing_id` is accepted only from the identities the sub-project may use (`iv_choices_for_sub`), and shown only to roles with financial visibility. Sub-project contacts are accepted only from the vessel's linked contacts (whitelist), roles trimmed to 80 chars; the e-mail pre-tick changes which boxes start checked, nothing about who can be mailed. The ✉ button is pure client-side (opens an existing card).

## 98. v1.49.0 — Release D2: budgetary offers & AACE classes, payment terms, proforma invoices, invoice links, financial status (GAS-CP-R44-001…004)

**Code.** `app/pi.php` (new): classes (`offer_classes` seeded with AACE 18R-97 classes 5…1; `oc_all/oc_get/oc_pill/oc_save_from_post/oc_delete`, `oc_apply_from_post` called by `of_save`, `oc_convert` = copy header + `offer_lines` + `offer_blocks` + terms to a draft firm offer on a sub-project, `converted_to/from` links, events `converted`), payment terms (`offer_terms`; `pt_of`, `pt_default` per branch in cp_settings `pt_default_<branch>`, `pt_save_from_post` keeps terms that already have a live PI, `pt_seed`, `pt_amount`), proformas (`proformas`; `pi_create` from a slot or free — number `num_pi()` → `PRJ….PI.<id>.n`, invoicing identity via `iv_for_sub()` / vessel default / customer default, VAT from `invoicing_details.vat_pct`, due from the term; `pi_save_from_post` (draft editable; ERP PDF upload), `pi_issue` (PDF `pi_render_pdf` stored external, status issued, notification), `pi_set_status`, `pi_mark_sent` hooked in `em_draft_from_post` (hidden `proforma`), `pi_refresh_status` from Σ paid of `proforma_invoices` (`pinv_save_from_post`, `pinv_delete`), `pi_list`, `pi_overdue`, `pi_delete` admin), financial status (`fs_totals`, `fs_pills`). Permissions: `pi_is_sales()` (`customer_users.can_sales` or admin), `pi_can_invoices()` (sales or accounting), `pi_can_view()` (staff with financial visibility). Views: `_offer_proformas.php` (offer page card: convert form, terms table, slots, free PI, financial pills), `proforma.php`, `proformas.php`, `admin_offer_classes.php` (classes + default terms per branch); `offer_edit.php` (kind + class), `offer.php` (pills, links), `projects.php` and `subproject.php` (fs pills), `admin_users.php` (Sales), nav Commercial › Proformas, settings tab, e-mail template kind `proforma` (`et.php`), notifications `pi:*` (commercial). Migration `migrate_cp_r44_proformas.sql`; check `r44`; selfcheck.

**Files.** `app/pi.php` (new), `app/of.php` (unchanged logic; SQ from R43), `app/pm_actions.php`, `app/em.php`, `app/et.php`, `app/nf.php`, `index.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/_offer_proformas.php`, `views/proforma.php`, `views/proformas.php`, `views/admin_offer_classes.php` (new), `views/offer.php`, `views/offer_edit.php`, `views/projects.php`, `views/subproject.php`, `views/admin_users.php`, `assets/portal.css`, manuals (Golden, PM), lang (129 keys × 5), `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r44_proformas.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r44_proformas-goldensas_portal.sql` (after R43), upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5; tick **Sales** on the sales accounts; check Settings › Offer classes & payment terms (classes seeded; set the default instalments per branch).

**Security review (R44).** Every financial page and partial is gated by `pi_can_view()` (staff + financial visibility) and masked otherwise; terms, PI creation/edit/issue/status and conversion require `pi_is_sales()`; invoice links require sales or accounting; PI deletion is admin-only. Amounts are parsed with the offer parser, clamped (VAT 0–100) and stored as decimals; the invoicing identity accepted on a PI is checked against `invoicing_details`; the PI number is generated server-side (atomic sequence) and is unique; a paid or cancelled PI is immutable; the ERP PDF upload goes through `cp_store_file` (type allow-list) and the generated PDF through `cp_store_bytes`. Converting copies only columns from the source offer (no user input besides the sub-project, validated against the project). Owners can see budgetary offers (decided) but never the terms, proformas or financial pills.

## 99. v1.50.0 — Release D3: cash flow per project and company report (GAS-CP-R45-001)

**Code.** `app/cf.php`: `cf_can()` (admin or `customer_users.can_cashflow`, financial visibility), `cf_periods/cf_key/cf_label/cf_range` (month `YYYY-MM` or ISO week `YYYY-Www`; 3 back / 9 ahead), `cf_scope($f)` (project | branch / vessel / PM, completed optional, `scope_sql`), `cf_series($f,$g)` — in actual (paid `proforma_invoices` by `paid_at`), in forecast (open PIs by due date, never before now; instalments of approved offers without a PI at approval date + due days), out actual (approved/invoiced timesheet hours × `br_hour_cost` by work date; `sp_costs` invoices; subcontracting delivered with a PO), out forecast (`sp_costs` orders; subcontracting with a PO not delivered by due date; remaining budgeted hours × average budget rate spread over the periods to the deadline) — per period in/out/net/cumulative + items; `cf_chart()` inline SVG. Views: `cashflow.php` (project or company, filters, KPI tiles, chart, per-period table with the four main items, sources table, rules), `report_cf.php` (PDF `GAS-CF-YYYY-MM[-project]`, Excel with Cash flow + Drivers sheets). Nav Commercial › Cash flow; 💰 button on the project tree row; permission on Portal accounts. Migration `migrate_cp_r45_cashflow.sql`; check `r45`; selfcheck.

**Files.** `app/cf.php` (new), `views/cashflow.php`, `views/report_cf.php` (new), `views/projects.php`, `views/_layout.php`, `views/admin_users.php`, `app/pm_actions.php`, `index.php`, `assets/portal.css`, manual (Golden), lang (42 keys × 5), `app/version.php`, `install/cp_selfcheck.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r45_cashflow.sql`. **Deploy.** Run `migrate_cp_r45_cashflow-goldensas_portal.sql`, upload over `customer/` + `assets/portal.css`, Ctrl+F5; tick Cash flow on the accounts that may see it.

**Security review (R45).** Read-only pages gated by `cf_can()` (403 otherwise, including the PDF / Excel); the project list is built through `scope_sql` so branch-scoped accounts see their projects only; every filter is cast to int; project ids are injected into SQL only after `intval` (no user strings in the IN lists); status slugs in the approved-offer query are quoted through PDO. No write path. The chart is server-rendered SVG with escaped titles.

## 100. v1.51.0 — External reports movable to a project; time zones (GAS-CP-R46-001 / R46-002)

**R46-001.** `fsr_ext_relink($id)` (action `fsr_ext_relink`, staff): an external report moves to a project (scope-checked) and optional sub-project; `customer_name` / `vessel_name` refreshed; its `sp_files` rows (ext/pdf/docx + `fsr_id`) follow; event `saved` with "Moved to …" (the events column is an ENUM). Card 🔗 *Link to a project / sub-project* on the report page (open by default when the report sits on a GEN- project), with a project → sub-project JS filter.

**R46-002.** `helpers.php`: `tz_app()` (cp_settings `tz_app` → config `app.tz` → Europe/Athens), `tz_db()` (`tz_db` → `app.db_tz` → UTC), `tz_local()`, `tz_abbr()`, `fmt_datetime()` now converts and appends the zone (EET / EEST), `fmt_date()` of a date-time uses the local day, `fmt_dt_doc()` for documents; `db.php` sets the connection `time_zone` to the configured database zone's current offset so `NOW()` keeps storing in that zone; `index.php` sets PHP's default zone to the company zone after lic.php. Direct `date('H:i', strtotime())` uses in fsr/isr documents and the admin log replaced. Settings › Numbering › *Time zone* card (both zones, live clocks and the check line). No migration.

**Security review (R46).** Relink requires staff + CSRF; the target project is validated through `scope_sql` and the sub-project against the project; only the report's own files are moved (by id / fsr_id). Time-zone values are accepted only from `timezone_identifiers_list()`; the SQL `SET time_zone` uses a PHP-computed `±HH:MM` offset, never user text.
