## 104. v1.53.0 — Engineering sections per sub-project (GAS-CP-R47-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r47_engineering.sql` (run `migrate_cp_r47_engineering-goldensas_portal.sql` in phpMyAdmin): `disciplines.is_engineering` (Electrical and Automation flagged, Mechanical / UI/UX / IT inserted), `timesheet_lines.activity` (engineering / production / site / commissioning), `customer_users.can_management`, tables `eng_budgets`, `eng_deliverables`, `eng_requests`. New module `app/eng.php`: sections = disciplines flagged as engineering (Settings › Disciplines › 📐 checkbox); per sub-project a budget in manhours and a lead per section, deliverables with a weight in hours, earned value `earned = done-deliverables' weight scaled to allowed` (no deliverables → booked hours, capped), `allowed = budget + approved extra`, Done % = earned ÷ allowed, Used % = booked ÷ allowed; states On budget / Near budget (90 %) / Budget extended (extra approved, indicator stays) / Approval pending / Exceeded / Finished. `eng_sub_pct()` feeds `pct_engineering` and replaces the document rule inside `pd_sub_stages()` / `pd_sub_fed()` (📐 on the Process tab, unlock still works). Timesheets: every line carries an activity (default engineering while the user's discipline is a section and the stage is open, site afterwards); `eng_check_booking()` refuses a sheet whose engineering hours would exceed allowed (message, PM notified once via `exceeded_notified_at`); untagged historic lines of engineering disciplines count as engineering. Requests: Engineering tab › Request extra hours (hours, cause misjudgement / miscalculation / scope change / rework / other, reason) → notifications to management + PM; decided on the tab or on Work › Engineering requests (management = admins or the new **Management** flag on Portal accounts; pending count badge in the nav) with a note; approval raises allowed. Declare finished (note required if deliverables pending) → 100 %, Reopen. Pills on the project tree (`eng_alerts()`), ⚠ on the tab, "Sections to watch" list. Manuals (Golden + PM) with figure `flow-engineering.svg`; 102 new keys × 5 languages; check `r47`; selfcheck line. **Security:** every action re-loads the sub-project through `load_subproject()` (scope) and `eng_can_edit()` (PM, section lead, management, admin) before writing; decisions require `eng_is_management()`; `discipline_id` is validated against the flagged sections, `reason_kind` and `activity` are whitelisted, hours are bounded (0 < h ≤ 10 000), notes/reasons length-limited and escaped on output; deliverable ids are checked against the sub-project before toggle/delete; the requests list is filtered by `scope_sql()`; owners never see the tab or the page (403); no new query uses interpolated input. No performance impact: `eng_rows()` is four indexed queries per sub-project and is only computed on the tab, the project tree (one query for alert sub-projects, states cached per request) and the requests page.

## 105. v1.54.0 — Engineering sections unified with the budget (GAS-CP-R47-002)
Migration `migrate_cp_r47b_eng_sections.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; run after §104's): table `eng_sections` (ELE, AUT, MEC, UIX, IT seeded), `disciplines.eng_section_id` (mapping, first guess from the names: Electrical Engineer → ELE, Automation* → AUT, Mechanical* → MEC, UI/U* → UIX, IT* → IT; installers unmapped), the three generic disciplines R47-001 inserted are deleted when unreferenced, and `eng_budgets / eng_deliverables / eng_requests.discipline_id` renamed `section_id` (`is_engineering` stays, unused). `eng_has()` now requires both tables. `eng_sections()` carries `disciplines`; `eng_section_of($did)`; `eng_budget_hours($sid)` = Σ `sp_budget_hours` of the mapped disciplines in `bg_current()` (manual `eng_budgets.hours` only without the budget module); `eng_actual()` maps timesheet disciplines to sections; `eng_rows()` adds `pct_budget` (booked ÷ budget) and `over_h`; the Used bar is drawn against the budget with the approved extra as a hatched extension and a budget tick; Budget & actual rows show `📐 +N h approved` for mapped disciplines. New page `admin_engineering` (Settings › Organisation): sections list (add / rename / retire) + discipline → section mapping; `admin_disciplines` shows the mapped section; actions `eng_sections_save`, `eng_mapping_save`; `eng_done` / deliverables / requests post `section_id`. Manuals, 26 keys × 5 languages (+ 6 reworded), check `r47b`, selfcheck line. **Security:** settings actions are `require_cp_admin()`; the section code is validated `^[A-Z0-9_-]{1,12}$` (unique), mapping values are checked against existing section ids, every other guard of §104 unchanged. Performance: `eng_budget_hours()` reuses `bg_current()` (two indexed queries) and the per-request caches of `eng_sections()` / `eng_section_of()`.

## 106. v1.55.0 — Stage sections: Production, Field & Installation, HAT / SAT, Commissioning (GAS-CP-R48-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r48_stage_sections.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; after §105's): `eng_sections.stage` (engineering | production | installation | sathat | commissioning) with PRD / FLD / HST / COM seeded, table `eng_section_disciplines` (many-to-many; the R47-002 single mapping carried over, site stages pre-ticked by name: *Installer* → FLD+HST, Service* → FLD+HST+COM, Automation* / Navigation* → HST+COM, IT* → COM, Production* → PRD), `sp_budget_hours.section_id` (NULL = whole discipline). `ENG_ACTIVITIES` = the stages + site; `ENG_STAGES`; `eng_sections()` carries `stage`; `eng_sections_of($did)`, `eng_stages_of()`, `eng_section_for($did,$stage)`, `eng_split_disciplines()`, `eng_activity_choices()`; `eng_budget_hours()` reads the per-section rows (`bg_current()['sections']`) and shares an unsplit older revision equally; `eng_actual()` groups by discipline × activity; `eng_sub_pct($sid,$stage)`, `eng_stage_pcts()`; `eng_feed_subproject()` writes every budgeted stage (`stage_defs` cols) unless unlocked; `pd_sub_stages()/pd_sub_fed()` take the section value for any stage with budget. `bg_save_from_post()` stores `hs[did][sec]` rows for split disciplines (`bg_has_sections()`); Edit budget renders one cell per section with a live row total. Timesheet guard `eng_check_booking($disc, [sub => [stage => h]])`; the activity select lists the discipline's stages + site. Approval: `eng_can_decide($sp,$stage)` = management/admin, plus the project's PM for non-engineering stages; `eng_is_pm()` opens Work › Engineering requests and the badge to PMs (scoped). Settings page = discipline × section matrix + sections with a stage; Sections tab grouped by stage (anchors `#st-<stage>`); tab renamed *Sections*. Manuals, 20 keys × 5 languages (+ 6 reworded), check `r48`, selfcheck. **Security:** matrix values validated against existing section ids; stage whitelisted (`ENG_STAGES`); split hours bounded like the discipline hours; `eng_decide_list` now defers to `eng_request_decide()`, which re-checks the deciding right per request; the requests list for PMs is filtered by `scope_sql()`; nothing else changed. Performance: one extra indexed query in `eng_sections()` (cached per request); the PM badge runs the scoped pending list once.

## 107. v1.55.1 — Session hardening (GAS-CP-R49-001) — security release
**Finding (reported):** closing the Edge window and reopening the portal signed the user in automatically. Cause: a 4-hour rolling idle window (`app.idle_minutes` = 240), a plain session cookie that Edge/Chrome restore with the window, no absolute lifetime and no device binding — a stolen or restored cookie was as good as a password. **Fix** — migration `migrate_cp_r49_sessions.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy): `customer_users.session_epoch`, `login_log.outcome` + expired / device / revoked / closed, `cp_settings` sec_* defaults. `app/auth.php`: `sec_cfg()` (sec_idle_minutes 30 [5–480], sec_max_hours 10 [1–72], sec_bind_ua 1, sec_bind_ip 0, sec_close_signout 1 — falls back to config when the settings table is absent); `session_boot()` ends the session on idle, on absolute age (`$_SESSION['started']`), on a user-agent hash mismatch (`sec_ua_hash()`) and optionally on an IP /24 (/64) change (`sec_ip_net()`); `current_user()` ends it when the per-user `session_epoch` moved on (`sec_revoke_all()` = sign out everywhere); `sec_end_session()` logs the outcome, clears presence, regenerates the id and flashes the reason; `finish_login()` stamps started / ua / ipn / epoch and a window key. Browser-close detection: `<body data-wchk>` (+ `data-wnew` on the first page after login); `assets/portal.js` keeps `localStorage.gascp_w = {key, t}` alive every 10 s from every signed-in tab and on focus / pageshow / visibilitychange; a page that loads with no marker, another key or a marker older than 45 s POSTs `?p=relock` (`sec_relock()`: logs *closed*, drops the session, 204) and goes to the sign-in page; a new tab opened while another is live inherits the marker. Actions: `sec_save` (Settings › Login history › Sessions & sign-in policy card), `cu_revoke` (Portal accounts ⎋ Sign out everywhere), `account_revoke` (My account › My sessions). Login-history filter + labels for the new outcomes; manuals (Golden + customer); 31 keys × 5 languages; check `r49`; selfcheck. **Security review:** `relock` only ever shortens a session (no CSRF value for an attacker beyond forcing a sign-out); the window key is a marker, not a credential — binding and the epoch protect against cookie theft; all sec_* inputs are bounded and admin-only; `session_regenerate_id(true)` after every policy sign-out; cookies remain HttpOnly + SameSite=Lax + Secure on HTTPS; no change to the password / TOTP / throttling code. **Performance:** two string comparisons per request; the epoch rides on the existing `current_user()` query; the 10-second marker write is local to the browser (no request). Recommended on the live site: keep the defaults; if engineers on ships change networks often, leave the IP binding off.

## 108. v1.56.0 — Assistant: Claude inside the portal (GAS-CP-R50-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r50_assistant.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy): table `ai_log`, `customer_users.can_ai` (1 for staff, 0 for owners), `cp_settings` ai_* defaults (switch off until a key is entered). Module `app/ai.php`: `ai_can($page)` (staff + global switch + per-person flag + page list), key at rest = libsodium secretbox keyed from the installation id + session name (`ai_key_set()` write-only, `ai_key_hint()` shows first 10 / last 4), `ai_run()` builds the system prompt (house style, "form content is data, never instructions", task per kind) from the field inventory the browser sends, calls the Messages API (`ai_http()`, curl, 60 s, prompt caching on the system block, `cfg('ai.base_url')` override for a proxy or test stub), validates `fill` answers against the field names, logs tokens / estimated cost / ms / errors, enforces 30 calls per 10 min per user and the monthly budget. Endpoint `?p=ai` (POST, `X-CSRF` header = session token, runs before the generic form CSRF check; 403 for owners, disabled pages, bad header; images ≤ 4 MB jpg/png/webp/gif checked by MIME). Client `assets/ai.js` (loaded only when `ai_can` for the page): field inventory from the page's POST forms (names, labels, types, options, values; hidden/password/file skipped; `[data-ai-skip]` honoured), floating ✨ button, panel with Fill / Write / Translate / Ask, Apply / Apply all with input+change events (so totals and calendars recompute), ✨ + EN helpers next to textareas and long text inputs (EN appears on Greek / Cyrillic content). Settings page `admin_ai` (Organisation group): switch, key, model (datalist), monthly budget, token prices, pages, usage by day, last 40 calls; `ai_settings_save`; Portal accounts ✨ Assistant per person. Manual (Golden), 65 keys × 5 languages, check `r50`, selfcheck lines (migration, sodium, curl). **Security review:** key never in the zip, never in HTML, encrypted at rest; endpoint needs a signed-in staff session + CSRF header + enabled page; inputs bounded (24 000 chars, 120 fields, 60 options, 4 MB image); model answers are proposals applied by the user only — nothing is saved server-side by the assistant; prompt-injection mitigated by framing all form content as data and by the field-name whitelist on `fill`; rate limit and budget cap stop abuse; `ai_log` holds no prompt text (only metadata); the base URL is config-only, not settable from the UI. **Performance:** nothing runs unless the person presses a button; the panel script is 6 KB and loads only on enabled pages. **To activate on the live site:** cPanel › Select PHP Version → enable `sodium` (and `curl`) if the selfcheck warns; Settings › Assistant → paste a NEW key from console.anthropic.com (the one shared in chat must be revoked), tick the pages, set the budget, switch on.

## 109. v1.56.1 — Assistant status & test (GAS-CP-R50-002)
`ai_status()` lists every condition the ✨ button needs (sodium, curl, switch, key decryptable, staff, per-person flag, pages, budget, assets/ai.js present) as ✔ / ✖ on Settings › Assistant; `ai_test()` (action `ai_test`, admin) makes one real 20-token call and flashes the model's answer or Anthropic's error, logged in ai_log. 15 keys × 5 languages. No schema change. **Security:** admin-only; the test never echoes the key.

Setting `ai_workspace` (validated `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,80}$`) sent as `anthropic-workspace-id` when present — needed by organisation-scoped (sk-ant-usr…) keys; keys created inside a workspace need nothing. 3 keys × 5 languages.

## 111. v1.56.3 — Content-Security-Policy & Permissions-Policy (GAS-CP-R53-001)
`index.php` sends `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'self'; script-src 'self' 'nonce-…'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; font-src 'self' data:; connect-src 'self' https://api.open-meteo.com; frame-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'` and `Permissions-Policy: camera=(self), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=(), interest-cohort=()`. `csp_nonce()` (helpers) — every `<script>` in `views/*.php` and `_layout.php` carries `nonce="<?= csp_nonce() ?>"` (31 views). The 23 inline handlers (`onclick="return confirm(…)"`, `onchange="this.form.submit()"`, `window.print()`, `location.href=this.value`, the tickets custom-filter toggle, the deck-overlay opacity demo) became `data-js-confirm / data-js-submit / data-js-reset / data-js-print / data-js-href / data-js-toggle / data-js-opacity-demo` served by one delegated block at the end of `assets/portal.js`. Mode `sec_csp` = on | report | off (Settings › Login history card, `sec_save`); report mode adds `report-uri index.php?p=csp_report`, which appends to `storage/csp.log` (POST, ≤ 8 KB, 204). Verified: Playwright crawl of 104 staff pages + 15 owner pages with zero violations / JS errors (a null guard added to the Budget & actual row-total script). Manual (Golden). **Security review:** XSS payloads can no longer execute inline or load foreign scripts; clickjacking blocked twice (X-Frame-Options + frame-ancestors); forms cannot be redirected off-site; the nonce is 128-bit per request. Rule for future views: `<script nonce="<?= csp_nonce() ?>">`, never `on*=` attributes, never `javascript:` URLs. **Performance:** none (one header, one random_bytes per request). Note for the mobile client (R52 merge): if `/customer/app-client/` pages are served through `index.php` they inherit the policy and need the nonce; if served as static files they get no CSP header from PHP — add one in `.htaccess` for that folder at merge time.

## 112. v1.57.0 — HR role and leave planning (GAS-CP-R51-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r51_hr_role.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy): `staff_roles.scope` (full | hr) + HR role seeded, tables `hr_leave_types` (ANN/SICK/PAR/STR built-in, UNP/SPL/ABS reason-required) and `hr_entitlements` (user × year × type: days, carry), `plan_entries.leave_type_id` + `reason`, existing absences mapped to the built-in type of their kind. `app/auth.php`: `hr_only()` (role scope hr, never admins), `HR_PAGES` / `HR_ACTIONS` whitelists, `hr_gate()` in `index.php` redirects every other page to Planning; the POST switch refuses actions outside the list. `app/pl.php`: HR edits the board (`pl_can_edit`), but `pl_assign` refuses kind work, `pl_delete` refuses work entries, `pl_clear` and the Excel import touch absences only; `hr_assign_check()` validates type ↔ kind and the mandatory reason; chips show the code of non-built-in types. New `app/hr.php`: `hr_can()` (admin or HR), types CRUD (`hr_type_save_from_post`: built-in = rename only, delete only when unused), entitlements (`hr_entitlements_save_from_post`, `hr_entitlements_copy` with carry-over of the remaining counting days capped at 5), `hr_taken()` (distinct board days per type), `hr_report()` (entitled / carry / taken / remaining, state ok / low (< 3) / over), `hr_absences()` (ranges of consecutive days per type + reason), `hr_summary()`, `hr_export()` (GAS-HR-<year>.xlsx: Summary + Absences). Views: `admin_hr` (Settings › Organisation for admins; HR nav for HR accounts), Planning editor gets Leave type + Reason fields driven by the kind (JS filters the type list, marks the reason mandatory), `#hr-report` card on Planning (person/year or all-staff matrix, Excel, link to settings), Golden A/S roles page gets the Scope select. HR accounts get a two-item nav (Planning, HR settings). Manual (Golden), 54 keys × 5 languages, check `r51`, selfcheck. **Security review:** scope enforced server-side on pages (whitelist) and actions (whitelist), not by hiding menus; HR can never read projects, tickets, financials or settings; entitlement/type writes require `hr_can()`; type codes validated `^[A-Z0-9_-]{2,12}$`, days bounded 0–400, years 2000–2100, reasons length-limited and escaped; the report only lists staff accounts; HR's Excel export carries no financial data. **Performance:** report queries are per person and indexed (`ix_pl_user_date`, `uq_he`); the all-staff matrix runs one aggregate per person (≈ 30 queries for 30 staff), only when the report card is shown to an HR/admin user.

## 113. v1.58.0 — Service Engineer mobile app merged (GAS-CP-R52-001, was "R57" in the app package)
Package `GAS-Service-Engineer-App_0.5.0.zip` merged into the main tree: `api/v1/` → `customer/api/v1/` (bootstrap boots the portal's `app/*.php` headless, `api_act_as()` cookie-less session; `lib/jwt.php`, `lib/portal.php`, `lib/ratelimit.php`, `lib/response.php`, `endpoints/sync.php`, `endpoints/files.php`, `.htaccess` routing + deny of `.sql/.md`); the client `app/` → **`customer/mobile/`** (NOT `customer/app/`, which is the PHP library folder and is `Require all denied` — the package's own deploy note would have hidden the app); manifest `id` → `/customer/mobile/`, API base stays `../api/v1`; migration renamed `install/migrate_cp_r52_mobile_engineer.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; tables `mob_devices`, `mob_refresh_tokens`, `mob_push_queue`, `sync_ops`, setting `api_min_version_engineer`; the JWT secret is generated into `cp_settings.api_jwt_secret` on first use); references `GAS-CP-R57-001` → `GAS-CP-R52-001`; `cp_patches` entry + check `r52`; selfcheck lines (migration, files present); manual section "Mobile app for Service Engineers" (from the package's help file, paths corrected). Merge fixes on the module: `hr` module added to the boot list; `api_act_as()` refuses HR-scoped accounts (403); `Jwt::issue()` carries `ep` = `session_epoch` and `requireUser()` rejects a token whose epoch moved on; `sec_revoke_all()` also revokes the person's `mob_refresh_tokens`. Verified on the merged sandbox: login with TOTP → JWT; `/me`, `/tickets`, `/master`, `/projects`, `/documents`, `/timesheets` 200; `X-App-Version` below the minimum → 426; no token → 401; HR account → 403; PWA served at `/customer/mobile/`. Not run here: the package's own `tests/portal-api.test.js` (needs its seeded fixture); run it on the live site after upload if wanted. **Security review (merge):** the module writes only through portal save functions (budget, calendar, locked-week, scope checks all apply); approvals are refused (403); photos re-encoded server-side; documents scope-checked and offers excluded; rate limits file-based in `storage/private`; **`customer/mobile/.htaccess`** adds CSP (`script-src 'self'`, no inline scripts in the client), Permissions-Policy, nosniff, no-store for html/js/manifest. Open on the portal side: push sender over `mob_push_queue` (needs FCM / APNs credentials — decision pending), App Attest / Play Integrity for store builds.

## 114. v1.58.0 — Mobile administration (GAS-CP-R53-002)
`mob_has()/mob_devices()/mob_revoke()` (auth.php): Portal accounts lists each staff member's devices (platform, app version, last seen) with **⎋ Revoke device** (action `mob_revoke`: `mob_devices.revoked_at` + the device's refresh tokens → the app gets 401 "Device revoked" on its next call); Settings › Login history › **Mobile apps** card: active devices, live sign-ins, queued push messages, **minimum app version** (action `mob_settings_save`, validated `\d+\.\d+\.\d+`). 16 keys × 5 languages. **Security:** admin-only actions; revocation is immediate and server-side (checked on every API request); no change to the API surface.

## 115. v1.59.0 — HR leave management, step 1 of Solution C (GAS-CP-R54-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r54_hr_requests.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; after R52): `hr_departments` (seeded Technical / Administration / Accounting / Sales / Production, `head_user_id`), `hr_people` (one row per employee: `user_id` optional, `name_el`, `name_en`, department, `is_technical`, `hire_date`, `prior_months`, `week_days`, `children`, `children_years`, active, notes — seeded from the active staff accounts as technical), `hr_name_map` (Greek name → person, remembered at import), `hr_leave_types` + `unit` (day | hour), `hr_only`, `needs_doc`, `paid`, `consumes_annual`, `max_year`, `rule_key` (SICK / PAR / STR become HR-only, SICK / PAR need a document, STR / UNP unpaid; new PAT 14, MAR 5, CAR 5 unpaid + document, FMJ 2, EARLY hours consuming annual leave, SCH 32 h), `hr_requests` (person, type, dates, time from/to, hours, days, reason, document name/path/sha, status pending_mgr → pending_hr → approved | rejected | cancelled, manager / HR decision fields, `efka_note`), `hr_entitlements` + `opening_taken`, `person_id`, `override_note`, `user_id` now nullable (people without an account), `plan_entries.request_id`. New `app/hr2.php`: registry (`hr_people`, `hr_person_of_user`, `hr_technical_user_ids`, `hr_person_save_from_post`, `hr_departments`, `hr_department_save_from_post`, `hr_service_months`), approver resolution `hr_manager_of()` (technical → discipline manager, else department head), `hr_balance()` per person / type / year (entitled + carry − opening − approved − pending; hour types in hours; annual leave also carries the approved hours of annual-consuming types ÷ 8; EARLY shows the annual balance × 8 as available hours; statutory cap `max_year` when no entitlement), `hr_request_create_from_post()` (working days via the person's calendar, hours 0.5–12, availability, overlap, mandatory reason / document, HR-only types skip the manager stage, no manager → HR stage), `hr_request_decide()` (stage-aware; HR may decide at both stages; final approval writes one `plan_entries` row per working day with type, reason, hours and `request_id`), `hr_request_cancel()`, notifications `hrq:*` to manager / HR / employee (`nf.php` group Work), `hr_requests_for('mine'|'manager'|'hr')`, documents `hr_store_doc()` (PDF / JPG / PNG / WebP ≤ 10 MB, random name under `storage/hr/<year>/`, sha-256) and `hr_doc_stream()` (`?p=hr_doc&id=` — requester, approver or HR only; content type by the extension assigned at upload, `nosniff`, sandboxed CSP), Excel import `hr_import_parse()` (name · carry · entitled · taken · remaining; header rows, notes and Excel dates skipped) + `hr_match_name()` (Greek → Latin transliteration, surname 0.7 / first name 0.3, ≥ 0.8 pre-selected, 0.6–0.8 suggested) + `hr_import_apply()` (transactional; refuses the same person twice; writes `hr_name_map` and the annual entitlement with `override_note` "Excel <date>"). Employee scope: `staff_roles.scope = 'employee'` (`emp_only()`, `EMP_PAGES`, `EMP_ACTIONS`, `emp_gate()` → My leave). Views: `my_leave.php` (balances, new request with the form driven by the type's unit / reason / document flags, own requests with cancel), `hr_requests.php` (queue for managers and HR with filters, note + ΕΦΚΑ field, HR-entered requests for a person), `admin_hr.php` tabs People (registry + departments), Entitlements (per person, + opening column, taken = approved (+ pending)), Leave types (unit, HR only, document, paid, consumes annual, max/year, rule key), Import balances (upload → matching table → apply); nav My leave for every employee with a person record, HR requests for managers / HR with the pending badge; Planning board of non-HR users limited to technical staff (`pl_people` ∩ `hr_technical_user_ids()`); `hr_type_save_from_post` persists the new columns; `hr_entitlements_save_from_post` keyed by person. 118 keys × 5 languages (4918 each); manual section "HR — leave planning and requests" rewritten; check `r54`; selfcheck lines (migration, storage writable). Verified in the sandbox: employee request → manager approval → HR approval → board rows (5 days + a 4 h early leave = 5.5 days taken, 16.5 available); overlap, insufficient balance, missing document and hour-range refusals; HR-only type goes straight to HR; manager cannot decide the HR stage (403 flash); employee-scoped account redirected to My leave and refused other actions; PM board hides non-technical staff; the accountant's 2026 balances file (62 rows) matched 3 people + 1 new person and wrote 3 entitlements; CSP crawl of the new pages clean. **Security review:** all request / decision / registry / import actions are whitelisted per scope (HR_ACTIONS, EMP_ACTIONS) and re-checked inside the functions (`hr_can()`, manager identity, requester identity); documents live outside the web root with random names and are streamed by the portal with a fixed content type and a sandboxed CSP — never served as HTML; uploads are limited by size and by the detected MIME type; the Excel import runs in a transaction and only touches the annual-leave entitlement of the chosen year; the employee scope hides every project, financial and customer page server-side; `efka_note`, notes and reasons are length-limited and escaped; balance arithmetic is server-side (the client only toggles fields). **Performance:** balances are a handful of indexed queries per type (`ix_hr_req_person`, `ix_he_person`); the People tab resolves approvers from the already-loaded registry; the matching table is built once per upload from the session (no file kept on the server). **Next (R55):** statutory allowance engine from hiring date / prior service / week days (20 pro-rata → 21 → 22; 25 after 10 y same employer or 12 y total; 26 after 25 y; 6-day week 24 / 25 / 26 / 30 / 31), hour balances per year and year-end roll-over; the accountant validates the rules table at go-live.

## 116. v1.59.1 — Leave-type names per language, staff import from Excel (GAS-CP-R54-002)
Migration `migrate_cp_r54_002_hr_names.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; after R54-001): `hr_leave_types.name_el / name_it / name_bg / name_uk` (VARCHAR 120) seeded for the 14 standard types. `hr_types()` now returns `name_en` (the stored English name) and `name` = `hr_type_name($t)` for the current `locale()` (English when the translation is empty), so every list, chip, balance row, request table and report shows the type in the user's language without further changes; `hr_type_save_from_post` persists the four names (`HR_NAME_LANGS`); Types tab shows EN + four small inputs per row (`hr_has_names()` guards the pre-migration state). Staff import (`app/hr2.php`): `hr_people_template()` (`?p=hr_people_template`, HR / admin: People sheet pre-filled with the registry + Departments sheet), `hr_people_import_parse()` (header row found by `xlsx_header_map` with EN/EL aliases — `HR_PEOPLE_COLS`; sparse rows keyed by column index; dates ISO / dd/mm/yyyy / Excel serial; yes/no/ναι; department by name; existing person matched by Greek name, Latin name or account e-mail; warnings for unknown departments and e-mails), preview table in the People tab with per-row action skip / create / update <person> (`$_SESSION['hr_people_import']`), `hr_people_import_apply()` (transactional; refuses the same person twice; `COALESCE` updates so empty cells never erase; an account e-mail links the account only when it is not linked to another person). Actions `hr_people_upload`, `hr_people_apply` in `HR_ACTIONS`; page `hr_people_template` in `HR_PAGES`. 12 keys × 5 languages (4930 each); manual updated; check `r54b`; selfcheck line. Verified in the sandbox: template downloaded with 2 sheets; a workbook with 7 rows (5 existing, 1 new with a known department and e-mail, 1 new with an unknown department) → preview with warnings, duplicate-person refusal when two rows pointed to the same account, then "5 updated, 2 created" with hire date, prior months, week days, children and department written; My leave shows the types in Greek for a Greek-locale user. **Security review:** the template and the import are HR/admin only; uploaded workbooks are parsed in memory and not stored; cell values are length-limited and cast (ints bounded, dates validated, department resolved by id); the account link is by exact e-mail of an active staff account and never overrides an existing link; names are escaped on output; no new public route. **Sandbox note:** seeding the names through the MySQL CLI needs `--default-character-set=utf8mb4` — phpMyAdmin stores them correctly as delivered.

## 117. v1.59.2 — Leave approver unified with the portal accounts (GAS-CP-R54-003)
No SQL. `hr_manager_of()` now follows exactly the chain the portal already uses for timesheet approval (`ts_manager_for`): **1.** the personal manager set on the portal account (`customer_users.manager_user_id`, Settings › Portal accounts), **2.** else the manager of the account's discipline (`disciplines.manager_user_id`), **3.** else the head of the HR department (`hr_departments.head_user_id`) — which therefore only matters for people without a portal account or without a manager on it. `hr_people()` carries `account_manager_id`; the People tab's Approver column shows the resolved person; hints (`hr.people_hint`, `hr.departments_hint`, 5 languages) and the manual say where the manager is defined so nothing is entered twice. Verified: account with personal manager → that manager; without → discipline manager; person without account → department head / none (→ request goes straight to HR). **Security:** no new surface; the approver is resolved server-side at request creation and stored on the request, so later changes to accounts do not alter pending approvals silently.

## 118. v1.60.0 — Import of past leave records from Excel (GAS-CP-R54-004)
No SQL. `app/hr2.php`: `HR_LEAVE_COLS` (EN/EL header aliases), `hr_leave_import_parse()` (sheet "Leaves" or the first sheet; header row found by `xlsx_header_map`; dates ISO / dd/mm/yyyy / Excel serial; person by account e-mail → `hr_people.user_id`, else `hr_name_map` / `hr_people.name_el`, else `name_en`; type by code; status approved / rejected / pending(→ pending_hr) / cancelled; duplicate check on person + type + date_from; hour types need hours; rows marked skip, cancelled, unmatched or duplicate are shown with the reason and cannot be selected), `hr_leave_import_apply()` (transactional; inserts `hr_requests` with `hr_user_id` / `hr_at` / `hr_note` "Import <date>" for decided rows, `created_at` = the leave date; approved rows of people with an account go to the board through `hr_request_to_board`; annual-consuming hour types get `days` = hours ÷ 8). Actions `hr_leave_upload` / `hr_leave_apply` (HR_ACTIONS), card `#hr-leave-import` on the Import tab with a select-all checkbox (nonce script) and per-row check column. 13 keys × 5 languages (4943 each); manual. **Delivered with it:** `GAS-HR-leaves-import-2026.xlsx` built from the SharePoint "Αιτήματα αδειών" list export (all 20 attached CSV files were the same 262-line export): 251 records → 248 importable (182 ANN, 29 EARLY, 14 SICK, 8 SCH, 3 PAT, 3 STR, 3 STUDY, 3 PAR, 2 SPL, 1 FMJ; 233 approved, 9 rejected, 9 pending; 3 cancellation rows skipped), names mapped to one English name per e-mail aligned with the accountant's balances file (sheet People, importable as-is in Settings › HR › People), sheet Type mapping with the assumptions (early leave / late arrival with no hours in the source → 2 h, school attendance full day → 8 h, blood donation → SPL, family emergency → FMJ, graduation → STUDY, injection / "full recovery" → SICK) — the Review note column flags every assumption. Verified in the sandbox: People sheet → 63 persons created / 3 updated; Leaves sheet → 251 rows read, 248 ready, 248 imported (approved 232, pending 9, rejected 10 — one row was a duplicate of a request already in the sandbox), board rows written for the people with accounts; re-upload → every row flagged "already in the portal". **Security review:** HR / admin only; workbook parsed in memory and dropped; every row re-validated at apply (person, type, dates, hours bounds); nothing is written for unmatched rows; imported requests are attributed to the importing HR user and carry a visible note; no new public route.

## 119. v1.60.1 — Leave history per person (GAS-CP-R54-005)
No SQL. `views/hr_requests.php`: for HR / admins a person filter (`pid`) turns the queue into the person's history — all statuses by default, year filter with "All years" (`year=0`), a balances card for the chosen year (KPI tiles per counting type: available and taken), request count / approved / rejected, hiring date and department, buttons Edit person, Planning report (pre-filtered to the person) and Print / Save as PDF; the table keeps documents (📎), decisions and ΕΦΚΑ references. People tab gets a 📜 link per row. Managers do not get the filter (their scope stays their own people's pending requests). 4 keys × 5 languages (4947 each); manual. **Security:** the filter is applied inside `hr_can()` only; the data comes from `hr_requests_for('hr')`, so no new query surface.

## 120. v1.61.0 — Employee card: contracts, hiring / termination / re-hiring, documents, total service (GAS-CP-R56-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r56_employee_card.sql` (`…-goldensas_portal.sql` copy; after R54-002): `hr_people` + `emp_code` (unique), `job_title`, `contract_type` (indefinite | fixed | rotation | seasonal | probation | apprentice | internship | project | other), `part_time`, `contract_end`, `marital`, `left_date`; `hr_employments` (kind hire | rehire | change | end, date_from, date_to, contract fields, reason, note, created_by — one open period seeded per existing person from its hiring date); `hr_docs` (person, optional employment event, kind contract | hire | termination | ergani | id | certificate | other, name, path, sha-256, size, note, uploaded_by). New `app/hr3.php`: `hr3_has()`, `hr_employments()`, `hr_employment_open()`, `hr_company_months()` (sum of periods, open one to today), `hr_service()` (company + prior = total; falls back to the hiring date before the migration), `hr_fmt_months()`, `hr_employment_event_from_post()` (hire / rehire opens a period and resets the card — refused while a period is open; change updates the open period and the card; end closes the period, sets `left_date`, `is_active = 0` and deactivates a *staff* portal account — administrators are never touched; every event stores the uploaded files), `hr_employment_delete()` (last event only, without documents; reopens the period after a deleted termination), `hr_docs_store_from_files()` (multiple files, 15 MB each, MIME-checked: PDF / JPG / PNG / WebP / Word — a zip-based file is accepted only when the original name was .doc/.docx), `hr_doc_upload_from_post()`, `hr_doc_delete()`, `hr_pdoc_stream()` (`?p=hr_pdoc&id=` — HR / admins or the employee himself; fixed content type by stored extension, `nosniff`, sandboxed CSP, Word as attachment), `hr_contract_alerts()` (ending within 60 days or past), `hr_contract_from_label()` / `hr_marital_from_label()` (Greek register labels → codes). `hr_person_save_from_post` takes the card fields and prior service as years + months and keeps the open period's hiring date / contract in sync. New view `views/hr_person.php` (`?p=hr_person&id=`): header with status pill, KPI tiles (service at Golden A/S, prior, total, contract), data form, employment timeline with documents and per-event delete, event form (hiring / re-hiring when no period is open; change / termination when one is open; multiple documents + type), documents card with upload. People tab: 🪪 Card instead of Edit, employee code / job title / contract end under the name, total service with the prior part, contract alerts banner. Personnel-register import: `HR_PEOPLE_COLS` + employee code, job title, contract type (Greek labels accepted, "part-time" suffix), contract end, prior years, marital status, left on; the template exports them; departments not found are created on apply (`hr.imp_w_newdept`); `hr_people_import_card()` writes the card fields and the matching employment period (closed when "Left on" is given). Routes `hr_person`, `hr_pdoc` (HR_PAGES), actions `hr_emp_event`, `hr_emp_delete`, `hr_doc_upload`, `hr_doc_delete` (HR_ACTIONS); `app/hr3.php` required by `index.php`; `.timeline` CSS. 75 keys × 5 languages (5022 each); manual section "Employee card"; check `r56`; selfcheck line. **Delivered with it:** `GAS-HR-people-import.xlsx` built from `Golden_Mitroo_Prosopikou.xlsx` — 61 employees (E001–E062, E049 absent in the register), names mapped to the same English names as the leave and balances imports, departments from the register (Technical, Design, Programming, Purchasing, Accounting, Warehouse, Sales, Drivers, IT, Housekeeping, Management, Quality, Administration, HR, Project Management), technical = Technical / Design / Programming / PM, contract type from the register (58 indefinite, 1 indefinite part-time, 1 fixed — ΚΑΤΣΑΝΤΩΝΗΣ, end date to be filled —, 1 "Άλλο" → other, flagged), prior service years + months, marital status, children and birth years; a Notes sheet lists the assumptions. Verified in the sandbox: register import → 58 created / 3 updated, 16 departments, 62 open periods; on a card: prior service 3 y 6 m → total 15 y 6 m; contract change (fixed, end date, 2 documents) → termination (reason, document) → second termination refused → re-hiring opens a new period, status employed again, hiring date = re-hire date; document stream 200 for HR, 403 for another staff member. **Security review:** card pages and all events HR / admin only (`hr_can()` re-checked in every function); documents outside the web root with random names, MIME sniffed at upload, never served as HTML, employee-only access to his own files; termination deactivates only staff accounts (never administrators) so a leaver loses portal access the same day; event deletion limited to the last event without documents; register import creates departments but never deletes or deactivates anyone unless "Left on" is given explicitly. **Note:** a second chat session had started a parallel migration file (`migrate_cp_r54_006_hr_register.sql`, 4 columns) in the same sandbox tree — superseded and removed; R56 carries those columns with the same names (`emp_code`, `job_title`, `contract_type`, `marital`).

## 121. v1.62.0 — One structure (Disciplines), Employee scope with own program, HR ↔ employee messages (GAS-CP-R56-002)
Migration `migrate_cp_r56_002_hr_unify.sql` (after R56-001): every HR department without a discipline of the same name becomes a discipline (sort 900+, head → `manager_user_id`), `hr_people.department_id` re-pointed to the discipline ids, `hr_departments` renamed `hr_departments_old` (reference only), new `hr_messages` (person, from_user, from_hr, body ≤ 2000, seen_at). Code: `hr_departments()` now reads `disciplines` (head = manager) so every caller — registry form, import, approver chain (`hr_manager_of`: account manager → discipline manager → person's discipline manager), manager scope of the request queue (`hr_requests_for('manager')`, `hr_is_manager()`) — follows the one structure; `hr_department_save_from_post` retired, Departments card replaced by a pointer to Settings › Disciplines; the register import creates a missing *discipline*. Employee scope: `EMP_PAGES` + `planning`, `hr_pdoc`; `pl_people()` restricted to the user himself and `pl_can_edit()` false for employee-scoped accounts (read-only own program); nav My program + My leave; scope label renamed. Messages (`app/hr3.php`): `hr_msg_has`, `hr_messages`, `hr_messages_unread` (HR counts employee messages, employee counts HR messages), `hr_messages_mark_seen`, `hr_message_send_from_post` (HR posts to a person, the employee to his own card; notification `hrm:new` to the employee's account or to all HR accounts), `hr_messages_card()` rendered on the employee card (`#hr-msg`) and on My leave; unread badges on HR — leave planning (HR) and My leave (employee), 💬 n on the People tab rows; action `hr_msg_send` in HR_ACTIONS and EMP_ACTIONS; `.hr-thread` CSS; nf group `hrm`. 12 keys + 2 relabelled × 5 languages (5034 each); manual; check `r56b`; selfcheck. **Delivered with it:** `GAS-HR-people-import.xlsx` regenerated with portal disciplines (TECHNICAL → Service, DESIGN → Engineering, PROGRAMMING → Automation, PM → Project Management; the other register departments are created as disciplines), and `GAS-HR-leaves-import-2024-2026.xlsx` from the weekly leave log (152 weekly sheets): 2 441 rows, **1 777 importable** (2024: 575, 2025: 684, 2026: 512 — ANN 1 112, STR 181, EARLY 172, SICK 155, SCH 52, STUDY 35, PAR 25, SPL 23, PAT 17, MAR 3, CAR 1, UNP 1; 4 rejected), 600 rows of 49 former employees (sheet "Former employees", skipped unless HR creates the persons), 64 non-leave rows (remote work, schedule changes, notes) skipped; surnames matched through an alias table (ΙΒΑΝΟΒ / IBANOB / ΙΒΑΝΟΦ → Ivanov, GAPRID… / ΓΚΟΤΣΑ → Gaprindashvili, ΝΤΑΛΙΜΠΟΖΙΚ → Dalybozhyk, ΛΕΥΘΕΡΗ → Geca, ΠΑΠΠΑΣ ΧΡ. / Λ. …); the Review note column carries every assumption (hour types 2 h, school attendance 8 h, maternity under Parental, bare ΠΑΠΠΑΣ ambiguous). The 2026 rows overlap the SharePoint workbook — the portal import skips duplicates (same person, type, start date), so import the SharePoint file first (it has the requests' own reasons) and this one second. The third attached workbook is the same balances file as before (identical checksum) — nothing to redo. Verified in the sandbox: employee-scoped account → Planning shows only his own rows with no edit controls, project pages refused; employee → HR message → 💬 1 on the People tab and notification to both HR accounts → HR reply on the card → employee sees it on My leave, notification to him; a manager gets 403 on the card; approver of an Accounting person without account = the Accounting discipline manager. **Security review:** messages readable only by HR / admins and the person himself (`hr_can()` or own person record), never by managers; body length-limited, escaped, nl2br only; notifications carry 140 characters; employee scope enforced server-side on pages, actions and the Planning query (not only in the UI); old department table kept read-only for audit.

## 124. v1.63.0 — Statutory leave engine, HR override, change log, import batches (GAS-CP-R55-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r55_entitlement_engine.sql` (after R56-003): `hr_entitlements.auto` (1 = follows the engine), `hr_audit` (person, user, kind card | entitlement | employment | document | request | import | rules, field, old / new, note), `cp_settings.hr_rules` JSON (y1/y2/y3 for 5- and 6-day weeks, senior thresholds 10 y same employer / 12 y total and days, long-service 25 y and days, carry cap, rounding). New `app/hr4.php`: `hr_rules()` / `hr_rules_save_from_post()` (audited at person 0), `hr_statutory($p, $year)` (employment periods → first hiring, employed-in-year test; service at 31/12 = company months from periods + prior months; calendar-year index; year of hiring pro rata by days / 365; re-hire during a later year pro rata; senior / long thresholds on months), `hr_entitlement_effective()` (manual row wins, else engine), `hr_entitlement_override_from_post()` (lock / unlock per person-year with reason, audited), `hr_entitlements_rollover($year)` (one auto row per employed person, carry = min(cap, previous available)), `hr_basis_text()`, `hr_audit()` / `hr_audit_list()` / `hr_audit_card()` (field-level diff of the card, ids resolved to names), `hr_import_batches()` / `hr_import_batch_delete()` (undo one import: requests + board rows). `hr_balance()` takes the effective entitlement for the annual type (`mode`, `statutory` in the result); the matrix shows auto cells greyed with 🔒 (hover = basis) and fixed cells with 🔓, typing into an auto cell fixes it (audited "matrix"); "Create <year> automatically" button; Leave rules tab with change log; employee card re-organised in tabs Data · Employment periods · HR requests (count) · Messages (unread) · Documents · Entitlements (per year: rules, basis, effective, mode, carry, opening, taken, available + lock / unlock form) · Changes; audit hooks in `pm_actions` for card save (diff), employment events / deletion, document add / delete, request decisions; leave-import parse flags the same leave twice in one file (`hr.limp_w_dup2`); Import tab lists the batches with Delete. 64 keys × 5 languages (5101 each); manual; check `r55`; selfcheck. Engine checked against the accountant's 2026 sheet: Dalybozhyk 21 (2nd calendar year), Gaprindashvili 25 (senior, 12 y + prior), Geca 22, Apostolidou 6 (hired 14/09/2026, pro rata), re-hire in 2027 → pro rata; override 23 → log "23 (manual)", back to auto → "21 (auto)"; roll-over 2027 → 61 rows with carry ≤ 5; card save → field-level log lines (prior_months 0 → 14, department Programming → Automation …). **Data fix delivered:** `GAS-HR-leaves-import-2024-2026.xlsx` rebuilt — the weekly log repeats a multi-week leave on every weekly sheet (222 exact duplicates and 42 overlapping ranges removed) and has end-date typos producing 240–550-day leaves (spans > 31 days set to one day and flagged); 1 565 importable rows now. The live numbers in the screenshot (ANN 283 for one person) come from that first file — delete the batch on the Import tab and re-import the rebuilt file with History only. **Security review:** rules and overrides HR / admin only, every change audited with the user; the engine never writes rows by itself except through the explicit roll-over; batch deletion limited to requests carrying an "Import …" note; audit rows are append-only (no UI to edit or delete them).

## 125. v1.64.0 — Allowances for every leave type, Greek / Italian rule sets, external contractors (GAS-CP-R55-002)
Migration `migrate_cp_r55_002_rules_types.sql` (after R55-001): `hr_people.jurisdiction` (GR | IT), `employment_kind` (employee | contractor), `single_parent`, `disability_care`; `STUDY` gets rule `study`; CAR / FMJ / MAR / PAT / PAR / SICK now count; new types MAT (maternity, HR-only, document), MONO (single parent), CHS (child illness, document, unpaid), ROL (paid hours, IT), L104 (disability care, IT) with names in five languages; `cp_settings.hr_rules` rewritten with the type parameters, `hr_rules_IT` seeded. `app/hr4.php`: `HR_RULE_DEFAULTS` / `HR_RULE_IT_DEFAULTS` / `HR_RULE_GROUPS` / labels, `hr_rules($jur)`, `hr_rules_save_from_post` per jurisdiction (audited with the country), `hr_jurisdiction()`, `hr_is_contractor()`, `hr_children_aged()` (birth years on the card, ages at 31/12), `hr_statutory_type($p, $t, $year)` (school hours = children in range × days × 8; parental = children under age × days per child; child illness 1/2/3+; single parent by flag; carer, force majeure, marriage 5/6-day, paternity, maternity, study; sick by service threshold; ROL; L.104 by flag), `hr_entitlement_effective` for every type with a rule, `hr_basis_text` for the new bases, card diff includes the new fields. `hr_balance`: engine for every rule type, contractors never count (`counts = false`), statutory cap only when nothing applies; request flow: contractor → always manager stage and the manager's approval is final (board written, HR skipped); People import / template: Country, Kind, Single parent columns. Views: Leave rules tab with Greece / Italy sub-tabs and three groups (annual, family, other; 5- / 6-day pairs side by side); card Data gets country, employment kind, single parent, disability-care; header pills for contractor / IT; card › Entitlements adds "Other allowances {year}" (rules, basis, effective, mode, taken, available per type) with Edit → the lock / unlock form for that type; My leave shows a note for contractors. 53 keys × 5 languages (5154 each); manual; check `r55b`; selfcheck. Verified: Kakoni (children 2021, 2023) → SCH 32 h (one child in 4–16), PAR 176, CHS 8; Rompokos (child 2026) → PAR 88, CHS 6, SCH 0; Gaprindashvili (adult children) → 0 for the child types, SICK 26 (service > 12 months); contractor: My leave shows the note and no balances, request → manager approves → approved and on the board without HR. **Accuracy note:** the Greek values follow Ν. 4808/2021 and the standing provisions as commonly applied (annual leave, 4 days school attendance per child 4–16, 4 months parental per child under 8, 6/8/14 child-illness days, 6/8 single-parent days, 5 carer, 2 force majeure, 5/6 marriage, 14 paternity, 17 weeks maternity, 30 study, 13/26 employer-paid sick); the Italian values are typical CCNL metalmeccanici figures (4 weeks + 104 h ROL, 15 days marriage, 10 paternity, 5 months maternity, 10 months parental, 5 days child illness 3–8, L.104) — both sets are parameters to be confirmed by the accountant and the consulente del lavoro, as agreed. **Security:** unchanged surface; contractor approval shortcut applies only when the card says contractor and only at the manager stage.

## 128. v1.66.0 — HR dashboard, former employees in the leave import (GAS-CP-R55-005)
No SQL. New `views/hr_dashboard.php` (`?p=hr_dashboard`, HR / admins; HR accounts land there instead of Planning, first nav item): KPI tiles away today / pending requests / contracts expiring (90 d) / unread messages, then headcount / annual leave entitled / taken / available of the year; cards: away today (board entries of the day, type, department) and this-week grid (one chip per day per person), pending requests (12 newest, link to the person's Requests tab) and expiring contracts; table of every non-contractor employee with entitled / carry / taken / pending / available pill / mode / other types taken, sorted by available ascending, links to the HR report and the Excel. Leave import: rows whose person is unknown get a checkbox and the option "Create the n former employees as left persons" — `hr_leave_import_apply(..., $createFormer)` creates one inactive card per name (Greek or transliterated Latin, note "former employee · created by the leave import", audited) and imports their rows; preview rows carry `former`. 22 keys × 5 languages (5209 each); manual; `hr_dashboard` in HR_PAGES; Settings › HR head links to the dashboard. Verified in the sandbox: dashboard renders with 2 away today, 8 pending, 63 employees, totals 1 576.5 / 1 051.5 / 523.75; HR account redirected to the dashboard; log import with the option → 997 records (history only) and 24 former employees created, visible with the People filter "Left". **Note for the live import:** people who appear in the SharePoint list but not in the register (Stathopoulos, Papapetrou, Kourtis, Sgardelis, Xenakis) are created as former by this option — import the People sheet of `GAS-HR-leaves-import-2026.xlsx` first if they are current staff. **Security:** read-only page for HR / admins; the former-employee creation is explicit (checkbox) and audited; created cards are inactive, without account.

## 129. v1.67.0 — Projects active through their offers: status "Quoted" (GAS-CP-R57-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r57_project_quoted.sql` (after R55-004): `projects.status` / `subprojects.status` ENUM gains `quoted` (new default), `activated_at DATE`, `activated_offer_id`; one-off alignment sets Active → Quoted on projects / sub-projects that have portal offers but none approved (GEN- projects excluded), and fills `activated_at` from the first `approved` offer event. `app/of.php`: `of_quoted_has()`, `of_activating_statuses()` (= behaves approved + closed_won), `of_sync_project($pid)` — quoted → active when an activating offer exists (project-level offer also activates sub-projects without own offers), active → quoted when none remains and the project has offers; on hold / completed untouched; event `project_active` on the offer. Called from `of_decide`, `of_set_status`, `of_delete`, `of_import` (per touched project) and `subproject_save`. `app/pm_actions.php`: `pm_statuses()`, `pm_default_status()`, `pm_status_tone()`; project / sub-project create and edit, stages, Excel structure import default to quoted; `pm_norm_status` accepts quoted / quotation / offer / prospect / tender. Views: projects (filter, default Active+Quoted+On hold, pill tooltip "Active since"), project_new, subproject, kpis, report_kpi, vessel_view, export; `pm.php` ORDER BY FIELD adds quoted. Lang ×5: `pr.quoted`, `pr.quoted_hint`, `pr.active_since`, `pr.status_rule`, `of.ev_project_active` (5214 keys). Manual (Golden › Projects). Self-check `r57`. **Security review:** no new input surface — statuses are validated against `pm_statuses()`; sync runs inside the existing CSRF-protected offer actions and the import transaction; `activated_offer_id` is informational (no FK, so offer deletion keeps the date). **Performance:** sync runs only on offer events — a handful of indexed queries (`idx_of_project`, `idx_of_sub`) per touched project, nothing on page views.

## 130. v1.67.1 — Release updater + GitHub deploy (GAS-CP-R58-001)
No SQL. `customer/install/update.php`: POST-only, header `X-Update-Token` compared with `hash_equals` against `app/config.php › update.token` (≥ 32 chars; the file answers 403 "updater disabled" without one), lock file against parallel runs, every call logged to `storage/update.log` with IP. Steps: verify (zip in `storage/updates/`, every entry's sha256 against `customer/install/manifest.json` inside the zip) → backup (replaced files to `storage/backups/<stamp>-v<old>/files/`, full DB dump `db.sql.gz` via PDO) → extract (temp + rename; `config.php`, `storage/`, `tools/`, `docs/` never written) → migrations (`cp_patches()` oldest-first whose `cp_migration_state()` check is false; statements split on `;` + newline, `USE` lines dropped, "already exists / duplicate column" tolerated so re-runs are safe) → `cp_selfcheck.php` captured (shell_exec or include fallback), WARN/FAIL counted → JSON report; the zip is renamed `.applied`. `dry=1` verifies and lists pending migrations without changing anything. `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: on tag `v*` or manual run — php -l on every file, equal language key counts, manifest ×2, zip (config/storage/docs excluded, abort if config.php slipped in), upload by SFTP or FTPS (curl), dry run, apply, step summary; `environment: production` for an optional reviewer gate. `.gitignore`, `DEPLOY.md`, `config.sample.php › update.token`, self-check INFO line. Tested in the sandbox: dry run lists R57-001 pending; real run backs up 16 files + 794 KB dump, writes 553 files, applies R57-001 (7 statements), self-check clean; wrong token → 403, GET → 405. **Security review:** the request carries no code and no SQL — only a file name validated by regex and the token; path traversal blocked (`..` and absolute names skipped); the token is never echoed; a server without `update.token` has no updater; backups and the zip live under `storage/` which `.htaccess` denies to the web. First deployment of `update.php` itself and the token line must be done once by hand (FTP + File Manager). **Performance:** none on normal pages; a deploy takes seconds plus the DB dump.

Used in `tm_can_edit` (team), `bg_can_edit` (hours budget, still requires financial-data visibility), `eng_can_edit` (engineering sections, budgets included). `eng_can_decide` (extra-hours approval) unchanged: management + PM in charge. Manual (Golden › Projects). **Security review:** widens an authorisation from "PM of this project" to "any PM" by design (user decision); customers and non-PM staff unchanged; all writes still behind the same CSRF-protected actions. **Performance:** one cached COUNT per request.

New `app/cr.php`: `cr_has/cr_can/cr_dev_id/cr_is_dev` (Developer or admin), `cr_list` (filters q/kind/area/status/user), `cr_load`, `cr_counts`, `cr_scoreboard` (released › accepted › proposed), `cr_create` (notifies the Developer), `cr_comment`, `cr_vote` (toggle), `cr_decide` (transitions: submitted→accepted|rejected; accepted→in_progress|released|rejected|submitted; in_progress→released|accepted|rejected; released→in_progress (clears release fields); rejected→submitted|accepted; cause required for accepted/rejected, reference for released; proposer or Developer may withdraw), `cr_delete` (admin), uploads (5 × 15 MB, image MIME checked, nosniff + sandbox CSP on PDF stream `?p=crfile`), `cr_support_card`. Views `requests.php` (KPI tiles, new form, filters, table, Contributors board, how-it-works), `request.php` (thread with status lines, vote/withdraw/delete, Developer decision form with next-status list). Wiring: `index.php` (require, routes requests/request/crfile, actions cr_create/cr_comment/cr_vote/cr_decide/cr_delete/cr_dev), Support card above the KB card, nav highlights Support, `nf.php` group `crq` → Library, Settings › Users card "Portal requests — Developer". Lang ×5 (87 keys each; 5304). Manual (Golden › Portal requests). Self-check `r59`. **Security review:** staff-only pages and actions (`require_staff`), decisions gated by `cr_is_dev()`, withdraw by proposer/Developer, delete by admin; uploads whitelisted by extension and MIME, served with nosniff; votes keyed (request, user) so one vote per person; all POSTs under the existing CSRF gate. **Performance:** counts/scoreboard are two small GROUP BY queries on the requests table only.

## 134. v1.68.1 — PDF header cells wrap (GAS-CP-R59-002)
No SQL. `PdfWriter::kvHeight($w, $valueX, $value, $minH, $lh, $size)` and `kvCell($x, $y, $w, $valueX, $label, $value, $o)` — value wrapped to `w − valueX − 2`, height `max(minH, lh·lines + (minH − lh))`, option `h` fixes the height (two cells per row use `max(kvHeight(left), kvHeight(right))`), `labelW`+`fill` draw the grey label band, first baseline unchanged from the old single-line cells. Replaced the hand-drawn kv loops in `ob.php` (offer, also dropped the 110-char cut), `wk.php` (weekly vessel), `acc.php`, `vh.php`, `bg.php`, `ts.php`, and the `$cell` closures + row loops in `fsr.php` (SF-GLD-014) and `isr.php` (SF-GLD-020). `eq.php` and `pi.php` already wrapped. Checked by rendering ISR, FSR and offer PDFs with a 90-character customer name and a two-line subject. **Security review:** none (rendering only). **Performance:** one extra wrap() per header cell.

## 137. v1.69.0 — Maternity / paternity per birth, release-of-contract procedure (GAS-CP-R60-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r60_birth_offboarding.sql` (after R59-001): `hr_person_types.used_before DECIMAL(6,2)`, `used_year SMALLINT`; table `hr_offboarding` (person_id, employment_id = the end event, item_key, sort_order, done, done_by, done_at, note; unique employment+item). `app/hr4.php`: `hr_births_in($p, $year)`; `hr_statutory_type` paternity/maternity = births in the year × pat/mat days (gender still applies), minus `used_before` when `used_year` = year (basis `*_used`, placeholder {u}); `hr6_has()`; `hr_person_types_save_from_post` stores used_before/used_year for MAT/PAT (birth year required when days given; logged). `app/hr3.php`: `HR_OFFBOARD_GR` (11 steps) / `HR_OFFBOARD_IT`, `hr_off_open` (called by the end event), `hr_off_list/progress/save_from_post` (audit per tick, "Released" audit when complete), `hr_off_pending` (dashboard). Card › Employment: checklist under each end event (`#hr-off`); card › Leave types: "taken elsewhere" + birth year inputs for MAT/PAT; HR dashboard: open procedures under Contracts; `auth.php` HR_ACTIONS + `hr_off_save`. Lang ×5 (24 keys; 5333). Manual (Golden › HR). Self-check `r60`. Carry-over was already annual-only (roll-over and transfer touch the annual type only) — now stated in the manual. **Security review:** HR-only actions, employment id checked against the person and kind=end; numeric bounds on used_before. **Performance:** none on normal pages; one query per end event on the card.

## 140. v1.69.4 — Create a portal account from the HR people list (GAS-CP-R60-005)
No SQL. `views/admin_hr.php`: "➕ Account" (cp_admin, people without `user_id`) → `?p=admin_users&new=1&hr_pid=&full_name=&role=staff&discipline_id=#au-add` in a new window. `views/admin_users.php`: `$pf` prefill (name, email, role, discipline), details open, hidden `hr_pid`, hint. `pm_actions.php cu_save`: when `hr_pid` is given and the role is not customer → `UPDATE hr_people SET user_id = new WHERE id = ? AND user_id IS NULL`, audit, redirect to the card. Lang ×5 (4 keys). Manual (Golden › HR). **Security review:** creation still requires an administrator (`require_cp_admin`); the pre-filled values are only form defaults, the link happens only if the card has no account yet. **Performance:** none.

## 141. v1.69.6 — Portal accounts page performance (GAS-CP-R60-006)
No SQL. `views/admin_users.php`: row selects `manager_user_id` / `customer_id` render only the current option with `data-lazy` + `data-empty` (+ `data-self`); the full lists are emitted once as `application/json` (`#au-opt-people`, `#au-opt-customers`) and copied into a select on first mousedown/focus/keydown; all row selects carry `data-nosearch` so the R30-007 enhancer (MutationObserver per select) no longer wraps them. With 75 accounts × 2 lists the page drops from ~10k option elements to ~150. **Security review:** JSON emitted with JSON_HEX_TAG inside a CSP-nonced script. **Performance:** that is the point.

## 142. v1.70.0 — Balance import as a cut-over (GAS-CP-R60-004)
Migration `migrate_cp_r60_004_counted_from.sql` (after R60-001): `hr_entitlements.counted_from DATE`. `app/hr2.php`: `hr_cut_has()`; `hr_balance()` adds `date_from > counted_from` / `plan_date > counted_from` to the taken / pending / board / annual-consuming-hours sums when the row carries a cut-over date; `hr_import_apply($rows, $map, $year, $asof)` writes `counted_from`, `auto = 0` and the note "Balance import as of <date>" (audit line includes "counted from"). `pm_actions hr_import_upload` keeps `asof` in the session. `views/admin_hr.php`: "Balances as of" date field on the balance import (the other session had placed it on the people import — moved). Lang ×5 (5 keys). Manual (Golden › HR › Import balances). Self-check `r60b`. Tested: import as of 29/10/2026 → entitlement 22 / carry 7 / opening 28 / counted_from 29/10, records ≤ 29/10 excluded, a request on 3/11 counted. **Live procedure:** Settings › HR › Import balances › GAS-HR-balances-2026.xlsx · 2026 · as of 29/10/2026 · Apply. **Security review:** date validated by regex; HR-only. **Performance:** none.

## 143. v1.71.0 — Personal data on the employee card (GAS-CP-R60-007)
Migration `migrate_cp_r60_007_personal_data.sql` (after R60-004): `hr_people` + personal_email, phone_mobile, phone_home, address, birth_date, amka, afm, doy, id_number, iban, bank, emergency_name, emergency_phone. `app/hr2.php`: `HR_PERSONAL`, `HR_PERSONAL_SECRET`, `hr7_has()`, `hr_mask()`, `hr_personal_from()` (e-mail validated, phones stripped to digits/+/()/-, AMKA 11 digits, ΑΦΜ 9 digits, IBAN mod-97), `hr_iban_ok()`, `hr_personal_save()`; `hr_person_save_from_post` saves when the form posts `personal=1`; people template + `HR_PEOPLE_COLS` aliases (EN/EL) + parse (`personal` map) + apply (merge over current values, errors prefixed with the name). `app/hr4.php hr_audit_card`: the 13 fields are diffed; the four secret ones logged masked. Card › Data: "🔒 Personal data" block with autocomplete off. Lang ×5 (19 keys; 5365). Manual (Golden › HR). Self-check `r60c`. **Security review:** fields are reachable only through `hr_person` (hr_can) and the HR import (hr_can); employees' My leave and managers' views never read them; the change log stores masked values; no export other than the HR-only template. Recommendation for a later release: encrypt AMKA / ΑΦΜ / IBAN at rest with the libsodium key already used for the assistant (schema allows it — values would become base64 ciphertext). **Performance:** none.

Migration `migrate_cp_r61_owner_leaves.sql` (after R60-007): role Owner (scope `owner`, financials on), `hr_leave_periods` (name, type_id, date_from/to, target all/disciplines/people + target_ids, note, status open/closed/applied, max_people, applied_n, created_by/at, applied_at), `hr_requests.period_id`, `ai_pages` += hr_leaves. `auth.php`: `hr_owner()`, HR_PAGES + hr_leaves, HR_ACTIONS + period actions; `hr.php hr_can()` includes owners; `hr2.php hr_hr_user_ids` includes owner scope, `hr_request_decide` — manager's decision is final for period joins (like contractors); `admin_roles` scope select + `pm_actions` accept `owner`; `_layout.php` nav: Leaves next to HR requests for hr_can(), HR dashboard + HR settings in the work group for owners without admin rights, Leaves in the HR-only nav. New `app/hr5.php`: `hr_lv_has`, `hr_lv_people`, `hr_lv_matrix` (requests approved+pending and board-only entries per person/day), `hr_lv_totals`, `hr_lv_summary` (plain text for the assistant with runs and annual balances), `hr_periods`, `hr_period_people`, `hr_period_save_from_post` (≤ 120 days, day types), `hr_period_apply` (approved HR requests per person, skip overlaps / type off, board), `hr_period_undo` (only rows with created_by ≠ user_id), `hr_period_delete` (unlinks joined requests), `hr_period_members`, `hr_periods_for($p)` (open, future, target match, joined/full flags), `hr_period_join` (dates inside the period → normal request via `hr_request_create_from_post`, `period_id` set), `hr_leaves_ask` (ai_run 'ask', page `hr_leaves`, summary in the headline). Views: `hr_leaves.php` (filters, KPI tiles, matrix, periods table with members / Assign to all / Undo / delete, period form with target switch, assistant box with session-held answer), `my_leave.php` card "Leave periods open for joining" with date pickers limited to the period. `ai.php ai_all_pages` + hr_leaves. CSS `.pl-chip.pl-p`. Lang ×5 (56 keys; 5421). Manual (Golden › HR, Roles). Self-check `r61`. Tested: HR creates "Summer 2027" open to all → employee joins 2–6 Aug from My leave → manager approves → approved + 5 board entries; Assign to all → 61 approved (the joiner skipped); Undo → 0 assigned, the join kept. **Security review:** all period writes under hr_can(); join only for the person's own card, target and open status re-checked server-side, dates bounded to the period; manager-final path reuses the contractor branch; assistant input goes through the existing ai_run gates (rate, cap, page allow-list) and the summary is data, never instructions. **Performance:** the matrix is two queries per page; the summary for the assistant computes balances for the people shown only.

## 145. v1.73.0 — Payroll record on the card, company closure, Training, annual evaluations (GAS-CP-R62-001)
Migration `migrate_cp_r62_training_eval.sql` (after R61-001): `hr_people` + father_name, mother_name, spouse_name, nationality, birth_place, passport_no, blood_group, education, experience_level, transport, phone_work, extension, postcode, city, country, gross_salary DECIMAL(10,2), oaed_benefit, payroll_code; `hr_leave_periods.mandatory`; `hr_trainings` (kind introductory / internal / external, title, provider, trainer, location, date_from / to, hours, description, target all / disciplines / people + target_ids, capacity, deadline, cost_eur, certificate, status open / closed / done / cancelled); `hr_training_members` (training, person, status applied / approved / rejected / attended / absent, applied_at, decided_by / at, note, score, cert_no, cert_issued, cert_expiry, cert_doc_id → hr_docs); `hr_evaluations` (person, year unique, eval_date, evaluator, scores JSON, overall, strengths, improvements, goals, training_needs, comments, employee_comment, status draft / final / acknowledged). Check `r62`.
Code: `app/hr6.php` (new — trainings, members, applications, queue, certificates alerts, evaluations, evolution, participation), `app/hr2.php` (HR_PERSONAL + 18 payroll fields, secrets incl. passport and gross salary, `hr8_has()`, import template / parse / apply — invalid personal values skipped with a warning, new-person id taken right after the insert), `app/hr4.php` (audit masks), `app/hr3.php` (doc kinds training / evaluation), `app/hr5.php` (mandatory periods), `views/hr_training.php` (new), `views/hr_eval.php` + `views/_hr_eval.php` (new — manager page and shared panel), `views/hr_person.php` (tabs 🎓 Training / 📈 Evaluations, payroll inputs), `views/my_leave.php` (Training opportunities / My trainings / My evaluations, mandatory periods), `views/hr_requests.php` (Training applications queue, Evaluations of my people), `views/hr_leaves.php` (mandatory checkbox / pill), `views/hr_dashboard.php` (certificates expiring), `_layout.php` nav, `auth.php` HR_PAGES / HR_ACTIONS, `hr2.php` EMP_ACTIONS, `pm_actions.php` (hr_tr_save / delete / add, hr_trm_save, hr_tr_apply, hr_ev_save, hr_ev_ack), 143 language keys × 5.
Flows: HR publishes (open → notification `hrt:new` to the people invited) → employee applies (My leave, `hrt:applied` to HR + manager) → manager (Work › HR requests) or HR approves / rejects (`hrt:approved` / `hrt:rejected`) → HR records attendance, result, certificate (file via `hr_docs_store_from_files(kind training)`, linked by `cert_doc_id`; download `?p=hr_pdoc`) → card › Training and HR dashboard alerts (≤ 90 days). Evaluation: HR (card) or manager (`?p=hr_eval&pid=`) saves draft / final (`hrev:final` to the employee) → employee acknowledges with a comment (My leave) → audit `evaluation`. Mandatory period: `hr_period_save` flag → Assign to all → My leave shows 🔒 Mandatory, no join form.
Workbook `GAS-HR-personnel-import.xlsx` built from the payroll export (`/home/claude/hrimp/build_personnel.py`): sheet People (64 matched), sheet "Check names first" (12 names not in the known list — correct the Latin name to the portal card before importing), Notes. Known data issues in the export: 10-digit AMKA for a few people (skipped by the import with a warning).
Security review of this revision: new pages gated by `hr_can()` / manager check (`hr_manager_of`), employee actions whitelisted in EMP_ACTIONS, HR pages / actions in HR_PAGES / HR_ACTIONS; certificate upload goes through the existing card-document store (extension whitelist, random file name, streamed with the extension we set — never sniffed); evaluation scores clamped 1–5, texts length-capped, JSON encoded server side; secrets masked in audit; per-row forms use the HTML `form=` attribute (no nested forms). No new endpoints without CSRF; no performance impact (queries indexed by training_id / person_id / year).

## 146. v1.73.1 — HR group in the left menu (GAS-CP-R62-002)

The HR-only and employee-only flat menus are unchanged. Manual references updated to "HR › HR requests".

## 147. v1.73.2 — Weekends and holidays never charged (GAS-CP-R62-003)
No SQL. Cause of the 14-day charge: `wc_day()` returned *workday* for any date when the calendar had no weekday rules (`work_hours` empty for `GR`) and `wc_holiday()` only knew years somebody generated. Fix in `app/wc.php`: no rule → `workday = dow < 6`; no stored holidays for the year → `wc_standard(calendar country, year)` (Orthodox Easter for GR). `hr_working_days()`, `hr_period_apply()`, `hr_period_join()`, `hr_request_to_board()` and the Planning range all go through `wc_day`, so requests, board entries and balances follow. `hr_period_recount($id)` (hr5.php, action `hr_period_recount`, button 🔄 Recount days on applied periods, HR_ACTIONS) recomputes `hr_requests.days` and rewrites the board entries of a period. Manual updated.

## 148. v1.73.3 — Celebrations: birthdays and name days (GAS-CP-R62-004)
No SQL. `app/cel.php` (new): `cel_table()` — ~60 feasts with Greek and Latin first-name variants, fixed `MM-DD` or Easter-relative rules (`E±n`, `george`, `mark`, `xmas-sun`); `cel_nameday($p, $year)` matches the first name of the Greek name (accents stripped, hyphen → first part) or the Latin first name; `cel_upcoming($days, $withAge)` merges birthdays (`hr_people.birth_date`, 29 Feb → 28 Feb) and name days of active people across a year boundary; `cel_card()` renders the dashboard card. `views/dashboard.php`: `cel_card(14, false, false)` for staff (no age, no card links — birth dates stay HR-only); `views/hr_dashboard.php`: 30 days with age and card links. Keys `cel.*` ×5. Security: no new input; ages shown to HR / admin only.

No SQL. `app/act.php` (new): `act_fleet($from,$to)` — distinct vessel ids touched via projects (created in period or active), tickets, timesheet lines (sub-project → project → vessel), plan_entries, offers, production_orders, fsr_reports (via project), acc_reports; `act_new_vessels($year)` — vessels whose first sighting (vessels.created_at, first project, first ticket) is in the year and that are in this year's fleet; `act_sources()` — 21 per-user count queries (bulk sources plan_entries / equipment / equipment_history count distinct days so an import does not dominate); `act_top($from,$to,10)` restricted to active staff / admin accounts, with timesheet hours; `act_card()` renders KPIs + two top-10 lists (medals, gold bar relative to the leader, breakdown as title). `views/dashboard.php` shows it to staff above Celebrations. Keys `act.*` ×5. Every query is wrapped in try/catch so an optional table missing on an installation cannot break the dashboard; results are cached per request.

Migration `migrate_cp_r63_social.sql` (after R62-001): `soc_wishes` (to, from, kind birthday/nameday/anniversary/welcome, on_date, emoji, message; unique per to/from/kind/date), `soc_kudos`, `soc_posts` (kind post/poll/photo, title, body, stored_name, pinned_until, closes_at) + `soc_post_options`, `soc_post_votes` (one per user), `soc_post_reactions` (one emoji per user), `soc_post_comments`, `soc_badges`, `soc_winners` (month PK), `soc_onboard` (user PK, buddy, done CSV, finished_at); settings `soc_prize`, `soc_cel_day`, `soc_digest_week`. Security review: all actions behind `require_staff()` + CSRF; targets validated against active staff accounts; self-wish / self-kudos refused; rate limit on kudos; posts / polls limited to the roles above, photos limited by extension + MIME + size and never served by sniffing; deletion by author, HR or admin only; winner / onboarding start by HR / admin only; onboarding ticks by the person, the buddy, HR or admin; all texts length-capped and escaped on output; no amounts or personal data (ages, birth dates) leave the HR scope. Performance: the dashboard block adds ~10 small indexed queries; `soc_tick()` runs its jobs once per day / week.

Check `r64`.

## 155. v1.75.2 — People template gated like the card (GAS-CP-R64-003)
No SQL. `hr_people_template()` drops the `HR_PERSONAL` tail of the header and of every row when `!hr_personal_can()` (22 general columns stay); `views/admin_hr.php` import preview shows `hr.imp_personal_skip` when `$r['personal']` rows exist and the account cannot write them (the apply path already skips them since R64-001). Key ×5.

## 156. v1.76.2 — People import: absent columns keep the card's values (GAS-CP-R64-004)
No SQL. Cause of the erased prior service on 31 Oct: `hr_people_import_parse()` produced 0 / 5 / 0 / 'indefinite' for prior months, week days, children, technical flag and contract type when the column was missing, and `hr_people_import_apply()` wrote them with plain `= ?`; the personal loop stored `null` for every absent personal column (`$pv !== ''` is true for null) so `hr_personal_save()` wiped the personal fields as well. Fix: parse returns `null` for absent / empty cells of those fields and skips absent personal columns; the UPDATEs use `COALESCE(?, column)` (hr_people and the open employment period); inserts fall back to the defaults. Preview shows `=` (hr.imp_keep) for prior service when kept. `GAS-HR-restore-import.xlsx` (62 rows: Technical, Working days, Children, birth years, Prior service) rebuilt from `GAS-HR-people-import.xlsx` (29 Oct) + `GAS-HR-prior-service-import.xlsx` (30 Oct) to put the values back.

## 157. v1.76.3 — Delete a portal account without history (GAS-CP-R64-005)
No SQL. `app/hr7.php`: `cu_account_refs($uid)` counts references in 21 tables (tickets, comments, timesheets, planning, offers, reports, production, employee card, HR requests and approvals, managed people / disciplines, portal requests, Q&A, equipment, accounting, community, kudos, successful sign-ins); `cu_account_delete($uid)` — admin only, never self, refuses with the list when anything refers to the account, otherwise removes notifications / devices / tokens / login log / AI log / targets / entitlements rows and the account, and logs `deleted:<email>` in login_log. Action `cu_delete`; button rendered only for deactivated accounts with no references (`views/admin_users.php`, confirm dialog). Keys `au.delete_*` ×5.

## 158. v1.76.4 — Hour types offset annual leave in whole days (GAS-CP-R64-006)
