## 160. v1.79.2 — Diagnostics on Settings › About (GAS-CP-R69-001)
No SQL. `app/diag.php`: `diag_run()` — every check in its own try/catch and time-bounded (CHECK TABLE QUICK on 16 core tables, processlist / innodb_trx / data_lock_waits, information_schema sizes, 14 orphan queries, duplicate detection with `disc_norm`, backlog counters, timings of act_top / hr_people / hr_balance ×10 / cel_upcoming, PHP ini + extensions, storage dirs, disk, sessions, backups, update.lock flock probe, config, assistant HEAD with 6 s timeout, error-log tail); `diag_text()` report; `diag_errlog_clear()`. `index.php` sets `log_errors` + `error_log = storage/php-errors.log` when storage is writable. `views/admin_about.php`: card #diag with Run / Run again / Download (`&diag=1`, `&export=diag`), grouped table, Clear-log button; action `diag_errlog_clear`. Admin only (About page gate). Keys `dg.*` ×5 (IT/BG/UK keep the English remedies — technical text for administrators).


## 161. v1.79.4 — Performance pass (GAS-CP-R69-002)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r69_perf.sql` (21 indexes, every statement tolerated when present; check `r69b`). `app/cache.php` (required right after db.php): `cache_get/set/forget/remember/flush/sweep` — APCu when enabled, else serialized files in `storage/cache` keyed `gascp:<CP_VERSION>:<key>` (a release invalidates everything); query meter `db_meter()` called from `q()` with the per-query time, `db_stats()`, `db_meter_finish($page)` appends to `storage/slow-pages.log` when a page exceeds 2 s or 150 queries (up to 5 statements over 300 ms), `db_slow_pages()`; `hk_tick()` once a day (cp_setting `hk_day`) purges read notifications > 90 d, login_log > 365 d, ai_log > 180 d, sent push items > 30 d, session files > 7 d, update backups beyond the last 3, trims slow-pages / php-errors / gate_debug logs, result in `hk_last`. Cached blocks: `nav_badges()` 45 s per user (forgotten on every POST of that user in index.php), `online_users()` 20 s, `online_detail()` 60 s, `act_card()` and `cel_card()` 10 min per locale, `kpi_cockpit()` 10 min. `cp_setting()` loads the whole settings table once per request (`cp_settings_all()`). Balance engine on GET requests: `hr_balance()` memo per person·type·year, `hr_employments()` memo, bulk loaders `hr_ent_row()` (all entitlements of the year), `hr_req_sum()` (one GROUP BY of the year's requests, counted / all, approved / pending, days / hours — a `counted_from` clause falls back to the direct query), `hr_board_days()`; POST requests always query directly. Measured in the sandbox (68 cards): Planning 8 024 q / 3.2 s → 416 q / 0.23 s, HR dashboard 8 566 q / 3.6 s → 415 q / 0.24 s, dashboard 321 → 132 q, page output byte-identical. `PRESENCE_MINUTES` 3 → 5, heartbeat in `assets/portal.js` 60 → 120 s. Footer for administrators: `N q · N ms` (title `perf.meter_tip`). Diagnostics rows Block cache / Daily house-keeping / Slowest pages; actions `diag_cache_flush`, `diag_slowlog_clear`. Keys `perf.meter_tip`, `dg.cache*`, `dg.housekeeping`, `dg.hk_never`, `dg.slow_*`, `dg.slowlog_*` ×5 (6147 each).

## 162. v1.79.6 — Community photo & CSP nonces (GAS-CP-R70-002)
No SQL. Root cause: `<script>` blocks without `nonce="<?= csp_nonce() ?>"` in `views/community.php` (compose kind switch), `views/hr_targets.php` (scope switch ×2, including the AI target form) and `views/hr_training.php` (target switch) — blocked by the CSP header (`script-src 'self' 'nonce-…'`), silently in production (curl-based sandbox tests do not execute JS; browser tests must be used for any inline script). Fix: nonce on all four. `soc_post_create()`: `$hasFile` — a photo is validated and stored for any kind when uploaded, mandatory only for `photo`; `UPLOAD_ERR_INI_SIZE` → `soc.photo_too_big`. `soc_photo_stream()` no longer filters `kind = "photo"`; the feed shows the image whenever `stored_name` is set. Compose: photo field always visible with "(optional)" label hidden for the photo kind, `required` toggled by the kind. Keys `soc.photo_optional`, `soc.photo_too_big` ×5 (6149). Rule for the future: grep `'<script>'` without nonce before every release.

## 163. v1.79.7 — @mentions on the Community (GAS-CP-R70-003)
No SQL. `views/community.php`: comment field is now `<textarea data-mt rows="1">` (the R31-007 enhancer in `assets/portal.js` attaches the picker to every `textarea[data-mt]`, fed by `<html data-mt-users>`); comment bodies and post bodies rendered with `mt_html()` (rich text + highlighted handles; `white-space:pre-wrap` removed since rt_html emits <br>/<p>). `soc_post_comment()`: `mt_parse()` → `mt_notify($b, 'Community · <title>', url#post, 'soc:<comment id>')` for the mentioned colleagues; the author's `soc:comment` notice skips people already mentioned. Staff manual Community paragraph updated.

## 164. v1.79.8 — World clocks per user + HR requests fatal (GAS-CP-R70-004)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r70_clocks.sql` (`customer_users.clocks VARCHAR(120) NULL`, check `r70d`). `app/clk.php`: `CLK_MAX = 7`, `clk_catalogue()` (34 codes → tz, city, lat, lon — the city feeds the Open-Meteo pop-over), `clk_default()` = the former fixed set, `clk_user_codes()`, `clk_strip()` (UTC first, always), `clk_save_from_post()` (action `clocks_save` in index.php, empty = NULL = default). `_layout.php` builds the strip from `clk_strip()`; the small city label now shows for every code outside the six capital-obvious ones. `views/account.php`: card #clocks with checkbox grid, live counter, the rest disabled at the maximum (nonce'd inline script), Save / Back to the company set. **Fatal fixed:** `views/hr_requests.php:69` called `hr_training_queue()` and `hr_team_of()` which never existed (R62-001 oversight) — every HR-requests page with the training module on died after the main content, so `portal.js` never loaded (clocks `--:--`, no @ picker, no row locks); now in `app/hr6.php`: `hr_team_of($uid)` (people whose `hr_manager_of()` is the user) and `hr_training_queue()` (applied members; all for HR, own team for managers). Lesson: a PHP fatal after the main content is invisible at a glance — Diagnostics › error log and the footer meter show it; the sandbox smoke test now greps `buildtag` on every page. Keys `clk.my_title`, `clk.my_hint`, `clk.utc_fixed`, `clk.reset`, `clk.saved`, `clk.too_many` ×5 (6155).
**Second fatal fixed (same release):** `kpi_snapshot_monthly()` reused `$to` for the notification recipients, so from the second project with a red drop `kpi_project($pid, $from, $to)` received an array → TypeError, dashboard dead for the first visitor of the month, and the `COUNT(*) > 0` guard then skipped the remaining projects for the whole month. Now `$rcpt`, per-project resume (`SELECT DISTINCT project_id … snap_date = ?`) and `cp_setting kpi_snap_done = <snap_date>` as the completion flag. Verified in the sandbox: 1 → 5 projects snapshotted on the next page.

Staff apps (`mobile/`, `mobile-pm/`): new route `#/lock` → `SCREENS.lock()` (logo, Unlock, Sign out instead; the prompt opens by itself except in demo mode); `UI.isLocked()` reads `<pfx>.locked` (gas./gpm.), `UI.biometricAvailable()` (Capacitor BiometricAuth or WebAuthn platform authenticator; true in demo); router sends a signed-in user to `#/lock` while the flag is set; start-up sets the flag when the "bio on open" setting is on (PM previously called `API.logout()` on a cancelled prompt — removed); More › 🔒 Lock app sets the flag (or signs out with `lock.need_bio` when the device has no biometrics); the Service app gets the `gas.bio` segmented setting PM already had. Tested headless in demo mode: lock → reload stays locked → unlock → home, no console errors. Next: passkeys (R71) with security review.

## 166. v1.80.0 — Passkeys (GAS-CP-R71-001)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r71_passkeys.sql` (`webauthn_credentials`, `webauthn_challenges`, login_log outcomes `passkey`, `passkey_failed`, `passkey_added`, `passkey_removed`; check `r71`). `app/pk.php` (required in index.php and in the API bootstrap): CBOR decoder `pk_cbor`, `pk_cose_to_pem` (ES256 / RS256 → SubjectPublicKeyInfo PEM), `pk_parse_auth_data`, `pk_check_client_data` (type, challenge, origin ∈ `pk_origins()`), challenges `pk_challenge_new/take` (session for the web, table for the API, 5 min, single use), `pk_registration_options` / `pk_register_finish` (UP+UV, rpIdHash, exclude list, ≤ 10 per account, notification), `pk_login_options` / `pk_assert_finish` (openssl_verify over authData‖sha256(clientDataJSON), counter regression → `pk_fail` → `sec_notify_locked`), `pk_list/remove`, `pk_stepup_ok` (fresh TOTP for enrolment), web endpoint `?p=pk` (`pk_endpoint`: login_options / login unauthenticated with `pk_rate_ok`; reg_options / register with X-CSRF), settings `sec_passkeys` (1), `sec_passkey_rpid`, `sec_passkey_origins`. `assets/passkey.js`: login page button + conditional mediation (`autocomplete="username webauthn"`), My Account enrolment. Views: `login.php` (button + script, only when `pk_enabled()`), `account.php` card #passkeys, `admin_log.php` policy checkbox `passkeys` + card #passkeys (`pk_admin_remove`); actions `pk_remove`, `pk_admin_remove`, `sec_save` stores `sec_passkeys`. API: `/auth/passkey/options`, `/auth/passkey/login` (role check per app, device registration, tokens), `/me/passkeys` GET/POST/DELETE, `/me/passkeys/options` (also reachable from the customer app). Apps: `pkB64` + `pkToJSON` + `passkeyCapable/passkeyLogin/passkeyRegister` in each api.js; login screen `#pkin` / `#btn-pk`; More / Account › Passkeys list + Add this device (code row appears on `totp` error). Versions Service 0.6.4, PM 1.0.2, Customer 1.0.2. **Testing passkeys needs a browser**: Playwright CDP `WebAuthn.addVirtualAuthenticator` on `http://localhost:8094` (an IP is not a valid rpId — use `localhost`, which `pk_origins()` accepts only when it is the request host). Side fix: `portal_pm.php` alias `system` back-quoted (reserved in MySQL 8; MariaDB on live tolerated it). Security review: `docs/SECURITY-REVIEW_R71-001.md`. Keys `pk.*` ×5 (6187). Open: native shells need a FIDO2 plug-in + associated domains (R71-003).

## 167. v1.80.1 — Passkey button vs. conditional request (GAS-CP-R71-002)
No SQL. `assets/passkey.js`: the conditional-mediation `navigator.credentials.get()` (autofill offer) is given an `AbortController`; the button click aborts it before its own `get()`. Chrome/Edge allow one WebAuthn request at a time — the second one rejected with NotAllowedError "A request is already pending". Verified headless: a pending autofill request + button → the real prompt ("Cancelled." with an empty virtual authenticator). The native-plug-in item keeps the next free ref (R71-003).

## 168. v1.80.2 — Native passkeys in the store builds, shells on Capacitor 8 (GAS-CP-R71-003)
No SQL. `mobile-native/package.json`: Capacitor 8.6.0 (+ plug-ins 8.x, @capacitor/assets 4, @capgo/capacitor-passkey 8.5.8 — the plug-in exists for Capacitor 8 only), `overrides` uuid / minimatch / glob → `npm audit` 0 (was 4 critical + 9 high + 4 moderate, mostly Capacitor 6 itself); workflows Node 22, Java 21; `prepare.js` adds `plugins.CapacitorPasskey { origin: https://goldensas.com, domains: [goldensas.com], autoShim: true }`; `wellknown.js <TEAM_ID> <SHA256,…>` writes the association files; build-android prints the upload-key SHA-256. Clients: `pkNative()` = `Capacitor.Plugins.CapacitorPasskey` on a native platform (the plug-in's JS shim is not loaded in a server.url WebView, so the clients call `createCredential({ publicKey: optionsJSON, origin })` / `getCredential(...)` directly — the plug-in takes and returns the same base64url JSON the API already speaks, no decoding); web path unchanged. Portal: `pk_origins()` adds `android:apk-key-hash:<b64url(sha256)>` for every fingerprint in `sec_passkey_android_sha256` (Settings › Login history field, action `sec_save`); iOS 17.4+ reports the https origin itself. `diag.php` duplicates: `WHERE merged_into IS NULL`, remedy text → Settings › Vessel duplicates (`admin_vessel_merge`, R30-001). Keys `pk.android_sha*` ×5, `dg.fix_duplicates` reworded (6189). Not testable headless (needs a device); contract verified against the plug-in's definitions.d.ts 8.5.8.

## 169. v1.80.3 — Passkey button inside the login card (GAS-CP-R71-004)
No SQL. `views/login.php`: the `.pk-login` block (divider, button, message, script) moved inside `form.gate-card` (the form *is* the white card); `btn--ghost` = navy text, readable only on the card. **Rule (Nikos):** every new element is checked against the palette and the stored styles on the background it actually sits on — screenshot the page (light and dark theme where the page has one) before release; the login page is always the light card on the navy gate.

## 170. v1.80.4 — Android SHA-256 field vs. Sucuri (GAS-CP-R71-005)
No SQL. Sucuri virtual patching blocked `POST p=admin_log` with the `AA:BB:…` fingerprints (block reason "Exploit attempt denied by virtual patching"). `views/admin_log.php`: visible input `#pk-sha-view` (no name) + hidden `passkey_android_sha256_enc`; a nonce'd submit handler strips everything but hex and commas and base64-encodes it. `sec_save`: decodes, splits on commas, keeps 64-hex values, stores upper-case colon-separated. Rule: never POST long hex / colon-hex strings in clear through the WAF — encode them.

Clients: `n.createCredential({ requestJson: JSON.stringify(options), origin })`, `n.getCredential({ requestJson: JSON.stringify({ ...options, allowCredentials: [] }), origin })`; the native result is already the WebAuthn JSON shape the API verifies. ## 183. v1.81.0 — Weekly notes: edit / delete in the portal, photo assessment (GAS-CP-R73-001)

## 194. v1.84.2 — Upload limits (GAS-CP-R76-003)
No SQL. Cause of the "crash above ~17 files": PHP's defaults (`max_file_uploads` 20, `post_max_size` 8 M) — a request over `post_max_size` arrives with empty `$_POST`/`$_FILES`, so `csrf_check()` bounced it as "session expired"; files beyond `max_file_uploads` were dropped silently. `customer/.user.ini` (new; manifest filter now ships `.ini`): upload 20 M, post 256 M, 60 files, input/exec time 300/120 s. `helpers.php`: `up_bytes()`, `up_limits()` (file/total/files from ini), `up_mb()`, `up_overflow_check()` (CONTENT_LENGTH > post_max_size with empty POST → flash `up.post_big`, redirect back) called in `index.php` before `csrf_check()`. `hr3.php`: per-file `UPLOAD_ERR_*` reported (`up.file_big`, `up.file_err`), `up.truncated` warning when count ≥ max_file_uploads. `_layout.php` body `data-up-file/total/files/msg`; `assets/portal.js` checks every `input[type=file]` on change (count, total, per file) → `.up-err` hint, submit buttons disabled. `hr_person.php` hints `up.hint`. `diag.php` PHP row adds max_file_uploads. Keys `up.*` ×5 (6385). Manuals: HR + Golden documents paragraphs. Note: `.user.ini` is honoured by cPanel PHP-FPM/LSAPI within 5 min; Settings › Diagnostics shows the effective values.

## 200. v1.86.3 — Refresh-token race in the apps (GAS-CP-R77-004)
No SQL. Symptom: sync chip "Refresh token invalid (POST /auth/refresh)" then "Too many requests". Cause: `pull()` fires 13 calls in parallel; when the access token had expired every call got 401 and each started its own `/auth/refresh` with the same refresh token — the first rotated it, the second hit `consumeRefresh()`'s reuse detection, which revoked every token of the device; the rest then failed and the auth rate limit (10 / IP) kicked in. Fix: `api.js` of Service / PM / HR and `lib/api.js` of Customer — `refreshOnce()` single-flight promise shared by concurrent 401s (clears tokens on 401 / 403 so the app returns to the login screen); `api/v1/lib/jwt.php` `consumeRefresh()`: a token rotated less than 60 s ago (TIMESTAMPDIFF in SQL — PHP `time()` vs MySQL `NOW()` time zones differ) answers 401 "Refresh token already rotated" without revoking the device; older replays still revoke. Test: three refreshes with one token → 200 + 2 × 401 already rotated; the winner's token refreshes fine. Devices already revoked must sign in again once.

Tested: admin without HR role → 403 on hr_dashboard / admin_hr / hr_person, 200 on settings and my_leave; CEO, Owner and HR → 200. The HR app (`PortalHR::allowed`) inherits the CEO rule.

No SQL. **R77-008** `hr2.php` `hr_missing_docs($personId)` (doc_path null, status open / approved, `doc_due` set or type `needs_doc`, last 12 months). `my_leave.php` flash `#hr-missing` (bad when overdue) + row pill / upload also for needs_doc types; `hr_person.php` Requests tab pill, reason label + due pill per row; API `/hr` → `missing_docs[]`, requests `doc_missing`; Service app 0.6.16 `hr.js` note (tap → requests tab), `screens.js` home chip; app i18n `hr.missing_docs`, `hr.missing_docs_hint`, `hr.doc_required_s` ×5; portal keys ×5. Reason labels come from the API in the app's `X-Lang` (`hr.rs_*` in all five languages). **R77-009** `pm.php` `cp_thumb_path()` — GD, 480 px JPEG q78 in `storage/thumbs/<id>.jpg`, regenerated when the original is newer, EXIF orientation kept, null → original streamed; `cp_stream_file()` honours `&thumb=1` for images. `isr_edit.php`: `<details>` closed for stored systems (open for a new one), summary shows 📷 count + risk pill, index strip `#isr-index` (one open at a time, expand / collapse all, MutationObserver keeps it in sync), `<img loading="lazy" decoding="async">` thumbnails wrapped in a link to the original; thumbnails also in `isr_view.php`, `fsr_edit.php`, `fsr_view.php`. Keys `isr.systems_fold_hint`, `isr.expand_all`, `isr.collapse_all` ×5 (6512).

## 205. v1.87.2 — Big reports: document build budget, cached photo copies, rebuild (GAS-CP-R77-010)
No SQL. Cause of the "issue crashed, no PDF": `isr_generate_documents()` read every original phone photo (3–8 MB each) twice — GD-decoded for the PDF (≈ 50 MB RAM per 12-MP image) and embedded as-is in the DOCX — and the status was already `issued` when PHP hit the 120 s / 256 M limits. Fix: `pm.php` `cp_photo_bytes($id)` → `cp_thumb_path($f, $path, 1200)` (`storage/thumbs/<id>-1200.jpg`, 900 kB skip threshold), `cp_doc_budget()` (`set_time_limit(300)`, `memory_limit` ≥ 512M, `ignore_user_abort`). `isr.php` PDF + DOCX photos via `cp_photo_bytes` (PdfWriter then takes the fast JPEG path, no GD), budget, PDF stored and recorded first, DOCX in try / catch (error_log). `fsr.php` same photo path + budget. Action `isr_regen` (staff, issued) + button in `isr_view.php` when pdf or docx is missing. Load test: 17 systems × 4 × 12-MP photos (68 files, 42 MB) → issue 17 s first time (thumbnail cache built), 0.7 s rebuild; PDF 7.7 MB / 19 pages, DOCX 6.2 MB. Keys `isr.regen*` ×5 (6516). For the live report with no PDF: open it and press Rebuild.

## 216. v1.88.3 — Project tree performance + error-log warnings (GAS-CP-R77-021)
No SQL. Profiled with a temporary query log hooked into `db_meter()` (env `CP_QLOG`, removed again): the sandbox tree (7 projects / 7 subs) ran 575 queries; the live tree ~9 100. Causes: `eng_alerts()` → `eng_sub_state()` → `eng_rows()` per sub, then `pd_sub_fed()` → `eng_stage_pcts()` → `eng_sub_pct()` → `eng_rows()` once more **per stage** (6 queries each), `fs_pills()` → `fs_totals()` per project (3 heavy queries), `of_project_summary()` + `of_by_sub()` both reading the offers of the project, `bg_current()` per sub several times. Fixes: `eng_rows()` memoised per request in `$GLOBALS['_eng_rows']` (`eng_rows_build()` does the work; `eng_rows_reset()` drops the memo and bumps the `eng` cache namespace); `eng_alerts()` = `cache_remember('eng_alerts:'.cache_ver('eng').':'.$pid, 180, …)` over `eng_alerts_build()`; `fs_totals()` = `cache_remember('fs:'.cache_ver('fs').':'.$pid.':'.$sid, 180, …)` over `fs_totals_build()`; `bg_current()` memoised in `$GLOBALS['_bg_cur']` (`bg_current_build()`); `views/projects.php` groups `$pof['rows']` by sub-project instead of calling `of_by_sub()`. New in `cache.php`: `cache_ver($ns)` (namespace version, memoised per request) · `cache_bump($ns)` · `cache_bump_later($ns)` (registers one shutdown function per namespace, so the bump lands after the write and its redirect). Every write path starts with the bump: `eng_budget_save_from_post`, `eng_mapping_save_from_post`, `eng_deliverable_save_from_post`, `eng_deliverable_toggle`, `eng_request_save_from_post`, `eng_request_decide`, `ts_save_from_post`, `ts_submit`, `ts_decide`, `ts_withdraw`, `ts_delete`, `bg_save_from_post` → `eng`; `pi_save_from_post`, `pi_issue`, `pi_set_status`, `pi_mark_sent`, `pi_delete`, `pinv_save_from_post`, `pinv_delete`, `ppay_save_from_post`, `ppay_delete`, `of_save_from_post`, `of_set_status`, `of_decide`, `of_delete` → `fs`. `api/v1/bootstrap.php` now loads `cache` too (the apps go through the same functions). Both caches are gated behind `is_staff()` / `fin_visible()` *before* the lookup and hold staff-wide figures only, so nothing per-user leaks; the files live under `storage/cache` (`Require all denied`). Result in the sandbox: 575 → 321 queries, 0.25 s → 0.14 s; the remaining per-sub cost is one timesheet sum and one stage read, linear. Warnings: `nf.php` `nf_src_isr_events` used `$r['created_at']` but `isr_events` keeps its stamp in `at` → `($r['created_at'] ?? $r['at'] ?? null)`; `views/admin_offer_statuses.php` `$behaves[$d['behaves']]` had no 'draft' entry (the built-in draft row) → `?? $d['behaves']`. `docx.php` memory exhaustion is the pre-1.87.2 issue reports (`cp_doc_budget()` / 1200-px copies, §208–209); `soc.php` line 134 was fixed in 1.87.9. Rule kept: a staff-wide cached value must be computed **after** the visibility gate, never before it.

## 217. v1.89.0 — Accounting › Collections + portal accounts for management only (GAS-CP-R78-001)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r78_collections.sql` (table `pi_followups`: proforma_id, next_at, promise_at, note 300, user_id/user_name, created_at; state key `r78`, selfcheck line). New module `app/col.php` (loaded by `index.php` and the API bootstrap): `col_has()`, `cp_role_name()` (role name of the signed-in account), **`mgmt_can()`** = `hr_owner()` (Owner scope or CEO) or role named "General Manager" / "Managing Director", **`require_mgmt()`**, `col_flag()` (customer_users flags), **`col_can()`** = staff ∧ (mgmt ∨ `is_accounting` ∨ `can_sales`) — plain administrators are *not* included; `col_money()` (never masked: the gate is `col_can`, not `fin_visible`); `COL_SELECT` (proforma + offer + project + vessel + customer + PM + paid_sum + last payment + invoice numbers + the latest follow-up by correlated `LIMIT 1`); `col_where()` (vessel/project/customer/pm/q + `scope_sql('p')`); `col_open($f)` → open proformas (`issued/sent/partly_paid`, open > 0) with `age` (days past due, or past issue+30 when no due), `bucket` (`current/d30/d60/d90/d90p`), `expected` (promise ▸ due ▸ issue+30), `promise_missed`, `fu_due`; the `bucket` filter also accepts `overdue` and `followup`; `col_paid($from,$to,$f)` (payment register); `col_summary($open, 30, $f)` → outstanding / overdue / expected (inside the window) / late (expected date already past) / `expected_total` = both / promised / missed / fu_due / collected last 30 d / buckets / `weeks[]` (7-day slots); `col_projects($f)` → per project: approved offers (`NULLIF(gross_total,0)` fallback to amount×VAT, `converted_to IS NULL`) + proformas not draft/cancelled → invoiced, collected, outstanding, overdue (open ∧ due < today), to_invoice; `col_followups()`, `col_followup_save()` (dates validated by regex, note ≤ 300, scope-checked proforma, also `of_event($offer,'followup',…)` → key `of.ev_followup`); `col_xlsx()` (Summary · Open items · Projects · Collected period). View `views/collections.php` (`?p=collections[&vessel&project&customer&pm&bucket&q&from&to&format=xlsx]`): 4 KPI tiles, projection table (CSS bars `.col-bar`, late in red), aging table, filter bar, open items grouped by vessel › project (group rows `.col-group`, group subtotal `.col-gsum`, ✎ toggles the follow-up row `.col-fu` via `[data-toggle]`, history in `<details class="col-hist">`), financial status per project with 📄 link to `report_fin`, collected-in-period with From/To; print via `[data-print]`. Action `col_fu` in `pm_actions.php` (keeps the GET filters on redirect, `#col-open`). Nav: Commercial › 📥 Collections when `col_can()`. CSS block "R78-001" in `assets/portal.css` — includes **`.is-hidden { display:none !important }`, which was missing since R77-013** (the Σ breakdown rows on the HR person card were therefore always open; they now fold and the Σ button toggles them). **Accounts for management only:** `views/admin_users.php` `require_mgmt()`; actions `cu_update`, `cu_save`, `cu_revoke`, `cu_twofa_reset`, `cu_unlock`, `cu_toggle`, `cu_delete` (+ `cu_account_delete()` in hr7), `mob_revoke()` (auth.php), `cr_dev`, `tm_strict` → `require_mgmt()`; `settings_tab_groups()` shows the Portal accounts tab only to `mgmt_can()` (also to a non-admin Owner/CEO/GM, as a one-tab Organisation group); key `au.mgmt_only` under the lede. Keys `col.*` (47) + `th.from`, `th.to`, `btn.print`, `of.ev_followup`, `au.mgmt_only`, `col.due_in` — en/el/it/bg/uk at 6583. Tests: CEO (admin+CEO) and Owner see the page and the accounts tab; a Service Engineer gets 403 on both; the same account made a plain administrator still gets 403 on both and no Portal accounts tab; follow-up POST stores the row and the offer event; Excel export opens with four sheets. Security: the page and the action share one gate; filters are cast to int / regex-validated dates; the note is length-capped and escaped; branch scope applies through `scope_sql`; no amount reaches a role outside the five named.

No SQL. `app/col.php`: `col_statement_vessels()` (picker: vessels with proformas, grouped by `v.id`, customer from `v.customer_id`, open gross, count); `col_statement($vesselIds, $asOf, $from)` → per vessel every proforma not draft/cancelled issued ≤ as-of (≥ from when given) with its payments (≤ as-of) and invoices, `paid_to` (payment rows + paid invoices without a payment row), `open`, `age`, `overdue`, bucket; per-vessel and grand totals + aging buckets; `iv` (invoicing details of the first proforma → bank / IBAN block); `doc_no` = `<branch prefix>-SOA-<yyyymmdd>-<vessel|nV>`; `single`, `multi_customer`; `col_statement_pdf($D)` (PdfWriter, header/footer/need pagination like `ob_render_pdf`, FROM = `br_doc(project)` lines, TO = customer legal block + "Attn: Accounting department", intro, summary table when several vessels, per vessel: project group rows, 7 columns `$cx[]` x-offsets, overdue days under the due date in red, payment sub-lines in green with "» Payment received · method · reference · invoice", vessel total row, aging line, bank block, closing paragraph with the open balance, prepared-by line; texts in the customer's locale via `t_in`); `col_statement_sheets($D)` (Summary · Items · Payments) → `col_statement_xlsx()` (download) and `xlsx_bytes()` for the attachment; `col_em_attachment($p)` for key **`st:<ids>:<pdf|xlsx>:<asof>[:<from>]`** (gated by `col_can`). `app/em.php` `em_attachment()` case `st` → `col_em_attachment`. `app/et.php`: kind **`statement`** in `ET_KINDS` with built-ins `et.d_s_statement` / `et.d_b_statement` (templates per branch / customer / language in Settings › E-mail templates as for the others). View `views/statement.php` (`?p=statement&v[]=…&asof=&from=&format=pdf|xlsx[&dl]`, inline PDF by default). Collections page: card "Statement of account" above the open items — vessel checkboxes (`v[]`, preselected from the vessel filter), As of (`asof`), Items from (`sfrom`), Prepare (GET back to `#col-soa`), ⤓ PDF / ⤓ Excel, then the standard ✉ E-mail card (`em_card_html`, kind statement, people = vessel contacts + customer portal accounts + the customer's own e-mail as "Accounting department", attachments PDF (ticked) and Excel) → `em_draft` → .eml with the statement inside. Keys `soa.*` (23) + `et.k_statement`, `et.d_s_statement`, `et.d_b_statement`, `em.w_statement` (en/el/it/bg/uk = 6611). CSS `.soa-*`. Sandbox: single and two-vessel PDFs rendered (summary table on multi), Excel 3 sheets, .eml with both attachments (names `GIT-SOA-20261105-MYGY8502.pdf/.xlsx`). Security: the download route and the attachment builder both check `col_can()` and `scope_sql`; the vessel ids are cast to int; dates regex-validated; the attachment key carries nothing but ids and dates.

## 222. v1.90.0 — Procurement R79: inventory · suppliers · price history · pricing rules (GAS-CP-R79-001)
Implements release R79 of GAS-2026-PORTAL-PRC-001 R3. SQL `install/migrate_cp_r79_inventory.sql` (state key `r79`, selfcheck line): `inv_categories` (parent_id, code_prefix, sort, active), `inv_warehouses` (branch_id, code, name, is_default; seeds HQ), `inv_items` (code UNIQUE, description, description_en, category_id, unit, pack_qty, maker, factory_code, barcode, location, min_stock, status active/obsolete/blocked, currency, last_price, last_price_at, last_supplier_id, avg_cost, preferred_supplier_id, notes, file_id), `inv_stock` (item × warehouse: on_hand, reserved, location), `inv_moves` (kind in/out/adj/xfer_out/xfer_in/opening, signed qty, unit_price, project/sub-project, ref_kind/ref_id, reason, note, user), `inv_prices` (priced_at, supplier_id, po_line_id, currency, unit_price, qty, source receipt/erp/manual/rollup, n_rolled/min/max for the rollup line), `inv_price_rules` (scope category|supplier, ref_id, rule, n, pct), `supplier_details` (company_id PK: vat_no, address, postal_code, terms_days, advance_pct, currency, lead_days, incoterms, rating, remarks); `customer_users.can_procurement`, `can_warehouse` (plain ADD COLUMN — the sandbox is MySQL 8, no IF NOT EXISTS). **Prefixes:** `inv.*` lang keys and `inv_*` functions already belong to invoicing (`inv_has()` in pm.php) — the module uses **`stk.*` keys and `stk_*` functions** on `inv_*` tables. Module `app/inv.php` (loaded by index.php after col.php): gates `pr_can()` (procurement ∨ warehouse ∨ mgmt), `pr_admin()` (procurement ∨ mgmt), `pr_wh()` (+ warehouse), `stk_price_visible()` (pr_admin ∨ accounting flag ∨ fin_visible); `stk_categories/…_find/…_save/…_merge`, `stk_warehouses/…_save`, `stk_default_wh`; `STK_SELECT` + `stk_items($f)` (q over code/description/description_en/factory_code/barcode=/maker/location, cat incl. children, maker, status, stock any|instock|zero|below|reserved, slow n months, sort), `stk_item`, `stk_item_by_code`, `stk_item_save_from_post` (code regex `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 ._\-\/+]*$`, unique, new category by text, photo via cp_store_file kind photo); `stk_move($item,$wh,$kind,$qty,$o)` (upserts inv_stock, refuses negative unless allow_negative, writes inv_moves) and `stk_move_from_post` (in → price record when a price is typed; out → `sp_costs` kind **`parts`** at last price with ref_no INV + `eng_rows_reset()`; adj as delta or counted quantity with reason; xfer = out + in); `stk_price_add` (records, recomputes last_price / last_supplier / avg_cost over the last 10 weighted by qty, then `stk_price_rollup` keeps `STK_PRICE_KEEP` = 100 detail lines and merges the rest into one `rollup` row); pricing: `stk_rule_setting()` from cp_settings `proc_price_rule/_n/_pct`, `stk_rule_for(cat, supplier)` (supplier > category > company), `stk_price_proposal($item, $supplierId)` → price · origin text · n · age · rule · from for `last | mean2 | meann | wmeann | minn | same_supplier | last_plus`; settings `stk_settings_save_from_post` (+ `proc_disciplines` JSON, `proc_pay_threshold`), overrides save/delete; suppliers `sup_list/sup_load/sup_prices/sup_save_from_post` (companies kind supplier + supplier_details upsert); import `stk_import_map` (Greek / English headers), `stk_import_parse` (session preview: rows, warnings, map, counts; numbers with Greek thousands separators), `stk_import_apply($parsed, mode full|items|stock|prices, wh, priceDate)` in one transaction (opening move when stock was 0 in full mode, else stocktake adjustment; identical ERP price on the same date skipped); `stk_valuation`, `stk_export_xlsx`. Views `inventory` (tabs items / valuation / categories), `inventory_item` (KPIs, details, stock, movement form with kind-dependent fields, movements, price history with inline SVG chart, price record form), `inventory_import` (3 steps), `suppliers` (list + card + price list + iv_section bank block), `admin_procurement` (rule, disciplines, threshold, overrides, warehouses, rules explained). Actions `inv_item_save`, `stk_move`, `stk_price_add`, `inv_cat_save`, `inv_cat_merge`, `inv_wh_save`, `stk_import_parse`, `stk_import_apply`, `sup_save`, `proc_settings`, `inv_rule_save` (ref from ref_id_cat / ref_id_sup), `stk_rule_delete`. Nav: group **Procurement** (Inventory, Suppliers) before HR; Settings › Procurement for pr_admin; account flags 📦 Procurement / 🏬 Warehouse next to Cash flow. Keys `stk.*` (≈220) + `sup.*` + `nav.g_procurement` + `btn.open` + `bg.k_parts` (6846). CSS `.inv-list`, `.inv-move`, `.pr-disc`. Sandbox: the real Warehouse_codes.xlsx imported in 11 s — 3 830 items, 168 categories, 3 830 opening moves, 3 532 prices, value 819 815,76 € (equals the Excel); list 0.11 s, search 0.07 s; re-import → 0 new, 3 830 updated, 0 prices, 1 stocktake line; all seven rules checked on one code (last 14.20 · mean2 13.95 · mean3 14.33 · weighted 14.45 · lowest 13.70 · same supplier 14.20 · +5 % 14.91); engineer without flags 403, with Warehouse flag sees quantities only and no settings / import. Security: three gates on pages and POSTs; prices behind `stk_price_visible`; import rows validated and applied in a transaction; code regex; stock cannot go negative; `cache_bump_later('inv')` on every write for the R80 caches. Default warehouse name is ASCII ("Keratsini - head office") — the sandbox mysql client mangled the em dash.

## 223. v1.90.1 — Procurement assistant: replacements and market research with web search (GAS-CP-R79-002)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r79_002_research.sql` (state `r79b`, selfcheck): `inv_research` (item_id NULL for free queries, mode alternatives|prices, query, constraints, result MEDIUMTEXT JSON, summary, searches, cost_eur, ms, ok, user) and `inv_alternatives` (item_id, alt_item_id when the part number matches an inventory code / factory code, maker, part_no, description, match_kind exact|equivalent|functional, note, source_url, price, currency, seller, research_id, status proposed|approved|rejected, user). `app/inv.php`: `stk_research_has`, **`stk_ai_can()`** = pr_admin ∧ `ai_can('inventory_item')` (page added to `ai_all_pages()` in ai.php — enable it in Settings › Assistant), `stk_ai_system($mode)` (procurement persona; data tags `<part>` `<constraints>`; JSON schema per mode; never invent — every price / part with its URL; prefer EU distributors, ex-VAT EUR), **`stk_research_run($item, $mode, $query, $constraints)`**: rate / cap checks as ai_run, Messages API with `tools: [{type: ai_web_tool (default `web_search_20250305`), name: web_search, max_uses: ai_web_max (3–12)}]`, `ai_http($body, 170)` (new timeout parameter; action sets `set_time_limit(200)`), text blocks concatenated + citation URLs collected, searches from `usage.server_tool_use.web_search_requests`, cost = tokens + searches × `ai_price_search` (€, default 0.01; both new fields on Settings › Assistant and counted in the monthly cap), row in `ai_log` kind `parts`, row in `inv_research`; `stk_research_data`, `stk_alternative_add` (URL only if http(s), price numeric, alt_item_id looked up), `stk_alternative_status`, `stk_alternative_delete`. View `views/_stk_research.php` (form with mode buttons, kept alternatives table with ✓ / ✕ / 🗑, latest or `rs=` research: part line + status pill, summary, alternatives table with sources and "Keep as alternative" (hidden-field form carrying the cheapest source), or market table with "Record this price" → existing `stk_price_add` with note "Market research · seller · url"; earlier-research links) included full-width at the bottom of `inventory_item` and as tab `research` on `inventory` (free query → `item_id` NULL, no keep buttons). Actions `stk_research`, `stk_alt_add`, `stk_alt_status`. Keys `stk.ai_*`, `stk.alt*`, `stk.m_*`, `stk.as_*`, `stk.price_record`, `ai.price_search(_hint)`, `ai.web_max` (6881). Sandbox: the AI stub (`/tmp/aistub/index.php`) answers the procurement persona with a server_tool_use + web_search_tool_result + text(JSON, citations) content and `server_tool_use.web_search_requests = 4`; both modes ran, 0,06 € each logged, an alternative kept with its source, ai_log kind parts ×2. Security: model output is data — only http(s) URLs are linked (`rel="noopener noreferrer"`, new tab), everything escaped, JSON parsed with ai_json and sliced (≤ 8 alternatives, ≤ 4 sources, ≤ 10 market rows); research and keep actions need pr_admin + CSRF; cost capped by the assistant's monthly cap and per-user rate; nothing is ordered or written to prices without a click.

## 224. v1.91.0 — Procurement R80-001: purchase requisitions, parts-budget approval, stock reservation, sub-project Sales / Procurement tabs (GAS-CP-R80-001)
Implements the first step of release R80 of GAS-2026-PORTAL-PRC-001 R3. SQL `install/migrate_cp_r80_requisitions.sql` (state `r80`, selfcheck): `pr_requests` (pr_no UNIQUE, branch_id, project_id, subproject_id, offer_id / order_id / bom_rev_id reserved for R80-002/003, purpose project|stock|service, title, need_date, status draft|submitted|mgmt|approved|rejected|closed|cancelled, est_total, currency, budget snapshot budget_parts / budget_committed / budget_actual / over_budget, requester + discipline, submitted_at, approved_by/name/at/note (procurement), mgmt_by/name/at/note (management), note), `pr_lines` (seq, item_id NULL = free text, description, maker, part_no, unit, qty, qty_reserved, qty_ordered, qty_received, qty_issued, est_price, price_origin, need_date, note, status open|reserved|to_buy|ordered|received|issued|cancelled, warehouse_id), `pr_events` (event, user, ip, note). Module `app/prq.php`: `prq_can()` (procurement / warehouse / management, `is_cp_admin`, a PM of any project — `prq_is_pm()` — or `customer_users.discipline_id` ∈ `stk_enabled_disciplines()`), `prq_can_view()` (own · PM of the project · procurement / admin), `prq_can_edit()` (draft, requester or `pr_admin`), `prq_number()` = `num_stem(pid, sid)` + `PR.` + `num_unique('PR')` (`doc_sequences` family PR; `GEN.PR.n` without stem), `prq_line_estimate()` (`stk_price_proposal` → `last_price` → typed), `prq_recalc()`, `prq_budget($sid, $excludeReq)` (parts_budget from `bg_current`, committed = Σ (qty − qty_issued) × est_price of the un-issued lines of the other open requests, actual = Σ `sp_costs` kind parts, available), `prq_submit()` (needs lines; stores the snapshot, `over_budget` when purpose = project and no/zero budget or est > available; `nf_push` to `prq_proc_ids()` — active accounts with can_procurement), `prq_decide()` (`require_pr_admin`; reject needs a reason; over budget → status mgmt with mandatory reason, notify `prq_mgmt_ids()` = `hr_owner_ids()` ∪ General Manager / Managing Director roles; else `prq_approve_apply`), `prq_mgmt_decide()` (`require_mgmt`), `prq_approve_apply()` (per open line: reserve min(qty, free) in the warehouse with the most `qty_on_hand − qty_reserved`, `inv_stock.qty_reserved +=`, line reserved when fully covered else to_buy; status approved; notify requester + procurement), `prq_issue()` (`require_pr_wh`; qty ≤ qty_reserved; reservation released, `stk_move(out, ref_kind 'pr')`, `sp_costs` kind parts ref_no = PR no at last_price, `eng_rows_reset`; line issued when qty_issued ≥ qty; request closed when no line left open; bumps `inv` + `eng`), `prq_cancel()` (requester draft/submitted, pr_admin any open; releases reservations), `prq_sub_summary()`, `prq_item_search()` (JSON picker; price only when `stk_price_visible()`), `prq_counts()` (KPIs). Views: `requisitions.php` (KPIs awaiting procurement / management / approved / mine, filters status · mine · search, paged), `requisition.php` (header form with project → sub-project filter JS, budget KPIs, decision boxes per role, workflow steps, lines table with edit / delete while draft and the Issue box for the warehouse, item picker with `fetch('?p=prq_items&q=')` debounced 220 ms, log), `prq_items.php` (JSON, no layout, 403 without `prq_can`), `_sales_tab.php` (offers + proformas of the sub-project, KPIs won / invoiced / paid; staff with `fin_visible()`), `_procurement_tab.php` (budget line, requisitions, "+ New requisition" pre-filled; staff with `prq_can() || pr_can()`, tab badge = open requests). `index.php` requires `app/prq.php` after `inv.php`; `$views` += requisitions, requisition, prq_items. Nav: the Procurement group now appears for `pr_can() || prq_can()` with Requisitions first; inventory / suppliers only for `pr_can()`. Actions `prq_save`, `prq_line_save`, `prq_line_del`, `prq_submit`, `prq_decide`, `prq_mgmt`, `prq_issue`, `prq_cancel` (all CSRF, role-gated in the action and again in the function). Lang +175 keys ×5 (`prq.*`, `spsl.*` — `sl.*` was taken by Sales KPIs — `tab.sales`, `tab.procurement`, `nav.requisitions`). Manuals: Golden (requisitions section), staff (asking for parts), PM (Sales / Procurement tabs). Security review: picker limited to 15 rows and 60 chars, prices masked by `stk_price_visible()`; every state transition checks the current status server-side so a replayed form cannot approve twice or issue more than reserved; management ids are resolved from roles, never from the form; notes are length-capped and escaped; no new endpoints without login. Performance: `prq_counts()` is four COUNT queries on indexed status/requester columns; the sub-project tab adds one summary query; `bg_current` memo reused.

## 225. v1.92.0 — Procurement R80-002: engineering BOM revisions, Procurement change proposals, coverage, requisition from BOM (GAS-CP-R80-002)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r80_002_bom.sql` (state `r80b`, selfcheck): `bom_revisions` (subproject_id + revision UNIQUE, title, status draft|issued|approved|superseded|rejected|withdrawn, file_id, est_total, created/issued/approved by·name·at, approval_note, note), `bom_lines` (seq, item_id NULL when not in the inventory, code kept as typed, description, maker, part_no, unit, qty, est_price, price_origin, section, note, status ok|changed|removed), `bom_changes` (line_id NULL for kind add, kind alternative|qty|remove|add, alt_item_id / alt_description / alt_maker / alt_part_no, qty, reason, status proposed|accepted|rejected|withdrawn, proposed/decided by·name·at, decision_note), `bom_events`. Module `app/bom.php`: `bom_eng_can($sid)` (admin, `pm_is_pm`, project PM, `sp_team` member, enabled discipline), `bom_pr_can()` = `pr_admin()`, `bom_can_view()` (eng ∨ `pr_can` ∨ `fin_visible`), `bom_lines()` (free = on hand − reserved, cover full|partial|zero|none, to_buy), `bom_coverage()`, `bom_recalc()` (reuses `prq_line_estimate`), `bom_create_from_post()` (next revision number per sub-project, copy_from lines, optional upload), `bom_import()` (xlsx/csv ≤ 8 MB via `xlsx_rows`, header map EN/EL incl. ERP names, `stk_item_by_code` match, `stk_unit_norm`), `bom_line_save_from_post()` / `bom_line_delete()` (draft only), `bom_issue()` (needs lines; notifies `prq_proc_ids()`), `bom_withdraw()` (issued → draft, proposals withdrawn), `bom_change_from_post()` (`pr_admin`, issued only, reason mandatory, alternative/add need an item or description, qty/add need qty; notifies `bom_eng_ids()` = creator, issuer, PM, team), `bom_change_decide()` (engineering; accept applies: alternative → line item/code/description/maker/part_no/unit replaced, note "was <code>", status changed; qty; remove → status removed; add → new line "added by Procurement"; reject needs a reason; proposer notified), `bom_change_withdraw()`, `bom_approve()` (`pr_admin`; reject needs reason → status rejected, proposals withdrawn; approve refused while `n_pending` > 0; earlier approved revision of the sub-project → superseded; bumps `inv`), `bom_to_requisition()` (approved BOM, `prq_can`; `pr_requests.bom_rev_id`, one `pr_lines` per line with to_buy > 0, qty = to_buy, est_price only for free-text lines; `prq_recalc`), `bom_sub_summary()`, `bom_current()`. View `views/bom.php` (`?p=bom&id=`; `&format=xlsx` export via `xlsxw`; KPIs; Procurement review box with approve disabled while proposals pending; lines table with coverage pills, strike-through for removed, ✎ propose per line; draft line form + upload; proposal form with kind-driven fields and the inventory picker reusing `?p=prq_items`; change proposals table with Accept / Reject (engineering) or Withdraw (procurement); log). `_procurement_tab.php` gains the BOM block (list of revisions with status and pending count, "+ New BOM revision" form with copy-from and upload, for `bom_eng_can`); sub-project tab badge adds issued revisions. Actions `bom_create`, `bom_import`, `bom_line_save`, `bom_line_del`, `bom_issue`, `bom_withdraw`, `bom_change` (`require_pr_admin`), `bom_change_decide`, `bom_change_withdraw` (`require_pr_admin`), `bom_approve` (`require_pr_admin`), `bom_to_pr` (`require_prq`). `index.php` requires `app/bom.php` after `prq.php`; `$views` += bom. Lang +102 keys ×5 (`bom.*`). Manuals: Golden (BOM section), PM and staff paragraphs. Security: uploads parsed only through `xlsx_rows` (no file stored), size cap 8 MB, every transition checks the revision status and the role server-side (a proposal can only be decided while the revision is issued and only by engineering of that sub-project; approval impossible with pending proposals), reasons mandatory and capped, no new unauthenticated endpoint. Performance: one query per lines table with correlated stock sums (same shape as the inventory list), revisions list on the tab is one query; nothing added to the projects tree. Known limit: two BOM lines of the same code each see the same free stock (the coverage is per line, not netted across lines) — netting comes with the production kits of R82.

## 226. v1.93.0 — Procurement R80-003: RFQs and purchase orders per supplier (GAS-CP-R80-003)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r80_003_po.sql` (state `r80c`, selfcheck): `po_orders` (kind rfq|po, po_no UNIQUE, branch_id, supplier_id, project_id, subproject_id, rfq_id, title, status draft|sent|quoted|awarded|confirmed|partial|received|closed|cancelled, currency, incoterms, terms_days, advance_pct, delivery_date, warehouse_id, supplier_ref / contact / email, subtotal, vat_pct, vat_total, gross_total, note, internal_note, sent_at, sent_to, confirmed_at, created by), `po_lines` (seq, item_id, alt_of_item_id, description, maker, part_no, unit, qty, unit_price, discount_pct, line_total, price_origin, lead_days, need_date, pr_line_id, bom_line_id, qty_received, qty_accepted, status open|partial|received|cancelled, note), `po_events`. Module `app/po.php`: `po_can()` = `pr_can`, `po_admin()` = `pr_admin`, `po_number($kind, $branchId)` (`doc_sequences` family `PO:<prefix>:<yy>` / `RFQ:<prefix>:<yy>` with the LAST_INSERT_ID pattern → `<prefix>-PO-<yy>-<nnnn>`), `po_to_buy($f)` (approved `pr_lines` with qty − reserved − ordered > 0, suggested supplier preferred → last), `po_save_from_post()` (supplier defaults from `sup_load`, `pr_line[]` pulled via `po_line_from_pr` — price from `stk_price_proposal($item, $supplierId)` or the typed estimate of free-text lines), `po_line_save_from_post()` (inventory or free text, `alt_of_item_id`, price typed or proposed, discount, lead, need date), `po_recalc()` (line_total = qty × price × (1 − disc), subtotal, VAT, gross), `po_editable()` (draft; RFQ also while sent / quoted), `po_quote_from_post()` (price[] + lead[] per line → status quoted), `po_award()` (new PO from the RFQ lines, `rfq_id`, prices "quoted by supplier"; RFQ → awarded), `po_mark_sent($oid, $to)` (needs lines; PO needs prices on every line; first send: `pr_lines.qty_ordered += qty`, status ordered, `eng_rows_reset`; records `sent_to`), `po_confirm()`, `po_cancel()` (reason; requisition lines `qty_ordered −= qty − received`, back to to_buy when nothing ordered / received), `po_close()`, `po_people()` (order contact → supplier Contacts → company e-mail), `po_em_attachment()` (key `po:<id>`), `po_pdf()` (PdfWriter, branch letterhead via `br_doc` of the order's branch, from / to blocks, details box, lines with wrapped descriptions, totals, notes, footers `po.pdf_po_foot` / `po.pdf_rfq_foot`, signature lines), `po_sub_summary()`, `po_counts()`. `app/em.php`: attachment key `po`, `em_draft_from_post` calls `po_mark_sent` when the hidden `po_id` is posted. `app/et.php`: kind `po` with built-ins `et.d_s_po` / `et.d_b_po`. `app/prq.php` `prq_item_search`: `&alts=1` adds the approved `inv_alternatives` of each hit (`alts[]` with id / code / desc / maker / pn / unit / free / note). Views: `purchase_orders.php` (tabs POs · RFQs · To buy; KPIs; filters status / supplier / search; paged; the To-buy basket is one form with check boxes carrying data-sup / data-p / data-s, JS picks the suggested supplier and links the project only when all ticked lines share it), `purchase_order.php` (`&format=pdf` streams the PDF; header form with supplier defaults via data attributes; KPIs net / gross / delivery / lines; ✉ card `kind => 'po'` with `hidden po_id`; confirmation box on sent POs; lines table with pull-from-requisition select, quote inputs on sent RFQs, edit / delete while editable; line form with picker + approved-alternatives buttons; log). `_procurement_tab.php` gains the Purchase orders block (replaces the "follows in R80-003" placeholder). `index.php` requires `app/po.php` after `bom.php`; `$views` += purchase_orders, purchase_order; nav Procurement › Purchase orders for `po_can()`. Actions `po_save`, `po_pull`, `po_line_save`, `po_line_del`, `po_send`, `po_quote`, `po_award`, `po_confirm`, `po_cancel`, `po_close` (all `require_po_admin`). Lang +150 keys ×5 (`po.*`, `em.w_po`, `em.w_rfq`, `et.k_po`, `et.d_s_po`, `et.d_b_po`; the PDF strings stay English in every language because the document goes to suppliers abroad). Manuals: Golden (Purchase orders section), PM paragraph. Security: supplier must be an active company of kind supplier; the supplier of a sent RFQ cannot change; PO cannot be sent without prices; every transition checks the current status server-side; cancel reason mandatory; e-mail recipients validated (`FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL`); PDF only for `po_can`; the hidden `po_id` on the ✉ form is honoured only for `po_admin`. Performance: counts are four indexed COUNTs; `po_to_buy` one join query; PDF built on demand (not stored) — ~0.5 MB with the logo, same as the statement.


## 227. v1.94.0 — Procurement R80-004: goods receipts, NCR, delivery timeline, logistic costs, PO costs in the financial views (GAS-CP-R80-004)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r80_004_receipts.sql` (state `r80d`, selfcheck): `po_lines` + promised_date, qty_rejected; `po_orders` + logistics_total, expected_date, cost_id (the sp_costs row of the order); `po_milestones` (order_id + kind UNIQUE; kind confirmed|ready|shipped|export|arrived|customs|delivered; planned_date, actual_date, carrier, tracking, ref, packages, weight_kg, note, file_id, user); `po_costs` (kind freight|insurance|customs|duties|import_vat|handling|other, cost_date, provider, ref, amount, currency, note, file_id, sp_cost_id); `po_receipts` (receipt_no `<PO>-GRn` UNIQUE, received_at, delivery_note, warehouse_id, note, file_id, user); `po_receipt_lines` (po_line_id, qty_received, qty_accepted, qty_rejected, ncr_reason, ncr_action none|return|replace|credit, ncr_status none|open|closed, ncr_closed_at/note, location). `app/po.php` (appended): `po_r4()`, `po_line_expected($o, $l)` (promised → confirmed/sent + lead_days → header date), `po_expected_update()` (max of open lines → `expected_date`), `po_milestones()`, `po_milestone_save_from_post()` (upsert by kind; a confirmed actual date on a sent PO sets confirmed; delivered sets the header date when empty; documents via `cp_store_file` kind file / internal), `po_promised_from_post()` (called by `po_confirm`), `po_timeline($o)` (sent + the seven milestones + receipt summary, late = planned < today and no actual while the PO is open), `po_costs()`, `po_cost_save_from_post()` (mirrors to `sp_costs` kind `logistics` with the PO number, keeps `sp_cost_id`; `po_logistics_recalc`), `po_cost_delete()` (removes the mirror), `po_sp_cost_sync($oid)` (one `sp_costs` kind `order` per sent PO with a sub-project — inserted on first send, updated on recalculation / credit, deleted on cancel; `po_orders.cost_id`), `po_receipts()`, `po_receipt_lines()`, `po_ncr_open()`, `po_receive_from_post()` (`require_pr_wh`; per line recv[] / rej[] / ncr[] / reason[] / loc[]; over-delivery only with `allow_over`; reason mandatory on rejected qty; receipt row; line qty_received / accepted / rejected and status partial|received; accepted inventory lines → `stk_move('in', reason receipt, ref_kind po)` at unit_price × (1 − discount), `stk_price_add(source receipt, po_line_id)`, and when the line came from a requisition: `inv_stock.qty_reserved +=`, `pr_lines.qty_received / qty_reserved +=`, status received; free-text lines only update the requisition; PO status partial|received; `po_progress_apply`; `nf_push` work:po_received to the requesters), `po_ncr_close()` (credit → line qty = accepted, recalc + cost sync), `po_progress($sid)` (received / ordered value) and `po_progress_apply()` (writes `subprojects.pct_procurement` when higher and not N/A). Hooks: `po_mark_sent` → `po_sp_cost_sync` + `po_expected_update`; `po_confirm` → promised dates; `po_cancel` → cost sync; `po_close` → progress; `po_counts` late by `COALESCE(expected_date, delivery_date)`; `prq_issue` skips the `sp_costs` parts row when the requisition line has `qty_received` > 0 (its cost is on the PO row). Views: `purchase_order.php` — KPI shows Expected (and the landed cost), lines gain an Expected column (red when late, ✓ when promised), the confirmation form lists a promised date per line, new cards: Delivery timeline (table + step form with edit buttons that pre-fill), Logistic costs (table, totals, add / delete), Goods receipt (per-line inputs; Warehouse / Procurement; sent / confirmed / partial only), Receipts with their lines and NCR outcomes and the open-NCR close forms; `purchase_orders.php` list uses the expected date; `_procurement_tab.php` shows Procurement % and the logistics total. CSS `assets/portal.css`: `.form-grid`, `.field--full`, `.kv--full` (the R80 forms used them without a definition — now a two-column grid, one column under 760 px); `grid-2` cards fixed to `grid grid-2`. Actions `po_milestone`, `po_cost_add`, `po_cost_del`, `po_receive` (`require_pr_wh`), `po_ncr_close`. Lang +81 keys ×5 (`po.*`, `bg.k_logistics`). Manuals: Golden (new section), PM and staff paragraphs. Security: receipts and costs only on orders in the right state; over-delivery explicit; uploads through `cp_store_file` (type whitelist) and only when the order has a project; reasons capped; milestone upsert keyed by order + kind (no duplicates by replay); the sp_costs mirror rows are owned by their PO (`cost_id` / `sp_cost_id`) so a deleted cost removes exactly its row. Performance: timeline is one query per order; nothing added to list pages beyond the stored `expected_date`. Known limits: the Procurement % is value-based (a cheap late item does not hold it back); free-text PO lines are received but cannot be issued from stock (no item) — create the item first when it must be stocked.

## 228. v1.94.1 — Budget & actual: disciplines on the budget form chosen in Settings (GAS-CP-R80-005)
No SQL. `app/bg.php`: `bg_disc_setting()` (cp_setting `bg_disciplines`, comma-separated ids), `bg_disciplines_form(?array $B)` (the selection, or every active discipline when empty; disciplines with hours in the current budget are always kept), `bg_disc_setting_save()` (`is_cp_admin`); `bg_save_from_post` and `_budget_tab.php` iterate `bg_disciplines_form()` — `bg_disciplines()` (all active) stays for the calculation and the actual hours so nothing is hidden from the numbers. `views/admin_disciplines.php`: card "Budget & actual — disciplines on the budget form" (check boxes, same pattern as the planning-board disciplines), action `bg_discs_save`.
