## 229. v1.95.0 — Payables R81: supplier invoices, three-way check, payment requests with management threshold, payments, statement, aging (GAS-CP-R81-001)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r81_payables.sql` (state `r81`, selfcheck): `si_invoices` (kind invoice|credit, si_no UNIQUE `<prefix>-SI-<yy>-<nnnn>` / `-SCN-`, supplier_id + inv_no UNIQUE, order_id, credit_of, project/subproject/branch, issue_date, due_date, received_at, currency, net, vat_pct, vat, gross, paid, status draft|checked|disputed|requested|approved|paid|cancelled, match_status none|ok|qty|price|both, note, file_id, sp_cost_id, created/checked by), `si_lines` (seq, po_line_id, description, qty, unit_price, line_total, po_qty_open, po_price, match_status), `si_requests` (req_no `<prefix>-PRQ-<yy>-<nnnn>`, supplier_id, amount, currency, pay_by, status submitted|mgmt|approved|rejected|paid|cancelled, over_threshold, note, requested / decided by), `si_request_items` (request_id + invoice_id UNIQUE, amount), `si_payments` (invoice_id, request_id, pay_date, amount, currency, method transfer|card|cash|cheque|other, ref, note, file_id, user), `si_events`; `po_lines` + qty_invoiced. Module `app/si.php`: `si_can()` (management ∨ is_accounting ∨ pr_admin), `si_pay_can()` (management ∨ is_accounting), `si_number()`, `si_save_from_post()` (PO → supplier / project / currency / VAT; due = issue + supplier terms; duplicate supplier invoice number refused; PDF via `cp_store_file`; `pull_lines` → `si_lines_from_po` = accepted − invoiced at PO price after discount), `si_line_save_from_post()` / `si_line_delete()` (draft / disputed only), `si_recalc()`, `si_match()` (per line: price within `SI_TOL_PCT` = 2 % of the PO price, qty ≤ accepted − invoiced; worst line → invoice match_status), `si_check($iid, $force, $note)` (mismatch → disputed unless forced with a reason; checked → `po_lines.qty_invoiced += qty` (negative for credits), `si_sp_cost_sync`), `si_sp_cost_sync()` (one `sp_costs` kind `invoice` per checked invoice, negative for a credit note, owned via `sp_cost_id`; the PO's `order` row (`po_orders.cost_id`) is set to subtotal − Σ invoiced net so Budget & actual does not double count), `si_cancel()` / `si_reopen()` (undo the invoiced quantities and the cost row), `si_request_create($ids, $payBy, $note)` (checked invoices of one supplier and currency; amount = Σ ± open; over `proc_pay_threshold` → status mgmt + `nf_push` to `prq_mgmt_ids()`; management creating it → approved at once; else submitted + `nf_push` to `si_accounting_ids()`; invoices → requested), `si_request_decide()` (mgmt decides status mgmt, Accounting / management decide submitted; approve → invoices approved; reject needs reason → invoices back to checked), `si_request_cancel()`, `si_pay_from_post()` (`require_si_pay`; approved, checked or requested invoice; amount ≤ open; request linked when approved; invoice paid at zero open; request paid when all invoices paid; proof via `cp_store_file`; `nf_push` to the registrar), `si_payment_delete()`, `si_statement($supplierId, $from, $to)` (invoices +, credits −, payments −, running balance, opening balance), `si_aging()` (buckets by days past due, credits in not-due), `si_po_open_lines()`, `si_counts()`. `app/cf.php`: payments → `out_a` by pay date, open checked invoices → `out_f` by due date (credits negative), and `sp_costs` rows owned by Payables invoices are skipped in the parts block (no double count). Views: `payables.php` (tabs Invoices — list, filters, check boxes → payment request form with pay-by / note, JS allows one supplier; Requests — list + request detail with approve / reject (role-gated by status) / cancel and the log; Aging; Statement — supplier + period, running balance, Excel via `xlsxw`), `payable.php` (header form with PO → supplier lock, KPIs net / gross / open / check, Check / accept-difference / back to draft / cancel buttons, lines table with PO open qty and PO price per line, line form with PO-line picker that pre-fills, Payments card with record / remove, log); `purchase_order.php` gains an Invoices block with "+ Register" pre-filled. `index.php` requires `app/si.php` after `po.php`; `$views` += payables, payable; nav Accounting › Payables for `si_can()`. Actions `si_save`, `si_pull`, `si_line_save`, `si_line_del`, `si_check`, `si_reopen`, `si_cancel`, `si_request`, `si_req_decide`, `si_req_cancel`, `si_pay` (`require_si_pay`), `si_unpay` (`require_si_pay`). Lang +146 keys ×5 (`si.*`). Manuals: Golden (Payables section), PM paragraph. Security: supplier invoice numbers unique per supplier (duplicate registration refused); the three-way check and every transition are server-side and status-gated; the decision on a request is tied to its status (management for over-threshold, Accounting / management otherwise); payment amount capped at the open amount; files only through `cp_store_file`; cost mirror rows owned by their invoice. Performance: the invoice list is one query with correlated counts; aging is one query; statement two queries per supplier; cash flow adds two queries scoped to the projects in view.

## 230. v1.96.0 — Procurement R82: offer lines from the inventory with margin, production kits, FSR parts → stock issues, barcode scanning in the Service app 0.6.17 (GAS-CP-R82-001)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r82_integration.sql` (state `r82` = `offer_lines.item_id`, selfcheck): `offer_lines` + item_id, cost_price, margin_pct (+ index). Offers (`app/ob.php` `ob_line_save_from_post`): kind `item` → `stk_item`, erp_code = inventory code, description / unit from the item unless typed, `cost_price` = `stk_price_proposal` (fallback last_price), `margin_pct` from the form or `cp_setting proc_offer_margin` (default 25), unit_price = cost × (1 + margin) unless typed; `views/_offer_builder.php`: kind option (when `stk_has`), inventory picker (`?p=prq_items`, now also open to `ob_can()`), margin field, JS preview "cost → sale", cost / margin under the code for `fin_visible()`; Settings › Procurement gains "Default margin on inventory items in offers" (`stk_settings_save_from_post` → `proc_offer_margin`). Production kits (`app/bom.php` `bom_to_kit`): approved BOM → `pr_requests` with `bom_rev_id`, title "Production kit — BOM Rn · title", one `pr_lines` per BOM line at full qty, `prq_submit` at once; button on `views/bom.php`; action `bom_to_kit` (`require_prq`). Service-report parts (`app/fsr.php`): the parts JSON of a task keeps `c` (inventory code), `q` (qty) and `m` (the `inv_moves` id once issued) next to `d` / `t`; form fields `wk_pcode[i][j]`, `wk_pqty[i][j]`, hidden `wk_pmove[i][j]` (read-only once issued) in `views/fsr_edit.php`; `fsr_parts_issue($reportId)` runs at the end of every save: each coded part without `m` whose code is in the inventory → `stk_move(out, default warehouse, allow_negative, ref_kind fsr, note report_no)`, `m` stored (idempotent), `sp_costs` kind parts on the sub-project unless type W. API (`api/v1/lib/portal.php`): the app's parts carry `code`, `qty`, `move_id` both ways (the "×qty" suffix in the name is only added for uncoded parts); `api/v1/index.php` `GET /items?q=` (staff token, ≥ 2 chars, 12 rows: id, code, desc, maker, pn, unit, barcode, free — no prices); `api/v1/bootstrap.php` now loads `inv`, `prq`, `bom`, `po`, `si`. Service app 0.6.17 (`mobile/`): `partRow` gains code / qty / 📷 and the issued chip; `API.items(q)`; lookup on typing (350 ms debounce, one match auto-fills, several listed to pick); `scanBarcode()` uses `BarcodeDetector` + `getUserMedia` (environment camera, EAN/UPC/Code128/Code39/ITF/QR/DataMatrix) with a cancel button and a prompt fallback where the API is missing; i18n keys `rep.partcode`, `rep.partissued`, `rep.partfree`, `rep.partnone`, `rep.scan_manual` ×5; version bump in `api.js`, `sw.js`, `index.html`. Lang +16 keys ×5 (`ob.k_item`, `ob.inv_item`, `ob.margin`, `ob.cost`, `ob.need_item`, `stk.offer_margin(_hint)`, `fsr.part_code_ph`, `fsr.part_code_hint`, `fsr.part_issued`, `bom.kit*`, `bom.ev_to_kit`). Manuals: Golden (new section), staff and PM paragraphs. Security: the picker stays behind `prq_can` ∨ `ob_can`; `/items` needs a staff token and returns no prices; stock issue from a report is idempotent by the stored movement id and only for codes that exist; offer cost / margin rendered only for `fin_visible()`. Performance: one extra query per coded part on report save; nothing added to list pages. Employee portal: no code — the POMS can be switched off once the users are on the portal; no accounts or data were migrated by design (decision of 05/11/2026).

## 231. v1.96.1 — Suppliers: ERP import and makers per supplier (GAS-CP-R82-002)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r82_002_supplier_makers.sql` (state `r82b`, selfcheck): `supplier_makers` (supplier_id + maker UNIQUE, is_primary, note), `supplier_details.erp_code` (+ index). `app/inv.php`: `sup_makers_has()`, `sup_makers($sid)`, `sup_maker_add($sid, $maker, $primary, $note)` (upsert, primary never downgraded), `sup_maker_delete()`, `sup_maker_primary()` (one ★ per supplier), `sup_for_maker($maker)` (case-insensitive, active suppliers, primary first), `sup_suggest_for_item($item)` (preferred → last → primary of maker → any of maker), `sup_all_makers()` (inventory ∪ suppliers), `sup_import_map()` (EL/EN headers: name, vat_no, erp_code, address, postal_code, city, country, phone, email, website, terms_days, advance_pct, currency, incoterms, lead_days, makers, notes), `sup_import_parse($path)` (xlsx via `xlsx_rows`; match existing by VAT digits → ERP code → lower-cased name; duplicates in the file and invalid e-mails warned; makers split on , ; / |), `sup_import_apply($pv, $updateExisting)` (new companies kind supplier; existing: only non-null cells written, notes only when empty; `supplier_details` upsert with COALESCE so empty cells never erase; makers added, first maker of a supplier without a primary becomes ★); `sup_save_from_post` / `sup_load` carry `erp_code`. `app/po.php` `po_to_buy`: `item_maker` selected; lines with neither preferred nor last supplier get `maker_supplier_id` / `maker_supplier` from `sup_for_maker` (item maker, else the line's maker); `purchase_orders.php` uses it as the suggested supplier (with the maker in brackets). Views: `suppliers_import.php` (upload → preview with new / update pills and warnings → apply with "complete existing" tick), `suppliers.php` (Import from ERP button, ERP code field, Makers / brands block with datalist of known makers, ★ / × forms; the contacts list query fixed — `position AS role`, the `role` column never existed in `contacts` and the card 500'd when a supplier had contacts). Actions `sup_maker_add`, `sup_maker_del`, `sup_maker_primary`, `sup_import_parse`, `sup_import_apply` (all `require_pr_admin`). `index.php` `$views` += suppliers_import. Lang +21 keys ×5 (`sup.import*`, `sup.imp_*`, `sup.erp_code`, `sup.makers*`, `sup.maker_*`). Manual: Golden inventory section (two bullets). Security: import only for `pr_admin`, parsed through `xlsx_rows` (nothing stored), preview kept in the session and discarded after apply; e-mails validated; names capped; existing rows never erased by empty cells. Performance: the maker suggestion adds at most one small query per basket line without a known supplier.


## 232. v1.97.0 — ✉ button on purchase orders / RFQs; parts from the phone in the Service, PM and HR apps (GAS-CP-R82-003)
No SQL. `views/purchase_order.php`: header button `✉ E-mail` (`data-open-email`, `po_admin()`, states draft / sent / quoted / confirmed / partial) opens the existing `details.em-card` (`em_card_html` kind `po`, attachment `po:<id>` PDF, hidden `po_id` → `po_mark_sent` on draft) — the Outlook .eml / mailto pattern of the whole portal, no SMTP. API (`api/v1/lib/portal_parts.php`, `final class PortalParts`, loaded by `api/v1/bootstrap.php`): `can()` = `prq_can()`, `list($status)` (open | mine | pending | all, 60 rows, with `can_approve` = `pr_admin()` and `priced` = `stk_price_visible()`), `one($id)` (header, lines with reserved / ordered / received / issued, events, `can_cancel`), `options()` (projects, sub-projects, purposes, units), `create($b)` (fills `$_POST` and runs `prq_save_from_post` → `prq_line_save_from_post` per line → `prq_submit` unless `draft`; lines: `item_id` or free text with maker / part no., qty, unit), `cancel($id, $note)` (`prq_cancel`), `decide($id, approve|reject, $note)` (`pr_admin` → `prq_decide`). Routes in `api/v1/index.php` (staff token): `GET /requisitions?status=`, `GET /requisitions/options`, `GET /requisitions/{id}`, `POST /requisitions` (RateLimit `prq`), `POST /requisitions/{id}/cancel`, `POST /requisitions/{id}/decide`; the Golden HR gate list admits `/requisitions*` and `/items`. Apps: `js/parts.js` (identical in `mobile/`, `mobile-pm/`, `mobile-hr/`) — `PARTS.list(q)` (segments Open / Mine / To approve / All, cards with state pill, budget flag, lines · value, cached in IndexedDB `meta` `parts:list:<q>` for offline reading), `PARTS.one(id)` (lines, log, Cancel, Approve / Reject with reason for Procurement), `PARTS.create()` (options from `/requisitions/options`, inventory search via `/items` with 350 ms debounce, 📷 `scanBarcode()` from the Service app reused, free-text lines, validation, submit → request screen); routes `#/parts`, `#/parts?s=`, `#/parts/new`, `#/parts/{id}` in each `app.js`; `API.requisitions / requisition / requisitionOptions / requisitionCreate / requisitionCancel / requisitionDecide / items` in each `api.js`; entry button 🧾 on the Service and PM home screens and on the HR inbox (`UI.goLinks` added to its bind); i18n `parts.*` (~85 keys) ×5 languages in each app; versions Service 0.6.17 → 0.6.18, Golden PM 1.0.9 → 1.0.10, Golden HR 1.0.3 → 1.0.4 (`api.js`, `sw.js`, `index.html` script tags). Manuals: Golden (apps bullet + PO send bullet), staff ("Parts from the phone"), PM paragraph. Security: every route needs a staff token and passes the same `prq_can` / `pr_admin` / `prq_can_view` checks as the browser; create is rate-limited; the API returns no prices unless `stk_price_visible()`; free-text lines are capped by the portal validators; the ✉ button only renders for `po_admin()` and never sends anything by itself. Performance: list = one query + counts; nothing added to portal pages; the apps cache the list only.

## 233. v1.98.0 — HR › Utilization: hours on projects vs. without, per portal user (GAS-CP-R83-001)
No SQL. `app/ut.php` (loaded by `index.php` before `si`): `UT_ABSENT` = leave, sick, parental, strike; `UT_INTERNAL` = office, warehouse, training, travel, other; `UT_TS_STATES` = approved, invoiced, submitted. `ut_has()` (planning + calendars present), `ut_can()` (staff), `ut_all()` (`hr_can` ∨ `mgmt_can` ∨ `is_cp_admin`), `ut_scope()` (null = everybody; else self + the active users whose `manager_user_id` is me or whose discipline I manage), `ut_can_view($uid)`, `ut_people()` (`pl_people(true)` filtered by the scope), `ut_ts_hours($from, $to, $uid?)` (timesheet lines × headers in the states, keyed user / date), `ut_user($p, $from, $to, $grid?, $ts?)` — day loop: capacity = `wc_day(calendar)['normal']` on workdays between `hr_people.hire_date` and `left_date`; board entries without hours share the day; project hours and pending (submitted) hours from the lines; absence capped at capacity, internal capped at capacity − absence − project (the timesheet wins over a planned office day); idle = capacity − absence − internal − project ≥ 0; overtime = project above the available hours; `idle_days` = workdays entirely idle; `missing_days` = planned `work` days with no timesheet hours; aggregated into months and totals, plus hours / days per project, hours per activity, per internal kind and per absence type (leave type name when known). `ut_pct` = project / (capacity − absence − internal); `ut_idle_pct`; `ut_tone` (≥ 80 green, ≥ 60 amber); `ut_team($from, $to, $did?, $techOnly)` (one `pl_grid` + one `ut_ts_hours` for everybody, totals only); `ut_period()` (year, default current year to date; or from / to ≤ 400 days). `views/utilization.php` (`$views` += utilization): filters person (scope) / discipline / year or dates / "Technical staff only" (default on when `hr_technical_user_ids()` is known; `p_applied` marks a submitted form), KPIs, months table with `.ut-bar` stacked bar, by project (links), breakdown (activities `eng.a_*`, internal, absent), links to timesheets / planning / evaluation; team mode: KPIs and one row per person sorted by project hours, name → person mode; `&export=csv` (months + projects, or team). Nav: HR group 📈 for `ut_all()` or `hr_is_manager()` (`views/_layout.php`). CSS `.ut-bar / .ut-p / .ut-i / .ut-a / .ut-x / .ut-key` in `assets/portal.css`. Lang +41 keys ×5 (`ut.*`). Manuals: Golden HR section, staff ("Your utilization"), PM monthly closing. Security: scope enforced in `ut_people()` and on `&user=` (outside the scope → team view of the scope); read-only page, no actions; CSV carries the same scope. Performance: team mode runs two queries for all people plus the calendar loop in memory (≈ 365 × people iterations); the person mode two small queries; nothing cached, nothing added elsewhere.

## 234. v1.99.0 — procurement e-mail templates, Contacts finder in the ✉ card, supplier import template with a Contacts sheet (GAS-CP-R84-001)
No SQL. `app/et.php`: `ET_KINDS` += `rfq`, `po_chase`, `ncr`, `remittance` (before `signature`); `et_builtin` subjects / bodies from `et.d_s_<kind>` / `et.d_b_<kind>`; labels `et.k_<kind>` (`et.k_po` renamed "Purchase order (to the supplier)"). `app/em.php` `em_card_html`: option `kinds` = [kind => label] renders a `select.js-em-kind` and embeds every kind's subject / body as `data-em-tx` JSON on the form (texts rendered server-side with the same ctx, so templates and placeholders behave as before); option `company_id` feeds the finder; markup adds `.em-added` (container for picked people) and `.em-find` + `.em-find-list` (dropdown, `prq-list` styling) when `ct_has()` and staff. `views/contacts_json.php` (`$views`): `?p=contacts_json&q=&company=` → up to 12 active contacts with an e-mail matching name / e-mail / company, the given company first — staff only, no-store. `assets/portal.js` (R84-001 block): kind change swaps subject / body from `data-em-tx`; the finder debounces 250 ms, fetches, lists `.prq-opt`, a click appends a checked `to[]` checkbox (or ticks the existing one), clears, closes on outside click. CSS `.em-find-list` absolute dropdown. `views/purchase_order.php`: `$emKinds` = rfq | po (draft / sent) | po_chase + po (confirmed / partial) [+ ncr when `po_ncr_open`]; card called with `kind` = first, `kinds`, `company_id`. `views/payable.php`: after the payments table, when `paid > 0`, a `remittance` card with `po_people()` built from the supplier (company e-mail + its contacts), doc = `si.w_invoice`. Supplier import (`app/inv.php`): `sup_import_template()` (`xlsx_download`, sheets Suppliers / Contacts with headers + one example row; `?p=suppliers_import&template=1`), `sup_import_contacts($path, $rows, $byVat, $byErp, $byName, &$warn)` reads sheet "Contacts" (`xlsx_rows($path, 'Contacts')`), maps Supplier code / VAT / Supplier / Full name / Position / Email / Phone / Mobile, ties each person to a row of the first sheet or an existing supplier (else warning `sup.imp_w_ct_sup`), detects an existing contact (company + e-mail, else name) → `$pv['contacts']`; `sup_import_apply` now updates `$pv['rows'][$i]['id']` after creation and inserts / updates the contacts (`category = 'supplier'`, `company_id`, `company`, `created_by`), result `contacts`; the preview lists them with new / update pills; `sup.imp_done_s` gains `{contacts}`. `views/suppliers.php`: the people block becomes a small table (name · position · ✉ mailto · phone) with "+ New" and "All contacts ›" (`contacts&company=`). Lang +22 keys ×5. Manuals: Golden (templates bullet, PO send bullet, import bullet). Security: `contacts_json` requires a staff session and returns only e-mail-bearing active contacts (no phones, no notes); the template select carries texts rendered for the current user and document only; the import keeps `require_pr_admin()` on template and apply and sanitises every cell as before. Performance: one extra `em_text` per kind at card render (3 at most); the finder is one indexed LIKE query per keystroke pause.

## 235. v1.99.1 — supplier import: match any company, per-row resilience (GAS-CP-R84-002)
No SQL. `sup_import_parse` builds `$byVat / $byErp / $byName` from every company (`ORDER BY kind = 'supplier' DESC, id`, so a supplier wins over a namesake of another kind) — `companies.name` is unique (`uq_co_name`), so a company created from the Contacts module under another kind collided on INSERT; `sup_import_apply` wraps each row in try / catch (`errors[]` = line: name — message, capped at 50), the UPDATE branch sets `kind = 'supplier', is_active = 1`; the result carries `errors`, shown by `suppliers_import.php` as a warning list (`sup.imp_err_n`). Verified with the S1 ERP export (2,385 rows, 3.5 s apply, idempotent re-import).

## 236. v1.99.2 — a company can be customer and supplier at once (GAS-CP-R84-003)
`install/migrate_cp_r84_003_is_supplier.sql`: `companies.is_supplier TINYINT(1)` after `kind`, back-filled from `kind = 'supplier'` and `supplier_details`; migration state `r84c`; self-check line. `app/inv.php`: `sup_flag_has()`, `sup_where()` (`co.is_supplier = 1`, else the old `kind = 'supplier'`), `sup_list` uses it; `sup_save_from_post` and the import UPDATE set `is_supplier = 1` and `kind = IF(kind IN ('other','supplier',''), 'supplier', kind)` — an owner / yard / class company keeps its kind; INSERTs set both. `app/po.php`: the supplier check accepts `is_supplier`. `app/ct.php` `co_save_from_post`: "Also a supplier" tick (or kind supplier) sets the flag and creates the empty `supplier_details` row so the supplier card opens. `views/companies.php`: filter option `is_supplier` (★ Suppliers, any kind), gold Supplier pill linking to `suppliers&id=` when the kind is not supplier, the tick on the form. Lang +2 ×5. Verified: owner company 385 re-imported → stays owner, listed under Procurement › Suppliers, accepted on a PO.

## 237. v1.100.0 — Organisations: one register behind customers, companies and suppliers (GAS-CP-R85-001)
No SQL. `app/org.php` (loaded by `index.php`): `org_has / org_can` (`ct_manage()` ∨ admin), `org_key()` (lower case, dots removed so "S.A." → "sa", punctuation → space, legal-form noise list removed), `org_vat()` (digits, ≥ 6, placeholders like 000000000 ignored), `org_matches()` → linked | match (VAT, then ERP code, then name key; a company already linked to another customer is never proposed) | none, `org_link($co, $cu)` (sets `companies.customer_id`, completes country / city / website / e-mail / phone both ways with COALESCE(NULLIF), customers.vat_no / erp_code / address / postal_code from `supplier_details`, and the reverse into `supplier_details` when the company is a supplier; derives `contacts.customer_id` for the company's people and `contacts.company_id` for the customer's people), `org_create_for_customer($cu, $kind)` (reuses a same-name company, else INSERT with the customer's data), `org_link_all()`, `org_derive_people()` (two UPDATE … JOIN), `org_counts()`, `org_dupes()` (groups by VAT / ERP / name key; keep = linked → not "OLD_" → supplier → most people → oldest; pairs with two different customers skipped), `org_merge($keep, $drop)` (contacts, po_orders, si_invoices, si_requests, inv_prices, production_subs, supplier_makers (UPDATE IGNORE + DELETE), supplier_details moved or folded with COALESCE; kept company completed, `is_supplier` raised, notes concatenated; the duplicate deactivated, unlinked, renamed "[merged #id]"). `views/organisations.php` (`$views`): KPIs, Link all / Complete people buttons, Duplicate organisations table (Merge with confirm), Suggested matches (Link), Customers without organisation (kind select + Create), Linked. Actions `org_link`, `org_link_all`, `org_create_co`, `org_merge`, `org_derive_people` in `pm_actions.php`. `views/contacts.php`: 🏭 Suppliers (for `pr_can`) and 🏛 Organisations (with pending count) buttons. `views/companies.php`: ★ Customer pill → `admin_customers&edit=`. `views/contact_edit.php` + `ct_save_from_post`: "+ New organisation…" option reveals name + kind, saved through `co_find(..., create)`. Lang +41 keys ×5 (`org.*`). Manual: Golden contacts section. Security: every action behind `org_can()` + CSRF; merge refuses two different customers; nothing is deleted (duplicates retired). Performance: the page runs three queries over companies / customers in memory; `org_counts()` on the Contacts page is one of them (fine at 300 companies; cache if the register grows past a few thousand).

## 238. v1.101.0 — organisation roles, master data on the organisation, people in several organisations (GAS-CP-R85-002)
`install/migrate_cp_r85_002_org_roles.sql`: `companies.roles VARCHAR(160)` (CSV), `vat_no`, `erp_code`, `address`, `postal_code`; back-fill roles from kind + is_supplier + customer_id and the master data from `supplier_details` and the linked `customers`; table `contact_companies (contact_id, company_id, position)` seeded from `contacts.company_id`. State `r85b`, self-check line. `app/ct.php`: `co_roles_has()`, `cc_has()`, `co_role_list()` (customer + kinds), `co_roles($row)` (CSV, else derived from kind / flag / link), `co_main_kind($roles)` (priority owner → management → yard → class → agent → broker → consultant → supplier → other — written into `kind` so old code and pills keep working), `co_role_pills($row)` (★ Customer → customer record, Supplier → supplier card, kinds), `co_sync_roles($id)` (Customer role: UPDATE the linked customer or the same-name one with COALESCE, else INSERT; `customer_id` set, people derived; Supplier role: `is_supplier`, `supplier_details` upsert with VAT / ERP / address), `ct_companies($cid)` (all organisations of a person, primary first), `co_people($coId)` (primary + additional memberships). `co_save_from_post`: `roles[]` ticks (+ old `is_supplier`), kind derived, master data saved, `co_sync_roles`. `co_find(create)` writes roles. `ct_save_from_post`: `other_companies[]` → `contact_companies` rewritten (primary always included). `ct_list` company filter includes memberships. `app/po.php` `po_people` and `views/suppliers.php` use `co_people`; `views/contacts_json.php` ranks memberships of the document's company first. `views/companies.php`: role ticks + VAT / ERP / address / postal fields, role pills in the list, filter ★ Customer / ★ Suppliers; `?role=customer` pre-ticks. `views/contact_edit.php`: "Also belongs to" multi-select; `views/contact.php`: all organisations with role pills. `views/admin_customers.php`: "+ New customer" → organisation form with the Customer role, Organisations button. `app/iv.php` `cu_save_from_post`: the linked organisation follows name / VAT / ERP / address (unless the name would collide). `app/org.php`: VAT / ERP read from the organisation first; `org_link` adds the customer role + copies master data + seeds memberships; `org_merge` moves memberships and unions the roles; `org_create_for_customer` writes roles + master data. Supplier import and manual supplier save keep `roles` and the organisation's VAT / ERP / address in step. Lang +6 ×5. Manual: Golden contacts section (Roles paragraph). Security: all writes behind `ct_manage()` / `require_pr_admin()` as before; the customer record is only ever created or completed (COALESCE), never blanked; customer sync refuses a name collision. Performance: one extra UPDATE per organisation save; `co_people` one query with an EXISTS-style join.

## 239. v1.101.1 — organisation matching by VAT only (GAS-CP-R85-003)
Decision of Nikos 06/11/2026. `app/org.php`: `org_matches` proposes a company only when `org_vat()` of the customer equals the organisation's VAT (ERP code / name branches removed); `org_dupes` groups by VAT only; `org_create_for_customer` reuses an existing organisation only when it carries the same VAT (`REGEXP_REPLACE(..., '[^0-9]', '')`, MySQL 8 / MariaDB ≥ 10.0.5), else creates. `app/ct.php` `co_sync_roles`: the Customer role links an existing customer by VAT only, else creates one. `app/inv.php` `sup_import_parse`: an existing organisation is recognised by VAT, else by the ERP code (the ERP's own key — 59 suppliers carry no VAT and would otherwise duplicate on every re-import); the name is never used. Texts `org.lede / h_match / h_dupes / h_none` ×5 updated.

## 240. v1.102.0 — Organisations, step 3: customer import → organisations; vessel related companies on organisations (GAS-CP-R85-004)
`install/migrate_cp_r85_004_vessel_orgs.sql`: `vessel_companies.customer_id` nullable, `company_id` added (+ index), unique key `(vessel_id, customer_id)` dropped, back-fill `company_id` from `companies.customer_id`. State `r85c`, self-check line. `app/org.php` `org_ensure_for_customer($cuId)`: linked organisation → name / master data / Customer role refreshed (name only when it would not collide); none → `org_create_for_customer` (VAT reuse, else create kind owner + roles owner,customer). `app/iv.php` `cu_import`: match by VAT (digits, ≥ 6, no placeholder) → ERP code → name only when the row has no VAT; after each row `org_ensure_for_customer` (`res['organisations']`). `app/vm.php`: `VM_ROLES` + `class`; `vm_orgs_has()`; `vm_related` joins `companies` (via `company_id`, else the customer's organisation, else the customer name) and returns kind / roles / is_supplier / org_customer_id / email; `vm_related_set($vid, $companyId, $role, $note)` (update by (vessel, company) else insert with the organisation's customer_id); `vm_related_set_customer` for imports (ensures the organisation first) — used by the vessel import in `pm_actions.php`. `index.php` action `vm_related_set` reads `company_id` (falls back to `customer_id`). `views/vessel_view.php`: picker = `co_all()`, rows link to the organisation with `co_role_pills` and e-mail. `views/companies.php`: ⚓ Vessels block on the organisation card (owned as customer + related with role). Lang `vm.role_class` ×5. Manual: Golden contacts section. Security: unchanged gates (`require_staff` on vessel relations as before, `require_ct_manage` on imports); the organisation's name is never overwritten into a collision. Performance: one query per vessel page block as before; import adds one UPDATE per customer row.

## 241. v1.102.1 — procurement module end-to-end test and corrections (GAS-CP-R86-001)
Harness (sandbox, `scratchpad/ptest*.php`, 308 checks, 0 failures at the end): page sweep for admin / procurement / engineer; requisition (engineer) → Procurement decision → Management (over budget) → reservation → warehouse issue; to-buy → RFQ → ✉ sent → quotes → award → PO → line → ✉ sent → confirm with dates → milestone → receipt with NCR → NCR close (replacement) → second receipt → logistic cost → balance cancel → invoice → pull lines → three-way check → payment request → decision → payment → remittance card → close; BOM create → line → issue → Procurement change proposal → Engineering decision → approve → production kit; direct PO without lines refused; inventory item, stock in / over-issue refused, supplier price hidden from engineers, maker link; API login / list / options / create / one / cancel / decide refused / 404 / 401; reservation never above free stock, stock never negative or over-reserved, numbering unique, sp_costs orphans; i18n sweep of 11 screens × 5 languages for raw keys and PHP warnings; nested-form detector; .eml drafts with the PDF. Corrections: `views/purchase_order.php` line-delete buttons use `form="dl<id>"` with the forms emitted after the quotation form (nested forms are dropped by browsers → "Save quotes" posted `po_line_del`); `app/po.php` `po_receive` open = qty − qty_accepted (rejected goods can be received again), `po_cancel` on an order with accepted goods → open lines cancelled, partial lines fixed at the accepted quantity, status `received`, event `cancel` with `po.cancel_balance` (flash adapted in `pm_actions.php`); `app/prq.php` `prq_cancel` refuses when Σ qty_issued + qty_received > 0 (`prq.cannot_cancel_issued`), over-budget approve without note → `prq.need_note_over`; `app/fsr.php` parts issue below zero → `nf_push` to `prq_proc_ids()` (`stk.neg_title / neg_body`); lang `po.ev_ncr_close / ev_cost / ev_cost_del / ev_milestone` ×5; `views/suppliers_import.php` passes only scalars to `t('sup.imp_done_s')` (Array-to-string warning). Observations, no change: a user's session ends on a request with another User-Agent (sec_bind_ua — correct); a TOTP code cannot be reused within its window (correct); the seeded demo `sp_costs` row PO-2026-118 has no order (sandbox data only).

## 242. v1.103.0 — PO line item picker (drop-down, new item, codes) and CSP fix of the procurement scripts (GAS-CP-R86-002)
No SQL. `app/inv.php`: `stk_items` filter `supplier` (preferred_supplier_id ∨ last_supplier_id ∨ inv_prices ∨ supplier_makers by maker); `stk_item_quick_create($d)` (code · description · maker · part no. · unit · category · supplier → `inv_items`, existing code reused, maker added to the supplier's makers). `app/prq.php` `prq_item_search($q, $limit, $supplier)`: empty query with a supplier lists that supplier's items then the rest (dropdown on focus); each row carries `codes` [{k: pn|barcode|code, v}]. `views/prq_items.php`: `&supplier=`, empty `q` allowed with a supplier, limit 20. `app/po.php` `po_line_save_from_post`: `new_item=1` → `stk_item_quick_create` with `new_code`, `new_category_id`, the line's description / maker / part_no / unit and the order's supplier → `item_id`. `views/purchase_order.php`: `#lf-new` panel (hidden; `new_item` flag, `new_code`, `new_category_id`, Cancel), `lf-makers` datalist from `sup_makers`, `lf-codes` datalist filled from the chosen item; JS: `render()` with the "➕ New inventory item" row, focus → supplier dropdown, `newItem()` (clears, description = typed text, code suggested from maker + part no. until typed), `codes()`. **CSP**: the 10 inline `<script>` blocks of requisition / purchase_order(s) / bom / payable(s) had no `nonce` and the 21 `onsubmit / onclick` confirmations were inline — `script-src 'self' 'nonce-…'` blocked them all in browsers (R53-001 policy), so the pickers, the project → sub-project filter, the selection buttons and every "are you sure" did not run (forms submitted without asking). All scripts now carry `nonce="<?= csp_nonce() ?>"`; confirmations use `data-confirm` (forms and submit buttons, styled dialog) and the two typed-reason cases `data-prompt="…" data-prompt-field="note"` (new handler in `portal.js`); `_procurement_tab` uses `data-toggle="#bom-new"`, `utilization` `data-js-print`, `community` `data-js-open` (new handler). Rule for future views: no inline `<script>` without the nonce, no `on*=` attributes. Verified with Playwright: no CSP console errors on 14 procurement pages, styled confirm shown, dropdown 21 options on focus, item chosen fills maker / part no. / codes, new-item panel.

No SQL. `app/prq.php` `prq_recent_items($limit)` (items by last pr_lines / inv_moves id, then items in stock; `stk_items` filter `ids`), `prq_item_search($q, $limit, $supplier, $recent)`; `views/prq_items.php` `&recent=1` allows an empty query; `prq_line_save_from_post` accepts `new_item` for `pr_admin()` (→ `stk_item_quick_create`). `views/requisition.php`: picker focus → recent list, `render()` with the "➕ New inventory item" row (Procurement only), `#lf-new` panel. API `GET /items?recent=1` with an empty `q` → `prq_recent_items(12)`; `js/parts.js` ×3: `lookup(v, auto, list)` — focus lists recent items without auto-picking; `api.js` adds `&recent=1` when the query is short. ## 244. v1.104.0 — stock control: typed movements, consumables, low-stock alarms, shortage console, Stock project (GAS-CP-R87-001)
`install/migrate_cp_r87_001_stock_control.sql`: `inv_moves.ref_no`, `company_id` (+ indexes), `inv_items.is_consumable`, `alert_low` (back-filled where min_stock > 0), `reorder_qty`; old in/out moves without reason → `manual`. State `r87a`, self-check. `app/stc.php` (loaded by `index.php`): `STK_IN_REASONS` (receipt, manual, return_project, return_customer), `STK_OUT_REASONS` (delivery_customer, delivery_production, consumption, issue, manual, scrap), `stc_has()`, `stc_reason_label()`, `stc_stock_sub()` (settings `proc_stock_project_id / proc_stock_subproject_id`), `stc_stock_project_create($customerId, $code)` (project category internal + sub-project STOCK, settings set), `stc_shortages($cat)` (alarm on, min > 0, free < min; on_order = open PO lines qty − received; requested = open stock-requisition lines qty − ordered − reserved; gap = min − free − incoming; suggested = max(reorder, gap); covered when gap ≤ 0), `stc_shortage_count()`, `stc_raise_requisition($items, $qtys, $note)` (`prq_save_from_post` → lines → `prq_submit`, purpose stock on the Stock sub-project), `stc_after_out($itemId)` (`nf_push` to `prq_proc_ids()` keyed `stk_low:<item>:<Ymd>`). `app/inv.php` `stk_move`: reason defaults to `manual` for in/out, `issue` of a consumable → `consumption` (`item_consumable` option), `ref_no` / `company_id` stored, alarm after any decrease; `stk_move_from_post`: `reason_in` / `reason_out` with validations (return_project / delivery_production / issue / consumption need a sub-project; delivery_customer / return_customer need `company_id`; manual / scrap exits need a note); `stk_item_save_from_post` saves the three flags; `stk_settings_save_from_post` saves the Stock sub-project. `app/prq.php` issue and `app/fsr.php` parts pass `item_consumable`. Actions `stc_raise`, `stc_stock_project`. `views/inventory_item.php`: reorder qty, alarm and consumable ticks; movement form reason selects, reference, organisation; movements list shows reason · reference · organisation. `views/inventory.php`: tab 🔔 Shortages (KPIs, table with select-all `data-check-all`, quantities, Raise button disabled until the Stock project is set). `views/admin_procurement.php`: Stock project select (#stock) and the create card. `assets/portal.js`: `data-check-all`. Lang +50 keys ×5. Manual: Golden inventory section. Security: raise / create behind `require_pr_admin`, movements behind `require_pr_wh`; organisation ids validated by FK-less lookup only on display (name lookup); notifications deduplicated per day. Performance: the shortage query is one grouped query with two correlated sums over indexed columns; the tab badge runs it once per Inventory page (cached in the request).

## 246. v1.104.2 — companies: VAT / ERP code duplicates refused with a message, customer linked by ERP code (GAS-CP-R87-003)
No SQL. `customers.erp_code` is UNIQUE (schema R2): creating / saving an organisation with the Customer role whose ERP code already existed on an unlinked customer record made `co_sync_roles()` INSERT a second customer → `SQLSTATE 1062 Duplicate entry … erp_code` shown raw on Companies. `app/ct.php` `co_save_from_post()`: after the name check, three guards (roles schema only) — VAT (`org_vat()` digits) on another company → `co.dup_vat`; ERP code on another company → `co.dup_erp`; ERP code on a customer record already linked to another company → `co.dup_erp` naming that company. `co_sync_roles()`: when the company has no `customer_id`, match the existing customer by VAT (R85-003) **then by ERP code** and link it (name / master data synced as before) instead of inserting. Lang `co.dup_vat`, `co.dup_erp` (5 files, 7751 keys). Verified in the sandbox as the assistant: new company with an existing ERP code → message; re-saving the existing company with the Customer role → linked to customer 730, still one customers row.

## 247. v1.105.0 — item links · software licences · RFO to several suppliers · delivery notes (GAS-CP-R88-001)
SQL `install/migrate_cp_r88_001_links_software_rfo_dn.sql` (run once; `cp_patches()` check `r88a` = `po_receipt_lines.dest`): `inv_item_links`; `inv_items.item_type` ENUM part/software/service + `inv_licences`; `po_rfq_suppliers` + `po_rfq_quotes` (existing RFQs back-filled: header supplier = first invited, typed line prices = its quotes); `inv_delivery_notes` + `inv_dn_lines`; `po_receipt_lines.dest` / `dn_id`.
**Modules.** `app/ilk.php` — `ilk_*` links (`ilk_url_ok()`: http(s) only, no credentials in the authority, no control characters; rendered with `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"`), `swl_*` licences (`SWL_SELECT`, `swl_list/expiring/save_from_post/delete/from_receipt/mask/keys_visible` — keys only for `pr_admin()`, `swl_sweep()` marks expired). `app/rfo.php` — `rfo_suppliers/add_supplier/remove/mark_sent/quote_from_post/decline/compare/order_for/award`; `po_award($rfqId, $rsId)` delegates to `rfo_award` when the tables exist; `po_em_attachment` accepts `po:<id>:<supplier_id>`; `em_draft_from_post` honours hidden `rfq_sup` (→ `rfo_mark_sent`). `app/dn.php` — `dn_create($h, $L)` books one `stk_move` out per item line (reason by kind: issue / delivery_customer / delivery_production / manual; `ref_kind dn`, `ref_no` = DN number), requisition lines through `prq_issue_dn()` (new `$o` parameter of `prq_issue()` carries ref_kind/ref_id/ref_no/company_id and it now returns the movement id); `dn_cancel` books the reverse movements and undoes `pr_lines.qty_issued`; `dn_pdf` (PdfWriter, same layout as the PO); numbers `<prefix>-DN-<yy>-<nnnn>` in `doc_sequences`. `po_receive_from_post`: `dest[lid]` per line; software items (`swl_from_receipt`) get a pending licence instead of a stock movement; project-routed lines collect into one DN per receipt (`receipt_id`, `order_id`), free-text lines go on the note without a movement (`skip_move`); event `dn`.
**Views.** `views/_inv_extras.php` (links card; licences card — `$extra` = links | licences, licences replace the Stock card for software items), `inventory_item.php` (Item type select), `inventory.php` (tab Software with register + filters, type filter, Delivery notes tab link, expiring licences under Shortages), `views/_po_rfo.php` (invited suppliers · quotation of the selected supplier `?sup=<rs_id>` · comparison), `purchase_order.php` (header lede lists the suppliers; ✉ card addressed to the selected supplier with `rfq_sup`; price columns on an RFO only once awarded; receipt Destination column; receipts list links the 🚚 note), `purchase_orders.php` (+n suppliers pill), `delivery_notes.php` (register + new note with the item picker, CSP-nonced script), `delivery_note.php` (card, PDF, ✉ kind `dn`, cancel). E-mail template kind `dn` (`ET_KINDS`, builtin `et.d_s_dn` / `et.d_b_dn`). Actions: `inv_link_add/del`, `inv_lic_save/del`, `rfo_invite/uninvite/send/quote/decline`, `po_award` (+`rs_id`), `dn_create/cancel`. Lang: 154 keys × 5 (`ilk.*`, `swl.*`, `rfo.*`, `dn.*`, `po.ev_invite/uninvite/send_sup/decline/dn`, `em.w_dn`, `et.*_dn`) — 7905 keys each.
**Tests.** `ptest4.php` (88 checks, 0 FAIL): links incl. `javascript:` / `ftp:` refused, bare host → https; software item, licence expiry states, masked / revealed key by role; RFO with 3 suppliers (✉ draft to supplier 2 with PDF marks it sent, quotes, totals 855 / 830, incomplete award refused, decline, award → PO 830 for supplier 2, states lost / awarded / declined); receipt to project → DN with 2 lines and 1 movement, stock unchanged, receipt lines carry dest/dn; manual customer DN, cancellation returns stock; software receipt → pending licence, no movement; engineer blocked, warehouse user allowed. Earlier harness (`ptest.php`) adapted to `rfo_quote`. Security review: new inputs bounded (`mb_substr`), URLs validated, keys never in lists / non-admin forms, all new inline scripts carry the CSP nonce, no `on*=` attributes, every write behind `require_pr_admin` / `require_pr_wh` / `require_po_admin`.

## 248. v1.106.0 – v1.108.1 — Recruiting, website feed & inbox, applicant evaluation, release hygiene (GAS-CP-R89-001 · R90-001 · R90-002 · R91-001 · R91-002)
Built in the website project (branch `feature/r89-recruiting`, contract `goldenas-website/hr/HR-INTEGRATION-SPEC.md` R3) and merged here with R91-002. **Public endpoints** (no session, no cookies): `customer/rcr_feed.php` — `?r=vacancies|events|set` (GET, CORS for `rc_site_origin`), `?r=apply|cv|register|request` (POST, `X-Site-Key` = `cp_settings.rc_site_key`, constant-time; per-IP minute limits `rc_feed_rl_read` 60 / `rc_feed_rl_write` 10; visitor IP from `X-Forwarded-For` as sent by the website proxy and by Sucuri) and `customer/web_feed.php` (GET counts / POST `?r=ticket|access`, `web_site_key`). Module `app/rcr.php` (`rcr_*`: sets with server-side scoring `rcr_score`, vacancies, events, applications, one-time CV token hashed with `password_hash`, CV under `customer/storage/rc/`, MIME-checked; evaluation `rcr_eval_*` with criteria in `cp_settings.rc_eval_criteria` normalised to 100 %, interviews `rcr_interview_*`, ranking, retention `rcr_retention_run`), `app/rcr_actions.php` (actions `rcr_*`, whitelisted in `HR_ACTIONS`), `app/web.php` (`web_*`). Views `rcr_dashboard|vacancy|event|application|sets|settings|cv`, `admin_website`, `web_inbox`. Access: `rcr_can()` = `hr_can()`; `rcr_can_view($vacancy)` adds the hiring manager. Tables `rc_*`, `web_inquiries`; migrations r89_001, r90_001, r90_002, r91_001 with checks `r89a` (`rc_vacancies`), `r90a` (`cp_settings.web_site_key`), `r90b` (`web_inquiries`), `r91a` (`rc_evaluations`).
**R91-002 hygiene.** (1) The updater applies a migration only when the patch has a `check` key that `cp_migration_state()` reports false and resolves `db` against `customer/` — R88-001 had a bare file name, so `migrate_cp_r88_001_…` was reported *file missing* on 2026-11-06 and the live schema had none of the R88 tables (the code guards with `*_has()`, so the features were silently off). Path fixed; with `r88a` false live, the updater applies it with this release. The four new patches got their checks (they had `null`, which would have skipped them too). (2) Site keys were only ever shown masked → the administrator could not copy them; now `$_SESSION['rc_key_once']` / `web_key_once` shows the full key once on the next page view (`hash_equals` against the stored value). (3) 245 `rc.*` / `web.*` keys were English in EL/IT/BG/UK → translated (8146 keys × 5). (4) Rate-limit files moved from `<webroot>/storage` to `customer/storage/{rc,web}/rl`. (5) `tools/` is excluded from the deploy zip, so `tools/cron.php` never reaches the server → recruiting retention now runs once a day from `nf_sweep()` (`rc_retention_last`). (6) `rcr_vacancy` / `rcr_event` with an unknown id → 404 instead of a TypeError. Manuals: HR Handbook › Recruiting, Golden manual › Recruiting + Website. Tests: `rtest.php` 100 checks 0 FAIL (settings, key once/masked, roles, vacancy/event creation, feed reads without scores or join links, apply pass/fail/knock-out/duplicate/validation/closed, CV token wrong/one-time/MIME, event capacity and duplicates, talent pool, write rate limit, hiring-manager access and its revocation, scorecards incl. refusal, interviews, ranking, status e-mails, criteria editor, retention, web feed counts/ticket/inbox, page sweep, clean error log) plus the real website proxy (`goldenas-website/api/hr.php`) against the sandbox: apply 201 → CV 200 → duplicate 409.
**Live steps after deploy (administrator):** HR › Recruiting › ⚙ → *Create the site key* → copy it once into the website repository secret `PORTAL_RC_KEY` (Settings › Secrets) → redeploy the website; set *Candidate e-mail From* so invitations send. Settings › System › Website → *Create the key* → secret `PORTAL_STATS_KEY` for the weekly fleet-stats job. Settings › About should list R88-001 … R91-002 as applied.


## 249. v1.108.3 — Recruiting on the live schema; CSP-clean Website inbox (GAS-CP-R91-004)
**Symptom on live:** HR › Recruiting listed no vacancies and `rcr_feed.php?r=vacancies` answered `500 server error` (so goldenas.com.gr/careers fell back to the demo data even after `PORTAL_RC_KEY` was set), while `?r=events` worked. **Cause:** R56-002 (v1.62.0) merged `hr_departments` into `disciplines` and dropped the table on live; the sandbox still had it (re-created by hand when the r54 migration was replayed), so `rcr_vacancies()`, `rcr_vacancy()`, `rcr_pub_vacancies()` and the department select in `views/rcr_vacancy.php` passed every test there and failed live. **Fix:** the three queries join `disciplines d` and the form uses `hr_departments()` (already backed by Disciplines). No SQL — `rc_vacancies.department_id` has no foreign key, existing ids are discipline ids. The harness now also reads `disciplines`; `hr_departments` was dropped in the sandbox to match live → 100 checks, 0 FAIL. **Also:** `views/web_inbox.php` filter selects used `onchange="this.form.submit()"` — blocked by the CSP (no inline handlers) — replaced by the existing `data-js-submit="1"` behaviour of `assets/portal.js`; a repo-wide grep finds no `on*=` attribute left in the views. **Security review:** no new routes, no schema change, the public feed still exposes only published vacancies/events; the CSP fix removes the last inline-handler exception rather than loosening the policy. Manuals (Golden, HR) updated; no new language keys.

## 250. v1.109.0 — Assistant drafts vacancies (GAS-CP-R92-001)
**What:** on `?p=rcr_vacancy&new=1` / `&edit=1` an "Assistant — draft the vacancy" card (shown when `ai_can('rcr_vacancy')`): HR types a brief, POST `rcr_vacancy_ai` → `rcr_vacancy_ai_draft($id, $brief, $_POST)` → `ai_run('fill', fields = rcr_vacancy_ai_fields(current form values), …, 'rcr_vacancy')` → the answer is validated (select values must be listed options, `description_html`/`summary` passed through `strip_tags`, length caps) and kept in `$_SESSION['rc_vac_ai'][$id]`; the view merges it into the form values and shows "draft in the form — nothing saved until Save"; `rcr_vacancy_ai_clear` discards; `rcr_vacancy_save` drops it. Same no-JS, CSP-safe pattern as the evaluation draft (`hr_ev_ai`). `ai_all_pages()` gains `rcr_vacancy` (label `rc.title`) so the administrator can enable it in Settings › Assistant; both actions are in `HR_ACTIONS`. The prompt carries the company context (marine electrical / automation / navigation integrator, Keratsini, IT/NL branches) and the house style (British English, factual, no superlatives); the current form values go as `<form>` data; **no candidate, employee or payroll data is ever included.** The recruiting forms (`rcr_vacancy`, `rcr_event`, `rcr_settings`, `rcr_sets`) now use the portal `.field` blocks instead of bare `<label>` wrappers. 7 keys × 5 languages (8157 each). Sandbox: mock model endpoint through `cfg('ai.base_url')`; draft applied, `<script>` stripped, engineer (non-HR) refused and not logged, Save clears the draft; harness 100/100; screenshots checked. **Security review:** reuses the assistant's gate (`ai_can`: staff + `can_ai` + page enabled + key), per-user rate limit and monthly cost cap; every returned value is whitelisted/sanitised before it reaches the form; no new public route; CSRF on both actions.

## 251. v1.109.1 — Vacancy code shown on the form (GAS-CP-R92-002)
`views/rcr_vacancy.php`: read-only **Code** field (`$v['code']`, or `rcr_code('VAC','rc_vacancies')` as a preview on a new vacancy — the real code is still assigned by `rcr_vacancy_save()` at INSERT, so two HR users opening the form at once cannot collide); the former "Vacancy *" input is now **Job title**. Keys `rc.job_title`, `rc.code_auto` × 5 languages (8159 each); HR manual. No SQL, no new action; the code input has no `name` so nothing from it is posted.

## 252. v1.109.2 — Screening set for Project Managers (GAS-CP-R92-003)
`install/migrate_cp_r92_003_pm_screening_set.sql` (`INSERT IGNORE` into `rc_screening_sets`, check `r92c` = row `PM-STD-8` exists): 8 questions, groups planning / stakeholders / cost / risk / class / team / loyalty×2, knock-out `loyalty`, pass 7. Each question has two scoring answers and one zero answer, as in ENG-STD-7, so the gate measures mindset rather than knowledge; scores stay server-side (`rcr_set_public` strips them — verified on `?r=set&code=PM-STD-8`). The website renders the set named by the vacancy; only its offline demo fallback hard-codes ENG-STD-7. HR manual; self-check line. **Security:** data-only migration, no code path change.

## 253. v1.109.3 — Ten role screening sets (GAS-CP-R92-004)
`install/migrate_cp_r92_004_role_screening_sets.sql` (one `INSERT IGNORE … VALUES` with ten rows; check `r92d` = `SRV-STD-8` exists; self-check counts 10/10). Generated from a Python definition (scratchpad `sets.py`) so every set has the same shape: six role situations (groups per role, e.g. security/ops/backup for IT, safety/method/code/protocols/class/team for automation, integrity/leadtime/cost for procurement, honesty/technical/pipeline/negotiation for sales) + the two shared loyalty questions, each question two scoring options and one zero option, pass 7, knock-out `loyalty`. **Gotcha:** the JSON is a quoted SQL string — a `"` inside a question text must reach MySQL as `\"`, i.e. the generator doubles backslashes (`\\"`); without it the CHECK on the JSON column fails and `INSERT IGNORE` silently drops the row (IT and SRV were missing on the first sandbox run). Apply with a utf8mb4 client (the updater uses PDO utf8mb4; the CLI needs `--default-character-set=utf8mb4` or the ellipses turn to mojibake). Verified on `?r=set&code=…`: 8 questions each, no scores, correct UTF-8. HR manual, self-check. **Security:** data-only migration.

## 254. v1.110.0 — Candidate e-mails through the e-mail card; HR notification group (GAS-CP-R92-006)
**E-mail card on the application** (`views/rcr_application.php`): the bare `mailto:` ✉ became `em_card_html()` (R31-008 card) with `people` = the candidate (ticked), `kinds` = five new template kinds `rc_general / rc_shortlisted / rc_interview / rc_offer / rc_rejected` (default chosen from `status`), `ctx` no = APP code, title = vacancy title, doc = VAC code, name/email = candidate, `locale` = candidate `lang` when it is a portal locale; hidden `rc_app` → `em_draft_from_post()` logs `email_drafted` in `rc_log` after `rcr_can_view()` of the vacancy. `app/et.php`: kinds added to `ET_KINDS` and `et_builtin()`; `et.k_rc_*`, `et.d_s_rc_*`, `et.d_b_rc_*` × 5 languages (8174 keys) — the bodies leave blanks for date / duration / format / decision date; placeholders `{name} {title} {no} {doc} {user} {branch}`. `em_draft` added to `HR_ACTIONS` so hr_only users can use the card. **HR notification group** (`app/nf.php`): `nf_groups()` + `'hr'`, `NF_GROUP_OF` hrq/hrm/hrt/hrev/hra/rc → `hr`, counts and pill tone (blue); `install/migrate_cp_r92_005_hr_notification_group.sql` retags the stored rows (check `r92e` = no HR kind left under `work`; self-check line). Application page converted to `.field` blocks. Harness 100/100; sandbox: card renders, draft .eml `To:` candidate / `X-Unsent: 1`, log row written, Notifications page shows an HR tab with the recruiting items. **Security review:** recipients are validated e-mail addresses only, the draft is built server-side as before; the log hook checks the user may see that application; no new public route; the mail-app path carries no attachments by design.
**Merge note (R92-005 / v1.109.4):** a parallel session pushed "CV retention controls" (per-application Keep with reason and expiry, talent-pool consent, separate pool retention, CV copied to the employee file on hire — `migrate_cp_r92_005_cv_retention_controls.sql`, action `rcr_keep`) labelled R91-004 / 1.108.3 with `check => null`, so the deploy that picked it up (`portal 1.109.3 -> 1.109.3`) did not run its ALTER while `views/rcr_application.php` already read `keep_until`. Re-labelled here as GAS-CP-R92-005 v1.109.4 with check `r92f` (`keep_until` column) and a self-check line; applied by the 1.110.0 deploy. Rule restated: every patch with `db` needs a `check` key in `cp_migration_state()`, and the version constant must move.

## 255. v1.110.1 — Event code shown on the form (GAS-CP-R92-007)
`views/rcr_event.php`: read-only **Code** field (`$ev['code']`, or `rcr_code('EVT','rc_events')` as preview; the real code is assigned at INSERT by `rcr_event_save()`), title input renamed **Event title**. Keys `rc.event_title`, `rc.code_auto_evt` × 5 (8190 each). No SQL; the code input has no `name`.

## 256. v1.110.2 — Website cache purge on save; Athens offset in the public feeds (GAS-CP-R92-008)
**Symptom:** an event's duration changed in the portal but goldenas.com.gr/careers kept the old value — the website proxy `api/hr.php` cached reads 5 min (+ `max-age=120` in the browser). **Second finding:** `rcr_feed.php` / `web_feed.php` are separate entry points and never called `date_default_timezone_set(tz_app())` (index.php does), so `date('c', …)` stamped event times `+00:00`: an 11:00 Athens event was shown as 13:00 by the site. **Fix (portal):** both feeds set the app time zone after loading modules; `rcr_site_purge()` in `app/rcr.php` is called from the four save/insert return points of `rcr_vacancy_save()` / `rcr_event_save()` — `POST {rc_site_origin}/api/hr.php?r=purge` with `X-Site-Key: rc_site_key`, 2 s timeout, never throws, origin must be `https://host` (loopback allowed for the sandbox). **Fix (website R12, spec R4):** `api/hr.php` purge route before the same-origin check — POST only, rate-limited, `hash_equals` on the shared key, deletes `api/_rl/cache/*.json`, returns `{purged:n}`; read cache 2 min, browser 30 s. Sandbox end-to-end with the website served on 8095 against the sandbox portal: cached 90 → save 60 → purge 200 → read 60 with `+02:00`; wrong key → 403. **Security review:** the purge is authenticated with the key the website already holds, idempotent and only deletes its own cache files; the portal call is outbound only with TLS verification; no new inbound route on the portal. Help: HR manual.

## 257. v1.111.0 — Work permits per main project (GAS-CP-R93-001)
**Module `app/pmt.php`** (`pmt_` prefix — `wp_` was taken), table `work_permits` (`install/migrate_cp_r93_001_work_permits.sql`, check `r93a`): project_id, person_id (hr_people, optional), holder_name, permit_type (`PMT_TYPES`: work_permit / residence / visa / a1 / yard_pass / port_pass / other), permit_no, authority, country (ISO-2), issue_date, expiry_date, status (`active / renewal / cancelled`), notes, one document (`storage/permits/YYYY/…`, pdf / jpg / png / webp ≤ 15 MB, MIME checked). State is derived: `pmt_state()` = expired / soon (≤ `PMT_WARN_DAYS` = 30) / renewal / valid / cancelled; `pmt_pill()`. **Pages** `permits` (KPI row expired / soon / renewal / all, tabs, project + search filters, table) and `permit_edit` (form, upload, delete with `data-confirm`), `permit_file` streams the document (`X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`). **Actions** `pmt_save`, `pmt_delete` in `pm_actions.php`. **Rights:** `pmt_can_view()` = staff; `pmt_can_edit($pid)` = admin ∨ `hr_can()` ∨ the project's `pm_user_id` (any PM for a new row without project). HR-only users: pages in `HR_PAGES`, actions in `HR_ACTIONS`, item in their sidebar; Fleet group gets a 🪪 item for staff. **Projects page:** `pmt_project_counts()` once for the tree → `pmt_project_pill()` in the summary line (red if expired, gold if expiring, green otherwise, title shows the next expiry) and `pmt_card_html()` inside the open project (only expired / soon / renewal, limit 6, + New permit when the user may edit); 🪪 button in the project action row. **Warnings** `pmt_remind()` from `nf_sweep()` (after `td_remind`): permits not cancelled with expiry within +60 / −30 days; thresholds 60 / 30 / 14 / 7 / 0 → `ref_key wkp:<id>:e<th>`, lapsed → `wkp:<id>:x<YYYYMM>` (monthly); recipients PM + users whose `staff_roles.scope ∈ {hr, owner}` + `nf_admins()`; de-duplicated on `ref_key` regardless of read state (nf_push alone would re-create a read notification every sweep); changing the expiry deletes the permit's notifications so the ladder restarts; kind `wkp` → group `work`. 66 keys × 5 languages (8256 each). Sandbox: project seeded (the sandbox had none — `projects.status` enum is `quoted/active/onhold/completed`, not `on_hold`), four permits created through the form as the PM (kostas), list / tabs / pills / file 200 / tree pill "🪪 4 · 1 expired · 1 expiring" / attention card; sweep → 9 notifications (3 permits × PM, HR, admin), second and third sweep 0; screenshots checked. **Security review:** staff-only pages with per-project edit rights, CSRF on both actions, upload extension + MIME whitelist and size cap, random stored names outside the web root, file streamed with nosniff; no public route. **Not included:** GoldenPM app view (the permits are visible in the portal only for now).

## 258. v1.111.1 — Website contact form in the Website inbox (GAS-CP-R93-002)
**Report:** "no e-mails from the contact form in the Website inbox". Two causes: (1) `api/contact.php` on the website only mailed sales@ — only `ticket.php` and `portal-access.php` called `gas_portal_post()`; (2) every hand-over needs `PORTAL_STATS_KEY` (= the portal's `web_site_key`, Settings › System › Website) in the website secrets, which has not been created yet, so nothing reached the inbox at all. **Fix (portal):** `web_inquiries.kind` ENUM gains `contact` (`install/migrate_cp_r93_002_web_contact.sql`, check `r93b` via information_schema); `web_pub_contact()` in `app/web.php` (name / email / message required, topic → subject prefix, dedupe 1 h on email+message, `system_kind` holds the topic); route `?r=contact` in `web_feed.php`; `web_notify_ids('contact')` = staff with `can_sales` or admins; inbox filter, row and detail head show ✉ Contact form; keys `web.kind_contact`, `web.nf_contact` × 5. **Fix (website R13):** `contact.php` calls `gas_portal_post('contact', …)` after a successful mail and returns the WEB ref. Sandbox: POST `?r=contact` → 201 `WEB-2026-0004`, inbox row and notification present. **Security:** same key check and per-IP limits as the other routes; input lengths capped; no HTML stored.

## 259. v1.111.2 — Delete Website inbox messages (GAS-CP-R93-003)
`app/web.php` action `web_inq_delete` — `require_staff()` + `is_cp_admin()` (others get the no-access flash; verified with the engineer user), deletes the `web_inquiries` row and the notifications with `ref_key = web:<id>`, flashes `web.deleted`; `views/web_inbox.php` shows 🗑 Delete (`data-confirm`) on the message for administrators only. Keys `web.delete_confirm`, `web.deleted` × 5 (8260 each). No SQL. **Security:** CSRF, admin-only server-side check (the button is only cosmetic), nothing else removed.

## 260. v1.111.3 — Delete files on the Files tab (GAS-CP-R93-004)
`app/pm_actions.php` action `file_delete`: `require_staff()` + `is_cp_admin()`, file must belong to the posted `project_id`, kinds `photo` / `fsr_photo` / `ga` refused (`fl.delete_linked` — they are owned by notes, FSRs and GA overlays), `cp_delete_file()` removes row + stored file, `sp_log('file_deleted')`, flash `fl.deleted`, `$back()`. `views/subproject.php` Files tab: extra column with a 🗑 form (`data-confirm`) for administrators only. Keys `fl.delete_confirm`, `fl.deleted`, `fl.delete_linked` × 5 (8263). Sandbox: upload → button present for admin, absent for staff → staff POST refused (row kept) → admin delete removes row and file. **Security:** CSRF, admin-only server-side, project scoping of the id.

## 261. v1.112.0 — Vessel › Documents tab (GAS-CP-R93-005)
**Module `app/vd.php`:** `vd_collect($vesselId, $staff, $f)` unions (1) `sp_files` of every project of the vessel (generated report PDFs, FSR / ISR documents, offer PDFs, drawings, test and class documents, photos …) — customers only `visibility='external'`, `fsr_sign` hidden from them; (2) `ticket_files` of the vessel's tickets (customers: own `customer_id` only); (3) `inv_delivery_notes` for staff with `dn_can()`. Grouping = the file kind's archive tab (`fk_tab()`), `offer` → Offers, `ga` → Drawings, unknown → Files; `VD_GROUPS` fixes the order (reports, service-reports, inspections, drawings, owner-approvals, class, tests, weekly-notes, offers, files, tickets, delivery-notes). Filters: project (the page's existing selector), group chip `g`, text `q`, `year`. Note `sp_files.archived_at` means "numbered into the document archive" (ar.php), not superseded — it is **not** a filter. **View** `views/vessel_view.php`: tab `documents` with count, year + search form, group chips with counts, one `<details>` table per group (document, doc no / rev with ↗ to the owning report / offer / ticket, kind, project · section, date, by, visibility for staff). Side fix: the tickets card (default branch) and `ar_issued_html()` box used to render under the Equipment / Invoicing / Documents tabs too — now excluded. 21 keys × 5 (8282). Sandbox: 8 files of 7 kinds + customer user → admin sees 8 in 7 groups, customer 6 in 6 groups with no internal rows; search works; screenshot checked. **Security:** read-only; visibility and customer scoping applied in SQL; links reuse the existing guarded routes (`file`, `tfile`, `delivery_note`).

## 262. v1.113.0 — BOM: delete, import template, staged import with inventory cross-check and AI proposals (GAS-CP-R93-006)
**Delete:** `bom_can_delete()` = admin ∨ `bom_pr_can()`; `bom_delete()` removes changes, lines, events, staged rows and the revision, refuses an approved revision with a `to_pr` / `to_kit` event; action `bom_delete`, 🗑 on the BOM page head; `sp_log('bom_deleted')`. **Template:** route `bom_template` → `bom_import_template()` (sheets BOM / Units / How to). **Staged import** (`install/migrate_cp_r93_006_bom_import_review.sql`, table `bom_import_rows`, check `r93c`): `bom_import_stage()` finds the header row within the first 10 lines (≥ 2 known headers, template names + the old aliases), requires a Description column, matches each row: Golden code → `code` 100; `factory_code` exact (maker preferred) → `part_no` 95; normalised part no (alphanumerics only, PHP-side map built once — no `REGEXP_REPLACE`, works on any MySQL) → `part_no_norm` 85; else `none`. Unmatched rows go to `bom_import_ai()` when `ai_can('bom')`: `bom_imp_candidates()` (up to 8 items by maker / description / part-no tokens with on-hand) → new AI kind **`match`** (`AI_KINDS`, system prompt in `ai_system()`, `$outp['matches']` parsed in `ai_run()`) in batches of 25, max 150 rows; a proposal is kept only if it is one of the offered candidates and confidence ≥ 50 (`match_kind ai`, reason prefixed "AI:"). `ai_all_pages()` gains `bom`. **Review** (`views/bom.php`, when the draft has staged rows): from-the-file / inventory proposal (code, confidence pill, kind pill, description, maker + pn, on hand, reserved, status, reason) / decision radios (Accept disabled when nothing is proposed; Reject = free text; New inventory item; Decide later); "pick another item" uses `prq_items` and enables Accept; bulk buttons by confidence; Save decisions / Apply (`bom_import_decide` with `apply=1` → `bom_import_apply()`): accept → line with item (unit / maker / pn from the item), reject → free-text line, new → `bom_imp_create_item()` (code = Golden code if free, else maker part no if free, else `BOM-<rev>-<seq>`, status active, note "Created from BOM #n by …", **no `require_pr_admin`** — the engineer may create items from here by design) then line; pending rows stay; `bom_import_discard`. Legacy `bom_import()` remains as fallback when the table is missing. 45 keys × 5 (8327). Sandbox with a mock model: template downloaded and filled (6 rows) → 3 deterministic matches (code / pn / pn≈), 1 AI proposal (62 %), 1 no candidate; manual pick + accept, reject, new item → 6 lines, item FAR-2228 created, 0 pending; engineer delete refused, admin delete removed everything; screenshot checked. **Security review:** all actions behind `bom_editable()` (engineer, draft) or admin / procurement; AI receives only BOM row text and inventory candidates (no prices, no personal data) and its answer is whitelisted against the candidate ids; CSRF everywhere; the item picker reuses the guarded `prq_items` endpoint.

## 263. v1.113.1 — Sub-project comments internal by default (GAS-CP-R93-007)
`views/subproject.php` Comments tab: the staff visibility selector lists Internal note first and selected; `app/pm_actions.php` `cm_post`: `$vis = is_staff() ? (visibility === 'external' ? 'external' : 'internal') : 'external'` — a missing or unknown value from a staff user is internal (previously external). `assets/portal.js` reply handler: sets `#cm-vis` from the parent `.msg` (`internal` class → internal, else external) so an answer to the owner still reaches the owner. Verified: staff POST without the field → internal, with `external` → external. Golden manual.

## 264. v1.113.2 — Archive path length (GAS-CP-R93-008)
PHP error log 9 Nov 11:55: `archive: Unsafe archive path: customers/3245_STREAMLINED-…/PRJ.000800/Y.002_Rectification-of-Engineers-…` — `ar_safe_rel()` refused any relative path over 230 characters; customer dir (code + 80-char name) + project + sub-project (code + 80-char title) + tab + 110-char file name exceed it. The exception is caught in `ar_file()` (file stays at its upload location, not filed). Fix: total limit 1500, per-component limit 200 (file systems cap a name at 255 bytes; our components are ≤ 110). Existing unfiled files: Settings › Archive › **Reorganise** (`ar_reorganise`) picks them up. The `hr_departments` fatals in the same log are from 8 Nov, before R91-004 (fixed).

## 265. v1.113.3 — Projects tree: dark-mode header, folds, sub-project order (GAS-CP-R93-009)
`.vg-btn` used `--cp-navy` (constant) → invisible on the dark theme; now `--navy-text` (themed). Offers block wrapped in `<details class="fold" data-fold="offers-<pid>">` (closed by default), sub-project list in `<details class="fold subs-fold" open data-fold="subs-<pid>">`; `portal.js` remembers `details[data-fold]` state in `localStorage` (`cp.fold.<key>`); CSS `details.fold` summary with rotating ▸. `pm.php` both sub-project queries: `ORDER BY SUBSTRING_INDEX(code,'.',1), CAST(SUBSTRING_INDEX(code,'.',-1) AS UNSIGNED), code, name` (was `sort_order, code` — new sub-projects got sort_order 0 and jumped to the top). Key `pr.subprojects` × 5. Golden manual.

## 266. v1.114.0 — Invoicing control: direct invoices, computed sub-project invoicing, hidden completed sub-projects, accounting checks (GAS-CP-R94-001)

**Why:** an invoice is sometimes issued for a work without a proforma; it had no place in the portal, so it was missing from the statement of account and the collections. The sub-project invoicing pill was typed by hand and drifted from the documents; completed sub-projects cluttered the tree and the pickers but their invoicing still had to be chased.

**Direct invoices.** `proformas.kind ENUM('proforma','invoice')`, `offer_id` now nullable, `customer_id` stored on the row. `pi_direct_create_from_post()` (`app/pi.php`) registers a row kind `invoice`, status `sent`, `pi_no` = the ERP invoice number (unique), project required (vessel / customer follow the project), optional sub-project, invoicing identity, PDF (`cp_store_file`, kind `issued`), notes. View `views/invoice_new.php` (`?p=invoice_new`, button on Proforma invoices and Collections), action `pi_direct_create`. All PI joins to `offers` became `LEFT JOIN`; `of_event()` ignores id 0; payments on a direct invoice are capped at its own gross; "invoices from accounting" is refused / hidden on it (the row is the invoice). Statement PDF prints the kind before the description; the Excel has a Kind column; the PDF of a direct invoice is titled *Invoice*.

**Computed invoicing.** `inv_compute_many()` / `inv_compute()` / `inv_apply()` in `app/pm.php`: contract = approved offers of the sub-project (gross, budgetary and converted offers excluded); invoiced = Σ `proforma_invoices.amount` on its proformas + gross of its direct invoices; state none / partial / full (%). `subprojects.invoicing_override ENUM(none,partial,full) NULL` — the form offers *Computed (…)* plus the three overrides; the legacy `invoicing` / `invoiced_pct` columns are still written for old readers.

**Completed sub-projects.** `sp_pick_cond($alias, $keep)` filters every sub-project picker (offers, proformas, tasks, tickets, timesheets, FSR / ISR, drawings, risks, function checks, weekly forecast, direct invoices) while keeping the current value; the tree hides them unless `&sc=1` and shows *n completed hidden ›* / *Hide completed*.

**Accounting checks** (`views/collections.php`, last card; `?format=checks` → xlsx via `col_checks_xlsx()`): `pi_check_coverage()` (every sub-project incl. completed — contract, invoiced, %, collected, state; filters project / status / state, default "not fully invoiced" which also includes invoiced sub-projects without an approved offer), `pi_check_mismatch()` (non-draft, non-cancelled proformas whose registered invoices ≠ gross), `pi_check_uninvoiced($days)` (issued proformas older than N days with no invoice).

**Security:** direct invoices need `pi_can_invoices()`; the invoice number is a plain string, unique on `uq_pi_no`; file upload through `cp_store_file` (type / size checks as every portal file); all new HTML escaped, inline script carries the CSP nonce. No new secrets, no new endpoints reachable without staff login.

**Migration:** `install/migrate_cp_r94_001_direct_invoices.sql`, check `r94a` (`proformas.kind`). Lang keys +45 per file (8378).

## 267. v1.114.1 — Service reports: specialty = technical discipline, tariff follows it (GAS-CP-R94-002)

**Why:** the time-line *Specialty* of a service report was a free-text datalist of nine fixed labels; accounting mapped it to a discipline by the first six letters, so an electrician booking automation hours could not get the automation tariff.

**Change:** `fsr_specialties()` (`app/fsr.php`) now returns the names of the active disciplines ticked in Settings › Disciplines › *Service reports* (`cp_setting fsr_disciplines`, action `fsr_discs_save`); nothing ticked = every active discipline with `sort_order < 900` (the HR departments imported at 900+ by R56-002 are left out). `fsr_specialty_default($userId)` pre-fills the engineer's own discipline. `acc.php` pricing matches the specialty to a discipline by exact name first (then the legacy fuzzy match), so the chosen discipline's rate card wins over the engineer's default. The API master data (`api/v1/lib/portal.php`) already served `fsr_specialties()`, so the Service Engineer app gets the same list; old reports keep their legacy labels (`fsr_specialties_legacy()` is the fallback when the disciplines table is empty). No DB step. Lang +4 keys × 5 (8382).

**Security:** the setting is admin-only (`is_cp_admin()`); the specialty stays a bounded string (80 chars) — no new input surface.

## 268. v1.114.2 — Direct invoice from the checks, the accounting report and the service report; specialty shows its rate card (GAS-CP-R94-003)

**Entry points to `?p=invoice_new`:** `&project=&sub=&gross=` from the coverage table of Collections › Accounting checks (gross still to invoice = contract − invoiced; the form derives the net from the VAT % until the net is typed — `data-gross` on the net input); `&acc=<acc_report id>` from an issued / sent / partly-invoiced accounting report (project, sub-project, net = `total_net`, title pre-filled; hidden `acc_report_id`); `&fsr=<id>` from an accepted service report (project, sub-project, title). On creation with `acc_report_id`, `pi_direct_create_from_post()` calls the new `acc_mark_invoiced($R, $ref, $date, $note, $ids)` (factored out of `acc_answer_from_post()`), so the report's reported lines turn invoiced under the invoice number and the report status becomes invoiced / partly_invoiced — the same effect as the accounting answer.

**Specialty ↔ rate card:** `fsr_specialty_options($projectId, $withRates)` returns each offered discipline with its card (`acc_card_of`), the card name from the current rate list (`rl_current` by branch of the project, `rl_cards`) and the normal local hourly rate (`acc_rate_item` → `rl_price`) when `$withRates`; `fsr_specialty_label()` renders "Automation · card D — Automation engineer · 95 EUR/h". `views/fsr_edit.php` uses a `<select>` (value = discipline name; a legacy label on an old report is kept as an extra option). API `/master` gains `specialty_options` (name, card, card_name, label); the PWA (`mobile/js/reports.js`, 0.6.20) shows the labels and keeps the name as value.

**Security:** entry points only pre-fill; creation keeps the `pi_can_invoices()` check, and the accounting link-back additionally requires `acc_is_accounting()`. Lang +14 keys × 5 (8396). No DB step.

## 269. v1.114.3 — Specialty: rate-card-only specialists (Atlas, Magnus, Aventics …) (GAS-CP-R94-004)

`fsr_specialty_options()` now appends, after the technical disciplines, every hourly card of the current rate list (`rl_cards`) whose card is not already used by a discipline — named after the card (`card_name`). `fsr_disc_names()` keeps the discipline-only list (Settings tick); `fsr_specialties()` = names of all options (API `/master` and the PWA follow). `fsr_specialty_card($specialty, $projectId)` resolves a specialty (discipline name or card name) to its card. In `acc_collect_fsr()` a line whose specialty names a card gets `rate_item_id` = `acc_rate_item(list, card, hour_type, 'local')` at registration, so `acc_price_line()` prices it from that card regardless of the engineer's discipline (the row carries local and overseas prices, zone is applied at pricing). Manuals (Golden FSR, Staff). No DB step, no new keys.

## 270. v1.114.4 — Website inquiries → tickets for PMs, management, sales (GAS-CP-R94-005)

All three website forms already post to the inbox (`api/ticket.php` → kind ticket, `api/contact.php` → contact since R93-002, `api/portal-access.php` → access; careers go to Recruiting). `web_make_ticket($id, $vesselId, $category, $priority)` (`app/web.php`) now accepts every kind: vessel optional (NULL vessel / customer → internal ticket, `created_side = golden`; a support request with a vessel keeps `customer`), category validated against `tk_categories()` with per-kind default (`web_default_category()`), priority against `tk_priorities()` (support-request priority mapped as before), title = subject or "<kind> — sender (company)", description carries sender / company / e-mail / phone / location / system or role + vessels; `rs_cover_ticket` only when a vessel is set. `web_can_convert()` = admin ∨ `mgmt_can()` ∨ `pm_is_pm()` ∨ `pi_is_sales()` ∨ `can_works`; enforced in the function and the view. `views/web_inbox.php` shows the conversion card (vessel pre-matched from the inquiry's vessel text, category, priority) on every unconverted message for converters. Lang +2 × 5 (8398). No DB step.

**Security:** conversion now checks `web_can_convert()` server-side (before, any staff could convert support requests); inputs validated against whitelists; no new endpoints.

## 271. v1.114.5 — Fix: `$pid` undefined in the FSR task block (GAS-CP-R94-006)

The specialty `<select>` added in §268 computed `fsr_specialty_options($pid …)` inside `$taskBlock`, a closure that does not `use ($pid)` → "PHP Warning: Undefined variable $pid … fsr_edit.php:124" on every form render (the list still rendered, without the project's branch rate list). The options are now built once before the closure and passed with `use ($spOpts, $spNames)`. No functional change otherwise.

## 272. v1.115.0 — Outgoing e-mail via Microsoft Graph, OAuth2 client credentials (GAS-CP-R94-007)

**Why:** goldenas.com.gr SPF is Microsoft-only; PHP `mail()` lands in junk, and authenticated SMTP needs a mailbox app password (not available with Security Defaults, and a password at rest on the host).
