**Portal (`app/mailer.php`):** `cp_graph_configured()` (config `graph.tenant`, `graph.client_id`, `graph.client_secret`, `graph.from`), `cp_graph_token()` (client-credentials token from `login.microsoftonline.com/<tenant>/oauth2/v2.0/token`, scope `https://graph.microsoft.com/.default`, cached in `sys_get_temp_dir()/gascp-graph-<hash>.json` 0600, refreshed 60 s before expiry), `cp_graph_send()` (`POST /v1.0/users/<from>/sendMail`, text body, Reply-To, optional ≤3 MB attachments, `saveToSentItems`, one retry on 401). `cp_mail()` order: Graph → SMTP → `mail()`; `cp_mail_configured()` true for Graph or SMTP; `cp_mail_transport()` graph / smtp / mail; `cp_mail_from()`. Settings › System › Website card shows the transport and offers `web_mail_test` (admin only, sends to the administrator's own address, result flashed). `config.sample.php` documents the block.

**Website (`goldenas-website` R14, `api/_lib.php`):** `gas_graph_token()` / `gas_graph_send()` with `GRAPH_TENANT`, `GRAPH_CLIENT_ID`, `GRAPH_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GRAPH_FROM` in `api/config.php`; `gas_mail()` tries Graph first, then SMTP, then `mail()`.

**Entra setup (admin, once):** App registration "Golden A/S Portal mailer" (single tenant) → Certificates & secrets → new client secret (24 months; note the VALUE) → API permissions → Microsoft Graph → Application permissions → `Mail.Send` → Grant admin consent. Exchange Online PowerShell: `New-ApplicationAccessPolicy -AppId <client id> -PolicyScopeGroupId <mail-enabled security group containing website@goldenas.com.gr> -AccessRight RestrictAccess -Description "Portal mailer"` (`Test-ApplicationAccessPolicy` to verify). Then fill the graph block on the server and press the test button.

**Security:** no mailbox credential anywhere; the app can send only as the policy-scoped mailbox; secret in `config.php` (git-ignored, outside the deploy zip); tokens 0600 in temp, never logged; Graph error bodies logged truncated without the token. Lang +7 keys × 5 (8405).

## 273. v1.116.1 — Register line engineer correction; FSR engineer pre-fill only for technical staff (GAS-CP-R94-008)

**Symptom (PRJ.000486.Y.002 / Y.003, S/Y ARIA):** reports prepared by office staff on behalf of the engineers carried the preparer's name on the first time line (the form pre-filled `$u['full_name']`), so `acc_collect_fsr()` registered the hours under her name.

**Fix:** `fsr_user_is_technical()` (user's discipline `sort_order < 900`, or no discipline) gates the pre-fill; `fsr_engineer_choices()` (active staff of technical disciplines, non-HR roles, current user first) feeds the Engineer datalist. `acc_line_set_engineer($lineId, $name)` (`app/acc.php`, action `acc_line_engineer`, accounting or preparers): FSR-source lines in state open / reported only; sets `user_name`, `user_id` (matched by full name), `discipline_id` = the engineer's unless the line's specialty names a discipline; clears `rate_item_id` / `unit_price` / `value` for re-pricing unless the specialty pins a rate card; logs `line_engineer` on the report. `views/acc_register.php` shows ✎ → inline form with the engineer datalist. CSS `details.inline-edit`. Lang +4 × 5 (8409). No DB step.

## 274. v1.116.2 — Bulk re-attribution of register lines (GAS-CP-R94-009)

`acc_reattribute($projectId, $subId, $from, $to)` (admin only) loops `acc_line_set_engineer()` over every FSR-source `acc_lines` row of the project (optional sub-project) whose `user_name` equals `$from` (case-insensitive); `acc_line_set_engineer()` now lets administrators touch invoiced lines (everyone else still gets `acc.eng_locked`). Register view: collapsible card above the lines when a project filter is set and the user is an administrator — sub-project select, "registered under" select built from the project's distinct names with counts, engineer datalist, `data-confirm`. Action `acc_reattribute`. Lang +7 × 5 (8506). No DB step. Used for PRJ.000486 (S/Y ARIA) where the preparer's name had been registered instead of the engineers'.

## 275. v1.117.0 — Phase 0 of the parties / invoicing / collections plan: matcher, import proposals, customer merge (GAS-CP-R95-002)

Decisions of 2026-11-10 (see `GAS-CP-R95-combined-analysis_R1.md`). **Matcher** `org_find($name, $vat, $erp)` (`app/org.php`): request-cached lists of active companies and customers; VAT (`org_vat`) → ERP code → `org_key` name; returns `company_id` / `customer_id` / `how` / `name`; `org_find_reset()` after a creation in the same request; `org_match_label()`. **Forms:** `cu_save_from_post`, `co_save_from_post`, `sup_save_from_post` throw `org.looks_like` on a match for a new record unless `force_new` (tick in the three forms). **`co_find()`** matches through `org_find` (a customer-only match gets its organisation via `org_ensure_for_customer`); in an import context without permission it proposes instead of creating. **Import gate:** `im_set_create()` from `create_new` in the import dispatcher (`sys_import` branch), the equipment import and the supplier import; `im_may_create()` / `im_propose()`; gated sites: `cu_import`, `co_import`, `ct_import` (via `co_find`), `pm_find_customer`, `pm_find_vessel`, `pm_import_vessels`, `eq_import`, `sup_import_apply` (returns `proposals`); the fleet import keeps its `cv` switch. `views/import.php`, `equipment.php`, `suppliers_import.php` show the tick and the "Proposed (not created)" list; counts add `proposed`, `customers_matched`, `companies_matched`. **Customers workbench:** `cu_dupes()` (VAT / ERP / key groups, keep = most projects / users / vessels) and `cu_merge($keep, $drop)` (every table with a `customer_id` column via information_schema, `UPDATE IGNORE` then delete of the refused rows, invoicing details moved with a single default, blanks of the kept record filled, duplicate deactivated and renamed, two linked companies → `org_merge`); action `cu_merge`; card on `views/organisations.php` with the vessel-duplicates count and link. Lang +21 × 5 (8526). No DB step.

**Security:** merge and the workbench are administrator-only; the matcher reads only; `force_new` and `create_new` are explicit user choices logged by the normal flash; no new endpoints.

## 276. v1.117.1 — Organisations: ERP / name matches to confirm, bulk link, bulk create (GAS-CP-R95-002b)

Live showed 322 linked / 0 to confirm / 393 customers without organisation — R85-003 had restricted proposals to VAT only. `org_matches()` now proposes ERP-code and `org_key` name matches too (how = erp / name; a company already linked to another customer is never proposed). `org_link_all($hows)` links a subset (`hows` POST field: `vat,erp` or `name`); `org_create_all()` creates an organisation (kind other, role customer) for every remaining customer via `org_create_for_customer()` (which itself links by VAT when the VAT already exists). Page: three buttons with confirmations. Lang +6 × 5 (8532).

## 277. v1.117.2 — Fix: ERP matches vs suppliers (GAS-CP-R95-002c)

§276 proposed 346 "ERP code" matches on live, almost all customer ↔ supplier (e.g. customer 4028 Aegean Shipping ↔ supplier 4028 Freshworks): SoftOne numbers customers and suppliers independently. `org_matches()` now accepts an ERP match only on an organisation that is not a supplier and carries the customer role; name matches skip suppliers too; `org_find()` ERP matching against companies requires `is_supplier = 0` and a linked customer. `org_unlink($coId)` + ⛓ Unlink on the Linked rows (admins) to undo a wrong link. Lang +3 × 5 (8535).

## 278. v1.118.0 — Phase 1: billing party, identities on organisations, ledger rows with party / customer / vessel (GAS-CP-R95-003)

**Migration** `install/migrate_cp_r95_003_billing_party.sql` (check `r95b`): `projects.billing_org_id`, `projects.invoicing_id`, `subprojects.billing_org_id`, `proformas.billing_org_id`; back-fill of `proformas.vessel_id` / `customer_id` from the project, `billing_org_id` from the identity's organisation or the customer's organisation; `projects.billing_org_id` from the customer's organisation where one exists (the rest resolves at read time and is stored on the next save).

**`app/party.php`:** `pty_has`, `pty_org_of_customer` (creates the organisation when missing), `pty_project` (stored or resolved, stored back), `pty_sub`, `pty_candidates` (customer's organisation + vessel-linked organisations via `vm_related` + current), `pty_identities` (party ★ → vessel ⚓ → other linked organisations 🏢), `pty_label`, `pty_identity_label`, `pty_project_save`, `pty_sub_save`, `pty_for_doc`. **`iv.php`:** `iv_owner_norm` (customer → organisation, contact → organisation), `iv_owner_where` (reads legacy customer / contact rows through the organisation), `iv_list` / `iv_count` use it, new rows stored normalised, `iv_migrate_owners` + `iv_pending_owner_moves` (button `org_iv_move`). **`num.php`:** `iv_choices_for_sub` party-first; `iv_for_sub` order sub choice → project choice → party default → vessel default → customer default. **`pi.php`:** `pi_set_party()` after `pi_create`, draft save and direct-invoice creation. Forms: `project_new.php` (party + identity on the project, party override on the sub-project), `proforma.php` and `invoice_new.php` pickers (labels, per-project narrowing with `data-p`), `subproject.php` and `proforma.php` show 🏛 party. Actions: `pty_project_save` in `project_save` / `project_update`, `pty_sub_save` in the three sub-project saves. Lang +13 × 5 (8547). Manuals Golden + PM.

**Security:** party / identity changes only for financial roles (`fin_visible()`), validated against the candidate lists server-side; identity rows never exposed beyond the existing owner pages.

## 279. v1.118.1 — Fix: vessel Invoicing details tab (GAS-CP-R95-003b)

`views/vessel_view.php` line 18 whitelists the `tab` parameter; R93-005 rewrote the list with `documents` and dropped `invoicing`, so `?tab=invoicing` fell back to Overview (the tab rendered, the click did nothing). Re-added.

## 280. v1.118.2 — Identity pickers: party + vessel only (GAS-CP-R95-003c)

Nikos (10/11/2026): a sub-project must not offer the identities of the other organisations linked to the vessel. `pty_identities()` returns the party's and the vessel's rows only; other organisations are reached by choosing them as billing party. `$ivr + ['src' => …]` did not override the existing `src` (array union keeps the left key) → ★ missing; now `array_replace`. `iv.sub_default` reworded.

## 281. v1.119.0 — Parties plan phase 2: one ledger (GAS-CP-R95-004)

Decisions 5–7 of 10/11/2026 (invoice = ledger document, Σ invoices ≠ proforma is a warning only, payments once) and "non-chargeable at source".

**Schema** (`install/migrate_cp_r95_004_ledger.sql`, check `r95c` = `pi_links`): `pi_links (invoice_id, proforma_id, amount, legacy_id)` unique per pair; `proformas.legacy_inv_id`; `acc_lines.invoice_id` (+ key); `fsr_lines.nc_reason`. The updater runs SQL only, so the data conversion is `pi_ledger_convert()` (app/pi.php), called from `index.php` after `i18n_boot()` while `cp_setting('ledger_v2') !== '1'`: every `proforma_invoices` row becomes (or joins, by number) a `proformas` row of kind `invoice` and a `pi_links` row; `acc_lines.invoice_id` is filled by `invoice_ref`; statuses are refreshed. `proforma_invoices` is left in place, read by nothing.

**Ledger SQL** (constants in app/pi.php, reused by col.php, finrep.php, cf.php — pi.php is therefore required before col.php): `PI_ADV_SQL` Σ payments on the row; `PI_INV_SQL` Σ links of a proforma; `PI_INVPAID_SQL` invoice payments allocated pro-rata to a proforma; `PI_PAID_SQL`; `PI_OPEN_SQL` = invoice: gross − payments, proforma: gross − advances − invoiced. `PI_SELECT` exposes `paid_sum, adv_sum, inv_sum, open_sum, n_inv, n_settles, invoice_nos, settles_nos`.

**Flows**: `pi_register_invoice()` creates or reuses the invoice row by number, grows its gross by the allocation, inserts the link, moves the proforma's advances to the invoice while they fit, optionally records a payment, returns the mismatch warning (`pi.inv_mismatch_warn`, flash `warn`). `pinv_delete($linkId)` removes a link (orphan invoice deleted, its payments back to the proforma). `pi_delete` refuses rows with links or payments. `ppay_save_from_post` caps per document. `acc_mark_invoiced(..., $invoiceId)` stores the invoice row on the lines (direct invoice from an accounting report passes it; otherwise resolved by number).

**Not chargeable at source**: `fsr_nc_reasons()` / `fsr_nc_has()`; form select `wk_ncr[i][k]` beside the type (hidden unless NC, toggled in the nonce script); API master `nc_reasons`, line field `nc_reason` (PWA 0.6.21). `acc_sync()` now collects NC lines as one `open` line with `exclude_reason = 'NC · <reason>'` (not for reports that already have reported / invoiced lines — no retroactive noise); they do not count towards the day's normal hours; `acc_create()` turns them `excluded` in the new draft. `fr_data()` adds `h_billed / h_open / h_excluded / h_nc`.

**Readers**: collections open items and ageing on `open_sum` (invoice pill, 🧾 numbers, "settles …"); statement skips fully invoiced proformas without advances; financial report invoices = invoice rows; cash flow IN actual = payments, forecast = `PI_OPEN_SQL` + issued accounting reports not invoiced (+30 d, driver `in_ar`), OUT labour from `acc_lines`.

**Security review**: all new writes go through existing role gates (`pi_can_invoices()`, `acc_can_prepare()`), CSRF-checked actions and prepared statements; `wk_ncr` and the API `nc_reason` are whitelisted against `fsr_nc_reasons()`; the conversion is idempotent and flag-guarded; no new endpoint, no inline handler, the one script addition sits in the existing nonce block. Proposals (no performance cost): (1) add `FOREIGN KEY`-like nightly integrity check of `pi_links` orphans to the self-check; (2) log invoice unlink / payment moves in the audit trail with before / after amounts; (3) restrict `pinv_delete` to accounting + management once the accounting team has taken over invoice registration.

## 282. v1.120.0 — Parties plan phase 3: statement per billing party, ownership periods (GAS-CP-R95-005)

Decisions 8 and 9 of 10/11/2026.

**Statement** (`app/col.php`): `col_statement($vesselIds, $asOf, $from, ?int $orgId)`. With `$orgId` the rows are `proformas.billing_org_id = org` (optionally `AND vessel_id IN (…)`), sections are the vessels found on the rows plus a pseudo vessel `id 0` "No vessel (general)"; the addressee is `col_party($org)` — the organisation's default invoicing identity, else the organisation record. Without it (vessel statement) the addressee is the single party of the rows, else `vo_at($vessel, $asOf)` when no row has a party, else the vessel's customer; several parties → `multi_party` (addressee line says so, the page offers a button per party). Return adds `party, by_party, multi_party, parties, owner_then`. `col_statement_parties()` feeds the picker (documents, open = Σ `PI_OPEN_SQL`). Route `?p=statement&party=<org>[&v[]=…]`; e-mail attachment key `st:o<org>,<v1>,<v2>:pdf|xlsx:<asof>[:<from>]` (the `o<id>` element is read inside `col_statement`, so `col_em_attachment` is unchanged). Document number `…-SOA-<date>-<PartySlug>[-<Vessel>]`. `COL_SELECT` now carries `pi.billing_org_id, bo.name AS party_name`; `col_where` knows `party`.

**Ownership** (`app/vo.php`, `install/migrate_cp_r95_005_ownership.sql`, check `r95d`): `vessel_owners (vessel_id, company_id, customer_id, date_from, date_to, note, created_by)`, back-filled with one open period per vessel that has a customer. `vo_change()` validates (date, not the same owner, after the current period), creates the client account of the organisation when missing (`vo_org_customer`, refuses supplier-only organisations), closes the open period the day before, inserts the new one and updates `vessels.customer_id` in one transaction. `vo_delete()` (administrators) removes the latest period and re-opens the previous. `vo_sync()` runs after `vessel_save`. Actions `vo_change`, `vo_delete` in `index.php`; they are not in `PA_ACTIONS` (the assistant cannot change an owner).

**Self-check**: `vessel_owners` present; orphan `pi_links` (proposal 1 of §281, now implemented).

**Security review**: `vo_change` / `vo_delete` gated by `vo_can()` / administrator inside the functions (not only in the view), CSRF as every POST, one transaction; the statement keeps `col_can()` and the project scope (`scope_sql`) — a party statement cannot show documents of projects outside the user's scope; `party` and `sparty` are cast to int and looked up; no new inline script. Proposals (no performance cost): (1) audit-trail entries for owner changes and for invoice unlink / payment moves; (2) restrict invoice unlink to accounting + management; (3) a statement for the customer portal only after the access rule is decided (which portal users of a party may see its account).

**Regression pass of phases 0–3 (same release)** — every routed page swept as administrator, PM, HR and customer (no PHP warning, no raw language key), role tests on every new action, ledger totals reconciled (Collections outstanding = Σ party statements = Σ `PI_OPEN_SQL`). Fixed:
1. `pinv_delete()` deleted (or shrank) a **direct invoice** when its last link to a proforma was removed, and `pi_register_invoice()` grew a direct invoice's gross from its second link on. New column `proformas.from_links` (1 = invoice row built by registering it on proformas, gross = Σ allocations; 0 = direct invoice with its own gross; back-filled in `migrate_cp_r95_005`), helpers `pi_links_col()` / `pi_from_links()`. A direct invoice is never grown, shrunk or deleted by link operations, keeps its payments, and only its not-yet-allocated amount can settle a proforma (`pi.inv_alloc_over`).
2. `org_link_all()` answered "0 organisations linked" to a user without the right instead of refusing — explicit `org_can()`.
3. `views/web_stats.php` used `onclick="window.print()"` (blocked by the CSP — the Print button did nothing) → `data-print` + nonce script.
4. Seven language keys used by views but never defined (`btn.filter`, `col.open` → `fr.open`, `im.working`, `pr.subs`, `pt.currency`, `sys.hidden`, `web.role`) — shown as raw keys; added × 5. `pay.exceeds` said "offer total" for a per-document cap; reworded (bg / uk were still in English).
5. Collections Excel › Open items gained the Billing party column.

## 283. v1.121.0 — Customer credit notes (GAS-CP-R95-006)

Decisions of 11/11/2026: credit notes are issued for an invoice **and** standalone; for the amount not absorbed by an invoice the accountant chooses refund or carry-forward.

**Schema** (`install/migrate_cp_r95_006_credit_notes.sql`, check `r95e` = `pi_credits`): `proformas.kind` gains `credit`; `proformas.credit_mode ENUM('refund','carry')`, `proformas.credit_of_id` (the invoice it was raised for, informative); `pi_credits (credit_id, invoice_id, amount)` unique per pair.

**Convention**: a credit note row stores `amount_net`, `vat_amount`, `gross` **negative**; a refund is a **negative** `proforma_payments` row on the credit note. Every plain `SUM(gross)` / `SUM(amount)` therefore nets by itself.

**SQL** (app/pi.php): `PI_CRIN_SQL` (credits applied to an invoice), `PI_CROUT_SQL` (applied by a credit note); `PI_OPEN_SQL` = `CASE kind WHEN 'credit' THEN gross + CROUT − payments WHEN 'invoice' THEN gross − payments − CRIN ELSE gross − advances − invoiced`; `PI_SELECT.cr_sum`. `pi_refresh_status()`: invoice settled by payments + credits, credit note by applications + refunds ("paid" = settled; no "paid" notification without money). `ppay_save_from_post()`: on a credit note the amount is stored negative, cap = |gross| − applied; an invoice's cap = gross − credits. `pi_delete()` refuses rows with applications. `pi_direct_create_from_post()` with `doc_kind=credit` (+ `credit_of`, `credit_mode`): copies project / sub-project / vessel / customer / identity / currency / party from the invoice, inserts negative, applies automatically (`cn_apply(…, null)`).

**Module** `app/cn.php`: `cn_has, cn_modes, cn_remainder, cn_applied, cn_on_invoice, cn_for_invoice, cn_candidates, cn_apply, cn_unapply, cn_set_mode`. `cn_apply` enforces: both issued, same currency, same billing party (same project when no party), amount ≤ min(remainder, invoice open). Actions `cn_apply`, `cn_unapply`, `cn_mode` (index.php), all behind `pi_can_invoices()`.

**Views**: `invoice_new.php` (`?doc=credit`, `?credit_of=<invoice>`), `proforma.php` (credit card: amount / applied / refunded / remainder + mode, applications, apply form, mode form; Refunds card when mode = refund or refunds exist; invoice page: Credit notes card + button), `proformas.php`, `collections.php` (pill, button).

**Readers**: `col_open` keeps credit rows with a negative open (bucket "not due", never overdue); `col_statement` dates applications by the credit note (`crIn` / `crOut` as of the statement date), invoice lines carry "credited …"; `fs_totals_build`, `fr_data`, `inv_compute_many`, proforma list totals and `cf_data` (open credit = negative forecast) count credits negative.

**Self-check**: `pi_credits` present; applications point to a credit note and an invoice; no credit note with a positive amount.

**Security review**: no new endpoint; the three new actions and the creation path are gated by `pi_can_invoices()` inside the functions, CSRF as every POST, prepared statements, amounts parsed server-side and capped; `credit_mode` whitelisted; `credit_of` re-read from the database (project and party are taken from the invoice, never from the form); candidates are limited by the project scope. Proposals (no performance cost): (1) audit-trail entry for credit applications, removals and refunds with before / after amounts; (2) a second approval for refunds above a threshold (as in Payables); (3) restrict credit-note registration to the accounting role only (today: everyone who may register invoices).

## 284. v1.122.0 — Executive block of the weekly and monthly reports (GAS-CP-R96-001)

Decisions of 11/11/2026: milestones = a list per project kept by the PM, pre-filled from the sub-project deadlines; LTI tile fed by a project safety log; summary paragraph = AI draft the PM edits.

**Schema** (`install/migrate_cp_r96_001_exec_summary.sql`, check `r96a`): `pm_milestones` (title, plan / forecast / achieved date, source manual | deadline, unique per project + sub-project + source), `pm_safety` (date, kind lti | medical | first_aid | near_miss | observation, title, description, action, lost days), `pm_exec_summary` (project, period `YYYY-Www` | `YYYY-MM`, body, status draft | final, ai).

**Module** `app/xs.php`: `xs_period`, `xs_calc` (closures `actual($date, $strict)` and `planned($date)`; loads snapshots, work packages and start dates once), `xs_data($pid, 'week'|'month', $ref, $staff)`, `xs_tiles`, `xs_auto_text`, `xs_summary_get/save`, `xs_ai_draft`, `xs_html`, `xs_svg`, `xs_editor_html`, `xs_pdf`, `xs_docx`, `xs_return`; milestones `ms_list`, `ms_seed`, `ms_status`, `ms_save_from_post`, `ms_delete`; safety `sf_kinds`, `sf_list`, `sf_save_from_post`, `sf_delete`.

**Figures**: actual = `pct_avg` of the sub-projects' `overall_pct` (snapshot in force on the date, live for today) — deliberately the same as the "Project average" of the weekly progress table and the headline tile of the weekly PDF; history points of the curve use snapshots only (`$strict`). Planned = per sub-project, work packages with dates (planned hours spread linearly between start and end), else linear from the start (first timesheet line / first snapshot / project creation) to the deadline, else — when Completed — done; equal weights over the sub-projects that have a plan. SPI = round(actual) ÷ round(planned) so the tile agrees with the two beside it; tone from `kpi_tone('spi')`. Curve = 12 ISO weeks (−7 … +4 around the report week). Milestone status on the report date: achieved; forecast in the past → late (weeks since plan); forecast after plan → −n weeks (red from 3); due within six weeks → on track; else planned.

**Wiring**: `views/weekly_vessel.php` (one block + editor per project before section 1), `wk_render_pdf` / `wk_render_docx` (`xs_pdf`, `xs_docx`), `views/monthly.php` (block inside `.report`, editor outside the print area), new `views/project_ms.php` (route `project_ms`), buttons in `projects.php` and `subproject.php`; actions `xs_save`, `xs_ai`, `ms_save`, `ms_delete`, `ms_seed`, `sf_save`, `sf_delete` (index.php); CSS `.xs-*` in `assets/portal.css`; `monthly` added to the assistant's page list.

**AI draft**: `ai_run('write', …)` under the page `weekly_vessel` / `monthly` / `weekly` (whichever is enabled in Settings › AI assistant, with the user's AI right, rate limit and monthly cap). The prompt carries project code and name, the figures, milestones, open risks (title, score, mitigation), safety events (date, kind, title) and the **customer-visible** site notes and PM remarks of the period — no internal notes, no personal or payroll data. The answer is stored as a draft; customers, print and exports use only a released text.

**Security review**: every write is gated inside the function by `xs_can_edit()` (administrator, management, the project's PM, PM role) and the project scope (`xs_project` → `scope_sql`); CSRF as every POST; the return address is rebuilt from validated fields (`xs_return`), never taken from the request — no open redirect; `period` is derived server-side from `kind` + `ref` and checked by pattern; rich text is rendered through `rt_html` (escaped); the SVG contains only numbers and escaped labels; the safety log asks for no names or medical details (stated on the form). Proposals (no performance cost): (1) audit-trail entries for released summaries and safety events; (2) notify management when a lost-time injury is recorded; (3) keep AI drafts out of the assistant provider's retention by enabling zero-retention on the API account, if available on the plan.

## 285. v1.122.1 — Offers filtered per branch (GAS-CP-R96-002)

`of_list($f)` accepts `branch`: `COALESCE(s.branch_id, p.branch_id, <default branch>) = ?` — the rule of `br_of()` (sub-project override, else project, else default); staff only, ignored for customers; the row now carries `branch_id`. `views/offers.php`: `$brOn` (staff, `br_has()`, `br_multi()`), select with `br_options()`, the tiles query takes the same branch, branch code beside the project; an unknown branch id is dropped. `app/flt.php`: `branch` added to the remembered keys of `offers`. No database change.

**Security review**: the value is cast to int, checked against the active branches and bound as a parameter; customers never get the filter (their list stays scoped by `scope_sql`). No proposals beyond those of §284.

## §286 v1.122.2 (R96-003) — payments keep the proforma they were received for

**Report**: a payment added to one proforma appeared split over the open proformas of the project.

**Cause**: since v1.119.0 an advance moves from the proforma to the accounting invoice when the invoice is linked. `PI_INVPAID_SQL` then gave every proforma the invoice settles a share of the invoice's payments in proportion to its allocation (`l.amount × paid / gross`). With one invoice number settling several proformas, 1 565,00 received for the first proforma showed as 816,41 / 521,67 / 226,93 on three proformas, all "partly paid". Recording a payment itself never split anything — one row is written on the document it is entered on.

**Change**: `proforma_payments.for_pi` (migration `install/migrate_cp_r96_003_payment_origin.sql`) = the proforma a payment on an invoice counts for; NULL = shared. `ppay_for_col()` (positive result cached) switches `PI_INVPAID_SQL`: per link, own money first (`PI_LNKOWN_SQL`, capped by the allocation), then the rest of the invoice's payments pro rata over what is still uncovered (`PI_INVOWN_SQL`). Writers: `pi_register_invoice` (moved advances and the "paid" of the link form get `for_pi`), `ppay_save_from_post` (`for_pi` from the form, accepted only for a proforma the invoice settles; a single-proforma invoice always its own), `pinv_delete` (money received for the unlinked proforma returns to it). `views/proforma.php`: "Counts for" select and the → label on invoices settling more than one proforma. Backfill: single-proforma invoices; on shared invoices a payment whose amount equals exactly one allocation and is the only payment of that amount. One-off status refresh on the first request (`cp_setting` `pay_origin`). Collections and statements list invoices as lines and were not affected. `pay.max_hint` reworded (it still described the offer-level cap removed in v1.119.0). Language keys 8 763 each.

**Security review**: `for_pi` is cast to int and accepted only when it is one of the invoice's linked proformas (a foreign id is stored as NULL — tested); writes stay behind `pi_can_invoices()` and CSRF; the new SQL has no user input; the form adds no inline script. Proposal (no performance cost): restrict "Remove link" and payment edits on invoices to the accounting role and write them to the audit trail — still open from §283.

## §287 v1.123.0 (R97-001) — System Budgets, release 1 (core) + GLD-BUD Excel import

Design: GAS-2026-PORTAL-DES-001 R0, accepted by the team with the proposed answers to OP-01 … OP-13. This release is the core plus the import the team asked for in addition; releases 2–4 (ticket, revisions, copy / templates, exports; BOM import, AI; convert to offer, budget feed) follow.

**Files**: `app/sb.php` (all logic), `views/system_budgets.php` (dashboard + list), `views/system_budget.php` (one budget), `views/system_budget_import.php` (review), migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_001_system_budgets.sql` — new tables only: `sb_budgets`, `sb_labour`, `sb_parts`, `sb_expenses`, `sb_comments`, `sb_events`. Nothing is added to the budget, offer, ticket, inventory or BOM tables. Actions `sb_*` in `index.php`; menu entry in `views/_layout.php` (Work).

**Access**: `sb_can()` = staff and (administrator, `mgmt_can()`, `pm_is_pm()` or `pi_is_sales()`); `sb_require()` on the three pages, `sb_guard()` inside every writer; reopen `sb_can_reopen()` (administrator / management); delete administrator only, draft or cancelled.

**Rules**: number `SB-<year>-<4 digits>` (unique key, retried on collision). Labour line: sale = (normal + travelling) × rate + overtime × overtime rate + days × allowance sale; cost = hours × internal cost + days × allowance cost; with the flat rate in use, lines without a rate card sell all their hours at the flat rate (`sb_labour_calc`). Part: sale = cost × (1 + margin / 100), line margin else budget margin (`sb_part_calc`); 124 lines max (`SB_MAX_PARTS`). Other costs carry `in_total` (0 = shown outside the total). `tot_hours / tot_cost / tot_sale` are stored on the budget by `sb_recalc()` for the list and the tiles. Finish: `sb_finish_blockers()`.

**GLD-BUD import** (`sb_xlsx_parse`, `sb_import_apply`): the workbook is stored under `storage/uploads/sbimp/<random 32 hex>.xlsx`, bound to the uploader's session, removed after the import and after one day otherwise. Sheets are read by label and header (never by row number): document control, revision history, build-up lines (logistics, pass-through, work-permit fee), commission, logistics cost, notes; rate inputs and work packages (code, rate basis, MH, rate, remarks, ERP bucket); parts (code, description, qty, unit, unit LCO, reference price, remarks), markup and distributor discount. Files that were never recalculated carry no formula results — rate by rate key, the blended rate and LCO = reference × (1 − discount) are computed by the portal, and the review page shows the recomputed totals to compare with the Budget Summary. Mapping: work package → labour line (MH as normal hours; `Rate X` → rate card X when the active rate list has it; bucket kept, section chosen per bucket on the review page; PM bucket → Project Management discipline); part → part line (LCO = unit cost, origin noted); markup → margin on parts; internal rate → internal labour cost; commission; expenses; description built from document control, revisions and notes. Into an existing draft / working budget the lines are replaced in one transaction, the flat rate is switched off (the workbook's rates price the lines) and the previously imported description block is replaced.

**Security review**: access enforced server-side on pages and actions (customer and HR-scope accounts tested: 403 / refused); CSRF on every form; all SQL parameterised, ids checked against the budget they belong to (`WHERE id = ? AND budget_id = ?`); output escaped, rich text through `rt_html`; no inline script, no `on*` attributes. Upload: `.xlsx` only, 5 MB, ZIP signature checked, any archive member above 30 MB refused (zip bomb), XML parsed with external entities off (PHP 8 default), random file name, token usable only by the session that uploaded it (tested with a second user), nothing executed from the file. Proposals without performance cost: (1) keep `storage/` outside the web root or confirm the deny rule covers `uploads/sbimp/`; (2) add the System Budget actions to the audit trail export; (3) rate-limit uploads per user (e.g. 20 per hour).

Language keys 8 956 each (en, el, it translated; bg, uk English).

## §288 v1.124.0 (R97-002) — System Budgets, release 2: revisions, copy, templates, ticket, Excel

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_002_sb_sources.sql`: `sb_budgets.is_template`, `sb_budgets.copied_from`, key on `ticket_id`, table `sb_revisions` (budget, revision, note, snapshot JSON, totals). `sb_r2()` guards every use, so the code runs before the migration too.

**Revisions**: `sb_snapshot()` = header settings (`SB_SNAP_HEAD`) + labour / part / other-cost rows without ids. `sb_revise()` stores the snapshot under the current number and increments `sb_budgets.revision`; `sb_revision_get()` shapes a stored revision like a budget for the same page (`?p=system_budget&id=N&rev=R`, read-only); `sb_restore()` first stores the working state as a revision, then `sb_write_snapshot()` in one transaction. Only while the budget is editable; templates have none.

**Copy / templates**: `sb_copy($src, $title, $asTemplate)` — a new draft via `sb_create()` with the snapshot written in (no sub-project, no target date; `copied_from`). Template: `is_template = 1`, number `SBT-NNNN`, excluded from the list and the tiles, no status changes, deleted by management (`sb_delete_template`). Create form: `from` = template id.

**Ticket**: `sb_from_ticket()` — returns the existing live budget of the ticket if there is one; else creates the budget (title `<ticket no> · <title>`, description starts with the request text, project / sub-project of the ticket, `ticket_id`), and resolves an open ticket through `tk_set_status()` with the fixed text `sb.tk_resolution`. Nothing is written to the ticket timeline with the budget number — customers read that timeline. The ticket page shows the button / the link only when `sb_can()`.

**Excel**: `?p=system_budget_export&id=N[&rev=R]` → `sb_export_xlsx()` through the in-house writer.

**Security review**: every new action passes `sb_guard()` / `sb_load_editable()`; export and revision pages behind `sb_can()` (customer: 403, tested); HR-scope account cannot convert a ticket (tested); snapshot rows are written back only through fixed column lists, so a stored JSON cannot set other columns; restore and copy are transactional; the customer-facing ticket shows no budget number or link. Proposals without performance cost: (1) log restore and template deletion in the audit trail; (2) cap stored revisions per budget (e.g. 50) to bound the table.

Language keys 9 002 each.

## §289 v1.125.0 (R97-003) — System Budgets, release 3: BOM import, inventory proposals, AI description

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_003_sb_assist.sql`: table `sb_import_rows` (budget, optional `part_id` of an existing line, file fields, `unit_cost`, proposed `item_id`, `match_kind`, `confidence`, `reason`). `sb_r3()` guards its use.

**BOM import** — `sb_bom_stage()`: the header aliases of `bom_import_stage()` plus `price` (unit price / unit cost / LCO); matching by `sb_match_row()` = Golden code (100), maker part number (95), normalised part number (85) with `bom_imp_norm()`; unmatched rows → `sb_stage_ai()` (`ai_run('match')`, candidates from `bom_imp_candidates()`, batches of 25, accepted from confidence 50) when `ai_can('system_budget')`. At most 400 rows staged. Review page `views/system_budget_bom.php`; `sb_stage_apply()` in one transaction: accept → part line with `item_id`, free → free line, skip → nothing; cost = file price, else `sb_item_cost()` (lowest price on record → last price → average cost) with the origin; the 124-line limit is enforced and the rows that did not fit are reported. No inventory item is created from a budget (unlike the Engineering BOM).

**Inventory proposals** — `sb_propose_stage()`: free lines (no `item_id`, not excluded) staged with `part_id`; match by code, then a token of the description (≥ 5 characters with a digit) equal to a normalised maker part number, then the assistant. Accept updates the line: `item_id`, cost kept when the line has one (origin "line cost kept · linked to <code>"), else inventory cost.

**AI description** — `sb_ai_description()`: prompt from title, work packages (code, description, section, hours, remarks), parts (code, description, quantity, unit, EXCLUDED flag), other-cost headings and the existing description; no prices, costs, margins, people or customer data. The text is kept in the session (`$_SESSION['sb_ai'][id]`), shown once in the textarea with a notice and saved only by the user's Save. Page key `system_budget` added to `ai_all_pages()` — the administrator enables it in Settings › AI assistant; the per-user "can use AI" flag, the monthly cap, the rate limit and the usage log apply as everywhere.

**Tested**: BOM file with exact, normalised, code and unmatched rows; review; apply; proposals from the description text; override by inventory code; AI disabled path. The assistant paths (matching and description) could not be run in the sandbox — no API key there — they use the calls already in service for the Engineering BOM and the report summaries.

**Security review**: uploads as §287 (xlsx, 5 MB, parsed from the PHP temp file, nothing stored); all actions behind `sb_load_editable()`; row ids of the review are read back from the budget's own staged rows (a posted id of another budget is ignored); the override resolves an inventory code server-side; AI input is technical text only and its answers are treated as data (item ids accepted only among the candidates offered; description escaped through `rt_html`). Proposals without performance cost: (1) purge staged rows older than 30 days in the nightly job; (2) show the assistant's monthly cost per page in Settings › AI assistant.

Language keys 9 062 each.

## §290 v1.126.0 (R97-004) — System Budgets, release 4: convert to offer, budget feed; module test

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_004_sb_convert.sql`: `sb_budgets.offer_id`, `budget_rev_id`, `converted_at`. `sb_r4()` guards their use.

**Conversion** — page `views/system_budget_convert.php` (preview by GET options `labour=line|section|lump`, `parts=line|lump`, `commission=0|1`), action `sb_convert` → `sb_convert()` in one transaction; allowed for `sb_can_convert()` = `sb_can()` and `ob_can()`, only on status finished. `sb_offer_lines()` builds the lines: labour (normal + travelling at the rate; overtime separately; grouped per section or as one line with the average price), daily allowances, parts (`item` when linked to inventory, else `free`; `cost_price` and `margin_pct` kept), other costs in the total as lines, costs outside the total as a `text` line; with the commission every unit price is divided by (1 − commission). The offer row: draft, layout `system`, next number by `br_offer_no()`, ticket of the budget, note "Created from System Budget …"; `ob_blocks_init()` then `scope` = `sb_scope_text()` (description without the imported working-file block) and the zero-hour / excluded lines appended to `exclusions`. Line texts generated by the portal (allowances, labour group names) are written in the language of the user who converts. The budget: status `converted`, `offer_id`, event.

**Budget feed** — `sb_feed_plan()`: hours = normal + overtime per labour line, keyed by discipline (`sb_line_discipline()`: own → first discipline of the section in `eng_section_disciplines` → discipline with the line's rate card) and section; `sb_convert()` writes a new `sp_budgets` revision (`replace`: only these rows; `add`: current rows + these) with `sp_budget_hours` rows at the internal cost rate, `parts_budget` = parts cost, `offer_value` = offer net; `none` writes nothing. Existing revisions are not updated. Lines without any discipline are listed on the preview and not written.

**Reopen** — `sb_set_status(working)` from `converted`: management only and only when the offer row no longer exists.

**Module test** (sandbox, script of 106 checks over all four releases): access of customer and HR-scope accounts to every page and every action; missing token; escaping of titles, workbook texts and notes; header, labour and part inputs with invalid, negative and localised numbers; ids of other budgets posted into forms; the 124-line limit (form, inventory add, BOM apply, workbook with 130 parts); status rules; revisions (unknown revision, converted budget); templates; copy of a stored revision; working-file import (wrong extension, other workbook, converted budget, single-use and path-like tokens); staged rows of another budget; conversion (twice, locked, export, add mode, old revision untouched, offer total = sum of lines, reopen rules); ticket; every page with odd parameters; PHP log. Findings and fixes: (1) part lines beyond the limit aborted the save before the totals were recalculated — what fits is now saved and totalled, then the message is shown; (2) `csrf_check()` compared the posted token with the session token by `hash_equals` only — a session that had not yet issued a token accepted an EMPTY token; both must now be non-empty (portal-wide; in a browser the window was the moment between sign-in and the first page, but it is closed); (3) the stored margin of an offer line now follows the price when the commission is included; (4) a route registration slip found and corrected before release. The assistant paths (matching, description) remain untested in the sandbox (no key).

**Security review**: conversion and feed behind `sb_can_convert()` and the status check; offer and budget rows written with fixed columns and bound values; no customer-visible page shows a System Budget; the token fix above. Proposals without performance cost: (1) write conversions to the audit trail; (2) add the System Budget tables to the nightly backup check list; (3) repeat the fresh-session token test in the release checklist.

Language keys 9 120 each.

## §291 v1.126.1 (R97-005) — security measures accepted on 11/11/2026

| Proposal (section) | What was done |
|---|---|
| Deny rule on `storage/uploads/sbimp/` (§287) | The repository never shipped a rule for `customer/storage/` (it is git-ignored) — its protection depended on a file placed by hand at installation. `cp_storage_guard()` (`app/helpers.php`) now writes `storage/.htaccess` (deny all, Apache 2.4 and 2.2) into the storage folder and into a configured upload path outside it; called from `index.php` once a day and after each release (cache key carries the version), from `tools/cron.php`, and by Diagnostics (row "Storage folder closed to the web") and `cp_selfcheck.php`. Nothing is served from the folder by URL — files are streamed by PHP and logos are read from disk — so no feature changes. |
| System Budget actions in the audit trail (§287, §288, §290) | `sb_events.ip` (migration `migrate_cp_r97_005_sb_audit.sql`), written by `sb_event()`; events are no longer deleted with a budget or a template — a `deleted` line is added instead. Restore, template deletion, conversion, uploads and the export itself are lines of the trail. Export `?p=system_budget_audit` → `sb_audit_xlsx()` (management / administrators; 24 months, 20 000 lines). |
| Upload rate limit (§287) | `sb_upload_guard()`: 20 uploads per person and hour (`SB_UPLOADS_HOUR`), counted from the `upload` events; applies to the working file and the BOM. |
| Cap of stored revisions (§288) | `SB_MAX_REVISIONS` = 50 in `sb_revise()`. |
| Purge of staged rows (§289) | `sb_housekeeping()` once a day from the System Budgets page (cache flag) and in `tools/cron.php`: `sb_import_rows` older than 30 days, uploaded workbooks older than a day. |
| Assistant cost per page (§289) | Settings › AI assistant: "This month by page" (requests, failed, €, share). |
| Conversions in the audit trail (§290) | Covered by the trail above (`converted` event with the offer number and the budget mode). |
| System Budget tables in the backup check (§290) | Verified: `install/update.php` dumps every table returned by `SHOW TABLES` into `storage/backups/<stamp>/db.sql.gz` before each update — the `sb_*` tables are included without a list to maintain. No change. |
| Fresh-session token test (§290) | `csrf_ok()` extracted from `csrf_check()`; Diagnostics row "Request-token check" asserts that empty and wrong tokens are refused; DEPLOY.md has the release checklist with the manual test. |

Not done, waiting for a decision: restricting "Remove link" and payment edits on invoices to the accounting role (§286) — it changes who may do today's work in Proformas and invoices.

**Tested**: guard file written after deletion; audit line with IP; trail kept after delete; export for administrator, 403 for PM and customer; 51st revision refused; 21st upload refused; old staged row purged, recent one kept; cron output; Diagnostics rows OK. Language keys 9 141 each.

## §292 v1.127.0 (R97-006) — System Budgets: priority

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_006_sb_priority.sql`: `sb_budgets.priority` (`urgent` / `high` / `normal` / `low`, default `normal`) and key `(status, priority)`. `sb_prio()` guards the column.

`SB_PRIORITIES`, `sb_prio_pill()`, `sb_set_priority()` (any `sb_can()` user; draft, working or finished; not templates; `priority` event with old → new). Action `sb_priority`; `sb_create` accepts `priority`; `sb_from_ticket()` copies the ticket's priority. `sb_list()`: filter `priority`; order = open budgets (draft, working, finished) first, `FIELD(priority, 'urgent','high','normal','low')`, nearest `target_date`, then last update. `sb_dash()`: counts of urgent and high among draft / working, shown on the "Past target date" tile (which opens the urgent ones). Budget page: pill beside the status and a selector with "Set priority"; Excel summary line. Copies and templates start at Normal; revisions do not carry the priority (it belongs to the budget, not to a state of its lines).

**Security review**: the value is checked against the fixed list (anything else refused — tested), bound as a parameter, changes behind `sb_guard()` and CSRF, recorded in the audit trail with the IP; customer and HR-scope accounts refused (tested). The list filter ignores unknown values. No new proposals — the checklist of §291 applies.

Language keys 9 152 each.

## §293 v1.127.1 (R97-007) — System Budgets: no labour hour lost on the way to the sub-project budget

**Report**: a converted budget of 1 138 h showed 612 h in "Sections — budgeted hours per stage" (Electrical 120, Production 40, Commissioning 452).

**Cause**: `sb_feed_plan()` writes a line only when it has a discipline — its own, the first discipline of its section, or the discipline of its rate card. On the live sections the working-file bucket **Site** found no default section (no section has the stage `installation`; the Field section is of stage engineering), and the PM bucket found no discipline called exactly "Project Management"; these lines were imported without a section and left out at the conversion, with a notice on the preview only. Travelling time is never fed (design OP-05), and a discipline without a section is written to Budget & actual but shows in no section card.

**Change**: `sb_labour.fed` (migration `migrate_cp_r97_007_sb_feed.sql`) marks at conversion which lines were written. `sb_assign_lines()` stores a section / discipline on lines that have none (only these two fields, also on finished / converted budgets; never on a line already fed). Conversion preview: table of the lines without a discipline with Section (pre-selected from the bucket) and Discipline selects, posted as `as[line][…]` and applied by `sb_convert()` before the feed. `sb_feed_state()` / `sb_feed_rest()` and page `views/system_budget_feed.php` (`?p=system_budget_feed&id=N`): for a converted budget the hours of the budget, those that count (normal + overtime), those written and those outside; after the choice a new `sp_budgets` revision = current rows + these hours, lines marked fed, in one transaction. Budgets converted before this release carry no marks: a line that can be written now is taken as written then (derived once, then stored). Notices: budget in work (`sb.blk_nosec`), converted budget (`sb.fd_warn`). `sb_bucket_defaults()` also matches the section by name / code (Site → field / install; Workshop → production; Commissioning; Engineering → electrical); PM discipline by `/project\s*manag|^pm$/i`.

**For the two budgets already converted** (same working file on two sub-projects): open each, follow "Assign and add them", choose the section for the lines listed and add — the sections then show the full hours as revision R1.

**Security review**: the new page and action behind `sb_require()` / `sb_guard()`; line ids are taken from the budget's own lines, section and discipline ids checked against the active lists; amounts of a locked budget cannot be changed through the assignment (two columns only); audit lines `assigned` and `fed`. No new proposals.

Language keys 9 177 each.

## §294 v1.127.2 (R97-008) — System Budgets: rate cards shown by name

No SQL. `views/system_budget.php`: the options of the Rate card select are `<letter> — <card_name> · <normal rate> €/h` from `sb_cards()` (active rate list, zone of the budget); a stored card that is not in the list any more is shown as `<letter> — not in the active rate list`; the select is 230 px wide at least. `sb_export_xlsx()`: the Rate card column carries `<letter> — <card_name>`.

**Security review**: display only — the card names come from the rate list and are escaped; the posted value is still checked against the cards of the list. No proposals.

Language keys 9 178 each.

## §295 v1.127.3 (R97-009) — Website statistics: trend chart size

No SQL. `ws_bars()` in `views/web_stats.php` shrank the SVG viewBox to the width of the bars (two days → 168 units) while the element had no width, so the browser stretched it to the card: two bars filled the page. The viewBox now keeps the full drawing width (980 × 220) and the element carries `width` / `height`, so it shows at its natural size (and still shrinks on a narrow screen through `max-width:100%`). Bars stay at most 56 units wide, from the left.

**Security review**: display only; no input involved. No proposals.

## §296 v1.127.4 (R97-010) — entry page: Employee Portal door removed

No SQL. Root `index.php`: the second door (`employee/`) and the sentence about employees are gone; `.doors--one` in `assets/portal.css` centres the remaining door. Nothing under `/employee/` was changed: the old parts-order application still answers at its own address for anyone who has it bookmarked.

**Security review**: an application that is no longer used but still reachable is attack surface that nobody watches (its own login, its own database `goldensas_poms`, its own storage folder). Proposal, no performance cost: once the team confirms nobody needs it, close `/employee/` — answer every request with a redirect to the portal (one `.htaccess` line), keep the files and the database for the record, and remove them after an agreed retention time.

## §297 v1.127.5 (R97-011) — entry page: banner to the company website

No SQL. Root `index.php`: `<a class="land-banner" href="https://www.goldenas.com.gr/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">` under the door; styles `.land-banner` in `assets/portal.css` (gold-tinted strip, same width as the door row, stacks on a phone).

**Security review**: a plain outbound link — `rel="noopener"` keeps the opened page from reaching back into the entry page; no script, no tracking parameter, no third-party resource is loaded. The proposal of §296 (close `/employee/` once confirmed unused) stays open.

## §298 v1.127.6 (R97-012) — entry page: renamed door, no caching

No SQL. After v1.127.4 / v1.127.5 the entry page still showed two doors: the root `index.php` sent no cache headers, so the firewall (Sucuri) and the browser served their stored copy of the page and of `assets/portal.css`. The page now sends `Cache-Control: no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0` (+ `Pragma`, `Expires`) and links the stylesheet as `assets/portal.css?v=<file time>`. The door reads "Golden A/S Portal" (badge G; "Projects, documents, service & procurement · Πρόοδος έργων · Stato progetti").

The copy the firewall already holds is not removed by a release: it expires by itself, or at once with Sucuri › Performance › Clear cache. Check without waiting: `https://goldensas.com/index.php?fresh=1`.

**Security review**: no-store on a page without personal data costs one small PHP response per visit; nothing else changes. The proposal of §296 stays open.

## §299 v1.127.7 (R97-013) — "logged in and automatically out" from the entry page

**Report**: pressing the portal door on the entry page showed the portal signed in and then signed the person out.

**Cause** (reproduced): the rule of R49-001 — every signed-in tab refreshes a marker in `localStorage` (`gascp_w`) every 10 s; a signed-in page that loads with a marker older than 45 s asks the server to drop the session (`?p=relock`) and goes to the sign-in page with "The browser was closed …". The server session was still valid, so the dashboard was rendered first and replaced a moment later. Not introduced today; the new entry page only made the path (no portal tab for a while → door) common.

**Change**: `assets/portal.css` — `body[data-wchk]:not(.w-ok) { visibility: hidden }` with a 1,5 s fallback to visible; `assets/portal.js` adds `w-ok` once the marker check has passed (or the page is the first after sign-in), so a session about to be dropped never shows the dashboard. `assets/landing.js` on the root entry page: when the marker is still fresh on arrival it is refreshed while the page is open — portal → entry page → portal keeps the session; a marker already stale is left alone. Manuals of all five audiences carry the explanation.

**Security review**: the policy itself is unchanged (setting `sec_close_signout`, Settings › Login history). The entry page only prolongs a marker that was valid when it was opened, in the same browser profile, and cannot create one; the server-side idle time-out (`sec_idle_minutes`) and the maximum session length still apply. No proposals.

## §300 v1.127.8 (R97-014) — project form: vessels of the customer's organisation

**Report**: customer = an organisation (BESECKE GmbH & Co. KG) with the vessel HADAR under it; New project offered "No vessel" only.

**Cause**: the form listed a vessel only for `vessels.customer_id` = the chosen customer (`data-customer`), and `project_update` dropped a vessel that failed the same test. A vessel attached to the organisation through the related parties of the vessel or its ownership history has another (or no) `customer_id`.

**Change**: `vessel_customer_map()` / `vessel_of_customer()` in `app/pm.php` — vessel → customers: its own customer; `vessel_companies.customer_id`; the customer of a linked organisation (`companies.customer_id`), or the customer with the same name when the organisation is not linked to a customer record yet; the same for `vessel_owners`. `views/project_new.php`: options carry `data-customers=",1,7,"` and the script shows a vessel when the chosen customer is in the list; `project_update` uses `vessel_of_customer()`.

**Security review**: staff-only form; the server-side check on update is widened only to vessels that have a recorded link to the customer; customers' own view of vessels and projects (`scope_sql`, related-party visibility) is unchanged. No proposals.

## §301 v1.127.9 (R97-015) — Customers: vessels assigned from the customer's page

**Request**: the customer page showed "0 vessels" and had no way to assign one; with an organisation the path (related companies of the vessel) was clear.

No SQL. `app/vm.php`: `vm_customer_vessels($cid)` — vessels with `customer_id` = the customer plus `vessel_companies` rows of the customer or of its organisations; `vm_customer_vessel_link()` — `vm_related_set_customer()` with the chosen role (the customer's organisation is created if missing) and `vessels.customer_id` set only when the vessel had none; `vm_customer_vessel_unlink()` — removes a link only when it belongs to that customer. `views/admin_customers.php`: card "Vessels" (`#cu-vessels`) with list, Assign a vessel (vessel + role) and Remove. Actions `cu_vessel_link` / `cu_vessel_unlink` in `app/pm_actions.php`, `require_ct_manage()`. Together with §300 the assigned vessel appears in the project form of the customer.

**Security review**: the actions need the right to manage customers (administrators, Owner, Personal Assistant — a project manager gets 403, tested); the unlink checks that the link belongs to the customer named in the form (a link of another customer is refused, tested); the role is checked against `VM_ROLES`; an existing customer of a vessel is never replaced from here. Visibility for the customer's own users follows the existing related-party rules. No proposals.

Language keys 9 191 each.

## §302 v1.128.0 (R97-016) — Vessels: web link to online information

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_016_vessel_link.sql`: `vessels.info_url VARCHAR(500)`. `vm_url_col()` guards the column; `vm_url_clean()` accepts http / https only (a bare `www…` gets `https://`), refuses spaces, control characters, quotes, angle brackets and backslashes, needs a host with a dot, at most 500 characters. `vessel_save` stores it (empty clears); `views/vessel_form.php` field; `views/vessel_view.php` button "Online information" + host name, `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`, for staff.

**Security review**: the stored address can only be an http(s) URL — `javascript:`, `data:`, `ftp:` and addresses with quotes are refused (tested), and the page checks the scheme again before printing the link, escaped. `noreferrer` keeps the portal's address (with ids) from being sent to the other site. Shown to staff only, so an internal folder address is not disclosed to customer accounts (tested). No proposals.

Language keys 9 195 each.


## §303 v1.128.1 (R97-017) — Vessels: preview of the web link

Migration `install/migrate_cp_r97_017_vessel_link_preview.sql`: `vessels.info_title VARCHAR(200)`, `info_desc VARCHAR(600)`, `info_checked_at DATETIME`. `vm_prev_col()` guards the columns. `vm_url_preview()` reads the linked page once on the server and `vm_url_meta()` keeps two plain texts — `og:title` or `<title>`, `og:description` or the meta description (other character sets are converted to UTF-8). `vm_url_preview_store()` runs from `vessel_save` when the link changed (the old texts are cleared) and from the new action `vessel_link_refresh` (↻; a failed read keeps the texts already stored). `vm_url_block()` prints the card on `views/vessel_view.php`: site, title (a link), description, "Open the page ↗", ↻. `views/vessels.php` shows a 🌐 link beside the vessel name. Staff only, as the link itself. A page that needs a sign-in or refuses automatic readers gives no texts — the card then shows the site and the button.

**Security review**: the portal now makes an outgoing request to an address typed by a user, so the request is fenced (server-side request forgery): http / https on ports 80 / 443 only, no user:password in the address, every address the host resolves to must be public (private, loopback, link-local and reserved ranges are refused — tested with 127.0.0.1, 10.x, 192.168.x, 169.254.169.254), the connection is pinned to the address that was checked (no DNS re-binding), at most 3 redirects and each one is checked again, 5 s time limit, 300 KB, HTML only, no cookies or credentials are sent. Nothing from the other site is loaded by the browser (no iframe, no remote image), so the content-security policy is unchanged and the other site learns nothing about the person viewing; the two texts are stripped of tags and escaped on output. The read happens only on save or ↻ (staff, CSRF-protected), never on page view — no effect on page speed. Proposal: none new. Not testable in the build environment (no internet): the read of a real page — check on live with one vessel.

Language keys 9 199 each.

## §304 v1.128.2 (R97-018) — System Budgets: the rate zone re-prices the labour lines

Cause of the report "changed to Abroad, the preview brings the same rates": a labour line stores its own `rate` / `rate_ot`, filled from the rate card when the line is entered; `sb_save_head_from_post()` changed `sb_budgets.zone` only, so totals, preview and conversion kept the old rates. Now the header save calls `sb_rerate($id, $from, $to)` when the zone changed: a rate-card line whose rate equals the card's rate of the old zone (or is empty) takes the rate of the new zone; other rates are kept. `sb_rate_drift()` counts rate-card lines that differ from the zone's card; the labour card shows the note and the action `sb_rerate` (`sb_rerate_all()`, editable budgets only, confirm) sets them all. The event log records the zone change and the counts. Zone names follow the rate list: Local (Attica) / Outside Attica / Abroad / Greek islands (`price_local` / `price_overseas` — the rate list has two columns, so the three outside places share one).

**Security review**: the new action runs behind `require_staff()`, `sb_load_editable()` (rights + not finished) and the CSRF token; rates come from the active rate list only, nothing from the request. No new input, no new query on page view beyond a loop over lines already loaded. No finding, no proposal.

Language keys 9 204 each.

## §305 v1.128.3 (R97-019) — System Budgets: out-of-Attica rate of a rate card

Report: zone Outside Attica selected, the rate card still shows and applies 80,00 €/h. Cause: the imported rate list keeps the zones on separate rows (a local row with `price_local` only, an out-of-Attica row with `price_overseas` only — the shape `acc_rate_item()` already handles for service reports). `sb_cards()` took the first row of the card and `rl_price()` fell back to the other zone's price, so the local row answered for both zones. `sb_cards()` now reads in two passes: rows that carry the zone's own price first, the other zone only for what is still missing. Reproduced and tested with both list shapes (one row with two prices; separate rows). Budgets already priced wrong show the R97-018 note and are corrected with "Apply the rate-card rates".

**Security review**: read-only change of a price lookup; no input, no new query. No finding.

Language keys 9 204 each.
